## Page 4

### CHICAGO PUBLIC LIBRARY

<!-- chunk-id: acg-src-lewis-guttman-1989.page-0004.chunk-001 -->
<!-- chunk-sha256: 46ded165dd07f052707fe2045d88b1ca83f96d1bd971ed0c510b66fe10ff9b38 -->
ROD Otel tt Everyone believes there is a special person, job and place for him or her. The Astro*Carto*Graphy Book of Maps explores those special places in the lives of celebrities and famous figures. This book by Jim Lewis and Ariel Guttman consists of maps, birth charts and personal profiles of people from all walks of life. The maps, based on the time of birth, graphically reveal lines of planetary influence or concentration at various geographic locations. A planet affecting a certain area is correlated with a person's success, failure or noteworthy activities there. See how Jane Fonda's life changed once she responded to her Moon line. Or maybe you'd like to know which line keeps cropping up at the site of assassinations. What lines similarly influenced the lives of Elvis Presley and Elizabeth Taylor? Gain some insight into possible actions and attitudes during George Bush's presidency. Astro*Carto*Graphy can also be used to bring about the stronger influence of a certain planet by showing its angular positions. Angular positions involve the Ascendant, the IC, the Descendant and the Midheaven. The maps show where planets would have been if you had been born at different locations than at your birthplace. Thus, if you'd like a little romance, maybe your true love is around a Venus line. You may start a successful enterprise near a Jupiter line. Crossings, places where lines intersect each other on the map, affect not only the area where they occur, but also any area on the crossing's latitude. This means that at sucha latitude you will feel the influence ofall the planets involved in the crossing, no matter how far away the actual crossing occurred at the time of birth. Charts and maps of personalities in the entertainment field, such as Joan Crawford, Marilyn Monroe, Grace Kelly, Yul Brynner, James Dean, John Lennon and David Bowie, are included in this compilation. Activists like Martin Luther King, Jr. and Lech Walesa and spiritual pioneers like Freud, Jung and Yogananda are presented. Events in the lives of painters, musicians and sports figures are explored through the maps. The successes, problems and tendencies of such politicians as FDR, Harry Truman, JFK, Richard Nixon, Ronald Reagan, George Bush, Jesse Jackson and Margaret Thatcher are related to their maps. The book closes with maps and charts of nations, including the U.S., the United Kingdom, and Israel, and events, such as the voyage of the Titanic, the first atomic bomb test and the first ingestion of LSD. The prominence of each planetary line in the lives and events of people and nations has also been tabulated. These charts reveal in a glance the correlation between one's profession and the general planetary vibrations s/he found most beneficial to progress and success. An order form at the back of the book allows you to receive your personalized Astro*Carto*Graphy map. Explore the world and your potential by finding your special places on Earth.

## Page 5

### JIM LEWIS &

<!-- chunk-id: acg-src-lewis-guttman-1989.page-0005.chunk-001 -->
<!-- chunk-sha256: e0c96d0bb3ace911c85902d43ae060e44b5622cb6bc94dac8a212b4e080f8b98 -->
Jim Lewis is an internationally recognized authority on relocation astrology. He began his professional career in 1969 asa personal service astrologer for American Astrology magazine, then under the guidance of the late Donald A. Bradley. In 1972, he moved to San Francisco where he has served a large clientele, taught classes, and appeared on numerous radio and TV programs. In 1975, he began work on ASTRO*CARTO#GRAPHY, and now devotes most of his time to popularizing the concepts of locational astrology. His work with ASTRO*CARTO*GRAPHY earned him the Marc Edmund Jones Award for the outstanding contribution to astrology at the 1978 National Astrological Society Convention. Since then, he has appeared on the faculties of almost every important astrological conference; he was one of five Americans selected to attend the First and Second World conferences in Switzerland in 1981 and 1984. His lecturing credits now include 12 nations and 20 states and provinces. ASTRO*CARTO* GRAPHY is available in six languages. In 1982, he completed work on CYCLO*CARTO*GRAPHY, an important adjunct to ASTRO*CARTO*GRAPHY, for which he was awarded a U.S. patent. In 1987, ASTRO*CARTO* GRAPHY E*PLAINED, a computerized interpretive service that analyzes locations according to the principles of A*C*G and creates a written report, was completed. As wellas contributing frequent articles on many aspects of astrology to the popular press, he annually publishes the Source Book of Mundane Maps. He has been very active in AFAN, an activist organization serving the astrological community, since 1982.

### ARIEL GUTTMAN

<!-- chunk-id: acg-src-lewis-guttman-1989.page-0005.chunk-002 -->
<!-- chunk-sha256: c2a79d73b24c4234d605bf59c6edeb1389a7eca06d1cb70119e68519ce6153c9 -->
Born in St. Louis, Missouri in 1949, Ariel began her formal studies in astrology in San Diego, California where she resided for 12 years. In 1980, she founded Astro Originals, an Encinitas, California-based consulting firm wherein the language of astrology could be utilized and recognized as an important self-awareness tool. In presenting astrological workshops to the public, Ariel has continually sought to introduce material that makes astrology more "grounded" while honoring its mystical and spiritual framework. As a lecturer for the astrological faculties of SWAC, ISAR, UAC, AFA and as past Research Director for the San Diego Astrological Society, Ariel has presented astrological research on topics of compatibility, the goddesses of astrology, A*C*G, and health and wellbeing. In addition, she has been a faculty member at Well Within, a Holistic Education Seminar Network in California and the Western States. There she has presented "Astrology's Role in the Healing Arts" to hundreds of nurses, nurse practitioners, chiropractors, acupuncturists, massage therapists, yoga instructors and health educators. Ariel and her husband Michael have recently relocated to Santa Fe, New Mexico, her "Mercury" line, where she finds a stimulating environment suitable for pursuing her writing career, the photographic arts and astrological counseling. She also maintains an office in Encinitas, California at the Infinite Winds Metaphysical Counseling Center. To Write to the Authors We cannot guarantee that every letter written to the authors can be answered, but all will be forwarded. Both the authors and the publisher appreciate hearing from readers, learning of your enjoyment and benefit from this book. Llewellyn also publishes a bi-monthly news magazine with news and reviews of practical esoteric studies and articles helpful to the student, and some readers' questions and comments to the author may be answered through this magazine's columns if permission to do is included in the original letter. The author sometimes participates in seminars and workshops, and dates and places are announced in The Llewellyn New Times. To

## Page 6

<!-- chunk-id: acg-src-lewis-guttman-1989.page-0006.chunk-001 -->
<!-- chunk-sha256: 9e4c4f1e6aaf4a67422f790411fa5fdb23d65196a48f671c8900c08952c1e960 -->
For those who are not aware of it, Astro*Carto*Graphy is one of the most revolutionary developments in modern astrology. While it grew out of the work of a number of independent workers, the astrological world owes Jim Lewis an everlasting debt for bringing it into a form that is accessible to all who have need of it. Generating an Astro*Carto*Graphy map is an instance of a computer-assisted astrological technique that is more than merely difficult to compute by hand, it is nearly impossible. Astro*Carto*Graphy is the shining example of how the computer can expand astrological horizons. But AstroxCarto*Graphy is more than a technically significant astrological method. It shows more clearly than almost any other natal astrology technique the close relationship between the individual and the cosmos. Not only are we connected to the larger rhythms of the solar system in time, Astro*Carto*Graphy shows how we are also connected to them in space. The horoscope is translated from a map of power points in time to a map of the power points in space. Those who have heard Jim Lewis lecture are aware of the power of the technique in explaining patterns in the lives of famous individuals and history. With this book of case studies, it is now possible for others who have not had access to Jim's lectures to see this as well. In alrnost every instance where I personally knew of the circumstances of an individual's life, | found that a quick perusal of the Astro*Carto*Graphy for that person showed symbolically appropriate planet lines at that place. Astro*Carto*Graphy is not one of those astrological techniques that requires bending and stretching of astrological principles to get results. The results are almost always clear and definite, and they assist the astrologer in understanding the dynarnics of astrologica] symbols. This book is important in another way—as evidence for astrology. In any single case study one could, if one were so inclined, choose to disregard the correlations between life events and Astro*Carto*Graphy lines as mere chance or the result of careful selection by the astrologer. However, there are so many case studies in this book which cover sucha wide range of personalities and life histories, some even involving several characters in a particular historical episode, that it is impossible to regard the results as solely due to chance. This book is powerful evidence for astrological effects in life and history. —Robert Hand

### President, Astrolabe Software

<!-- chunk-id: acg-src-lewis-guttman-1989.page-0006.chunk-002 -->
<!-- chunk-sha256: 57444a7ff51057da9e66f707b38dbcbee8a885c9fd8f6be0c1fad656aeb27e59 -->
The traditional way of charting the planets at the moment of someone's birth is convenient and relatively easy to figure, but it is also very limited and one dimensional. With Jin Lewis's Astro*Carto*Graphy maps, astrology moves beyond the static idea of an isolated birthplace. Your "horoscope" becomes the whole world, and you can emphasize various parts of the whole by moving to places marked by the lines on the map. Ariel] Guttrnan deserves high praise for sparking a working relationship that has produced a marvelous explanation of how Astro*Carto*Graphy works in the lives of real people. —Kenneth Irving Co-editor, American Astrology magazine . ## Page 9

### FOREWORD

<!-- chunk-id: acg-src-lewis-guttman-1989.page-0009.chunk-001 -->
<!-- chunk-sha256: a3887707c0942ad48f32a04a2adb5169f524661c9bbccdc9134e0533507cde75 -->
This is a book written by a committee of two, Jim Lewis and Ariel Guttman. Shortly after Ariel's successful coordination of the 1986 ASTRO*CARTO*GRAPHY Professional Seminar in Laguna Beach, she and Jim talked about the attendees' requests (heard often before) for a book of ASTRO*CARTO*GRAPHY maps of famous people from the examples used in the Seminar. Since Jim had lots to do running ASTRO* CARTO*GRAPHY and Ariel had her own busy practice and planned a move to Santa Fe, both agreed that the other one should do all the work. Somehow, this book was the result. It is truly a book about relocation and was created in San Francisco, Santa Fe, Boulder, and Encinitas, California. Ariel did the preliminary research in the library and offered ideas, particularly about the artists, writers, and entertainers. Jim drew from his decade of experience in ASTRO*CARTO*GRAPHY and contributed insights particularly to the analyses of politicians, activists, and spiritual pioneers. Special thanks should go to our editor, Kathy Halgren, who made the resulting patchwork into a whole cloth; to Lois Rodden, who verified birth data; and to Richard Nolle, who created the accompanying horoscopes using Matrix's Blue Star software on his IBM XT computer. Anyone with observations on the maps or ASTRO* CARTO* GRAPHY is welcome to write the authors at: ASTRO*CARTO*GRAPHY, Box 959, El Cerrito, CA 94530.

## Page 10

### INTRODUCTION What Is Astro*Carto*Graphy?

<!-- chunk-id: acg-src-lewis-guttman-1989.page-0010.chunk-001 -->
<!-- chunk-sha256: 960870fdfd4b4faf98ae39b87d3f333cbc2a35a1a1ad1edc2419ece9bde09cee -->
"Under Saturn's influence in Colorado, I worked very hard ona ranch for low pay and spent my spare time rock climbing."! Prior to 1976, few astrologers would have been able to make much sense out of this statement. But since then, astrology has been augmented by a new technique which has more to do with "where" than "when." Saturn used to be found only in the sky as a malefic, wizened killjoy, spending two anda half years making natives of any one astrological sign miserable as he "afflicted" them. How did he get to Colorado? Astrology was developed back when nobody went anyplace. But now, people move far and frequently; in California, most people live at least 500 miles away from their birthplace, a distance traversed only by a minute percentage of humans prior to this century. And so, astrology has had to catch up and has done so with ASTRO*CARTO*#GRAPHY which enables a person to determine which parts of the natal chart potential will be accented, highlighted, or brought into consciousness in a new location. Currently, astrology accepts that different places accentuate different potentials for an individual through a system that can be used to understand and plan in advance where you might find your Saturn, Venus, or Uranus. Using this system, astrology can anticipate how relocation will change an individual's life experience.

### About Modern Astrology

<!-- chunk-id: acg-src-lewis-guttman-1989.page-0010.chunk-006 -->
<!-- chunk-sha256: 7f5219c0ae175401a76cd1a4c7ad320e96e79faa3a148bbb390169dc44d85a7f -->
For those unacquainted with modern astrology, a few words might be useful. Most astrologers no longer believe in cause and effect as the principle behind astrology's validity—they do not believe that planets at birth or later on in life "make things happen." Rather, they see a "synchronistic" universe, one in which events ofa similar type are linked by a mysterious connecting principle, a principle which has only recently begun to be defined by scientist Rupert Sheldrake. Astrologers consider that this connecting principle (which Sheldrake calls "morphic resonance") links the birth moment and the individual with whatever else was going on in the world at that instant. Simplistically then, people born in the 1950s will tend as a group to the unquestioning conformity and optimism of that time. Others, born in the '60s, will be more rebellious, iconoclastic, and willing to change and modify institutions. Of course, within such large groups, individual differences will be apparent. And both the individual differences and the broader group identity can be associated symbolically with the positions of planets at the moment of the subject's birth. If Saturn was powerful in the sky at the moment of your birth, then its themes (which have been defined and developed by millenia of observation by astrologers) will dominate your character; you will be expected to be sensitive, reserved, ambitious, autonomous, and interested in rock climbing. Or, if you choose not to assimilate or express these attributes, astrology will expect you to find them outside yourself in other people and external situations.

Can You Take the Moon with You? But what happens if you move from your birthace? Do you take your planets with you, along with ur crystal collection? Since the birth chart is a product the time and place of birth and reproduces the sky at occurred right there and then, if you move away ym your birthplace, you have made a potential change. 5TRO*CARTO*GRAPHY creates a map geographilly identifying places where you are apt to find certain anets' influences to be stronger than they were where yu were born. So, if Saturn was straight overhead in ylorado at the moment of your birth in Massachusetts, is there in Denver that you are most apt to encounter s potential for austerity, individualistic accomplishent, hard work, and introspection. Your moment of birth cannot change, but if you ove, you encounter in a new location the birth chart at you would have had if you'd been born there. So, your Colorado birth chart shows Saturn in a power- ] position, when you go there you will meet up with m on both inner and outer planes of awareness. While it is probably best not to get into complex chnicalities here, suffice it to say that "strength" of a anet in a natal or relocated horoscope is determined ; that planet's angularity—how close it is situated to ur particularly sensitive points in the chart: the Ascenint, Descendant, Midheaven, and IC. So, a location here you meet Saturn is one where Saturn would ive occupied one of these positions of strength in yur chart if you had been born there. Of course, everyone's potential for Saturn is not e same. A few people thrive on his stern, ambitious, therly energy, while most others feel confined, limited, id thwarted. How you will react to any planet is

### rgely determined by other factors in your birth chart

<!-- chunk-id: acg-src-lewis-guttman-1989.page-0011.chunk-002 -->
<!-- chunk-sha256: 9c0b2fd4a3aa784b619454340707e2ef19eb6fb6ba8484e2227421515d36e480 -->
yw it relates to other planets, the "house" in which it ll at birth, and your social position, expectations, and heritance. Yet even with personal variations (which n best be understood through detailed study of your art and natal astrology), a consistent pattern will nerge for anyone under the influence of a particular anet. The ancients observed planets and personality r thousands of years. One of the mysteries of astrology e skeptics never try to address is how Chinese, ztec, and Babylonian astrologers all came up with ughly the same psychological characteristics for the anet Mars, characteristics that have been scientifically related (by Michel and Francoise Gauquelin) with ialogous occupations in modern times.

### To sum it up then, we have seen that 1. Planets don't make things happen, rather, events

<!-- chunk-id: acg-src-lewis-guttman-1989.page-0011.chunk-003 -->
<!-- chunk-sha256: 90560d8e2abb54b8d832ec78e54364c26d31bf8511c8aaba913715e2cb5e6e3a -->
linked by similarity of basic theme seem to cluster together so that a person bornina particular time is impressed with the character of that time.

### 2. These themes of personality and eventuality can

<!-- chunk-id: acg-src-lewis-guttman-1989.page-0011.chunk-004 -->
<!-- chunk-sha256: e2a0ca2afb26efd5a9c70b2300575a8342f9762eee2e928785e1d15dc7848201 -->
be recognized in a birth chart or horoscope of the moment of a person's birth.

### 3. The relative strength of planetary components of

<!-- chunk-id: acg-src-lewis-guttman-1989.page-0011.chunk-005 -->
<!-- chunk-sha256: 5a4c006a71b36d1b8c4fd86d1af021fe36eca5d1d09d7530ad168a6d49d05545 -->
this horoscope can be altered by changing location, which can bring different planets into the foreground of consciousness. The ASTRO*CARTO*GRAPHY map identifies just where, geographically, such forces are most apt to be encountered by drawing lines across a map showing where planets held positions of power at birth. The Voice of Experience The following is a short list of interpretive principles determined to be valid from the observation of hundreds of ASTRO*CARTO*GRAPHY maps over the past decade. These statements are not meant to be a definitive manual as to how to read your own ASTRO*CARTO*GRAPHY map but rather a guide to the elementary aspects of the technique needed to appreciate the significance of the example maps that follow.

### 1. Lines Are Permanent. Just as your genetic potential

<!-- chunk-id: acg-src-lewis-guttman-1989.page-0011.chunk-006 -->
<!-- chunk-sha256: a64bef09d653c7cb09208a370a20a51c09feedaa0f074535d54e218cee1a38ad -->
is encoded in each cell at conception, your astrological portrait is fixed at the moment of birth, as are the places where it is most apt to manifest.

### 2. Lines Are Modified by Time. To extend the genetic

<!-- chunk-id: acg-src-lewis-guttman-1989.page-0011.chunk-007 -->
<!-- chunk-sha256: 6372d6cf7e60adadd5587f964e24e7fd08bf68aa8ac30b8b969fd3c0c6e1a15f -->
analogy, time and interaction with the environment change an individual, allowing development of the expression of the natal potential. Astrologically, these modifications over time are symbolized by progressions and transits. On a map, they are represented by CYCLO*CARTO*GRAPHY, another set of lines, temporary and changing, used to supplement the birth lines and to indicate activation of the potentials by time. An individual with an ASTRO*

### CARTO*GRAPHY map may bring it up to date

<!-- chunk-id: acg-src-lewis-guttman-1989.page-0011.chunk-008 -->
<!-- chunk-sha256: 83d4625e93fc9a7026252fca3a5ef16c04221b7ec8970af313b3e68a923215fb -->
with a patented plastic overlay which fits on top of the natal map and adds temporary lines to the permanent ones. In the examples that follow, mention is made of such temporary influences, though overlays are not possible in the printed medium.

### 3. Remote Activation. One does not actually have to

<!-- chunk-id: acg-src-lewis-guttman-1989.page-0011.chunk-009 -->
<!-- chunk-sha256: a42651c20d52350cd627d2e18334bb2a87f6c2bdc48e46f0177469f0120e059d -->
visit a place to be affected by it; many examples show the individual being affected by a location's

<a id="acg-src-lewis-guttman-1989.page-0012"></a>
<!-- page-id: acg-src-lewis-guttman-1989.page-0012 -->
## Page 12

### Introduction

<!-- chunk-id: acg-src-lewis-guttman-1989.page-0012.chunk-001 -->
<!-- chunk-sha256: 6eade1013aeee18e949cd07e6ef4d5ac25149b68a125ea11e3f24fb032eed73b -->
planet energies just by dealing with it from afar. Places of No Influence. These don't come up often in the examples given but are generally places where no particular part of the planetary repertoire is accented or emphasized beyond its natal importance or where uniformly stressful natal potentials are evaded. Crossings. Obviously,a place where two or more lines cross is affected by each of the planet energies indicated. But also, anywhere in the world along the latitude of that exact crossing is similarly affected. Example: Jimmy Carter's Jupiter and Mars lines cross at Teheran, but Mars and Jupiter affected him while he was in Washington, which is near the very same latitude. How Close Do You Have toBe? The closerto the line, the more pronounced the effects, but some effects can be felt as far as 700 miles (1,000 kilometers) away. A line passing through Denver might be felt as far east as Kansas City and as far west as Las Vegas, particularly if it were the only line in that vicinity. On the maps in this book, this "orb" of influence amounts to about 1/4" or 7 millimeters. However, the closest line to a particular location should theoretically identify the most potent planetary influence, regardless of its distance. Those Little Boxes on the MH Lines are the zeniths— the places where each planet was directly overhead at the birth moment. They describe a potential center of intense energy and also have latitudinal effect; that is, they affect any place on the same latitude at which they occur. Where All the Lines Cross near the North Pole. Where this many lines cross, they would tend to cancel one another out and have much the same effect as no lines at all; still, it would be an intense place and one which most people have found unpleasant—for the climate, if for no other reason. "Good" Planets and "Bad" Planets. There is no good or evil in astrology. It is only what we do with the planetary energies that might be classified in this fashion. People are constantly looking for "sood" locations for themselves, but astrology teaches that the natal planets are all lessons, stages, ~ and necessary parts of one's maturation. Just as your liver isn't "better" than your pelvis, each is good for something and a part of the whole. Problems result only when one tries to use the right planet for the wrong purposes. It's like trying to run your automobile on margarine or the human body on Chevron No-Lead. Astrology hopes to help you to understand the nature of the planet and use its potentials for your growth and maturation. In this book, the same planet will emerge as "good" and "bad" in different cases, but in either case it will retain its essential character. The definition of this essential character is one of the purposes of this study. The Use of Famous People With few exceptions, we have used here the maps of well-known individuals, a practice that is sometimes questioned by astrologers. Famous people exhibit certain advantages as objects for astrological study. What has happened to them is generally well documented so that a technique can be judged on the basis of a known, historical past. And future developments in their lives are also apt to be widely publicized so the map can continue to be instructive as their lives unfold. In addition, it seems that famous people seem to offer some of the best examples of how ASTRO* CARTO*GRAPHY works. People become famous because they exemplify some universal concept of interest to everyone, and these universal concepts, called archetypes, are the same ones that are symbolized by the planets. So a "match" on their maps is apt to be even more impressive and memorable. Interpreting the map of an "ordinary person" is only slightly different. Liz Taylor moved from London to Los Angeles where she had four powerful lines. If you move to the same lines, it is unlikely you'll star in Cleopatra, be given gigantic diamonds, and pioneer fund-raising for AIDS research. But you might have a couple of ill-chosen affairs, take acting lessons, and volunteer to help the elderly. It's just a matter of degree in the expression of the same symbols. But why is there need for a map of the whole world since so few people ever move outside their own nation? Because even people who never move far from their places of birth are affected by remote locations, as discussed previously. Also, the four lines of any one planet often form consistent patterns of life experience. In the map of the U.S.A. (Boyd's "Declaration of War"), for example, the evolution of this nation's military attitude and involvement is obvious from al the locations of Mars' lines. If the examples offered in the book inspire the reader to wish to obtain his/her own map, s/he will fine

<a id="acg-src-lewis-guttman-1989.page-0013"></a>
<!-- page-id: acg-src-lewis-guttman-1989.page-0013 -->
## Page 13

### Astro*Carto*Graphy

<!-- chunk-id: acg-src-lewis-guttman-1989.page-0013.chunk-001 -->
<!-- chunk-sha256: 3d8a8a812572e38e476d68e7f6790321a2230b0dc8b4ac1403cdec1e397957fc -->
information for this purpose at the end of the volume. Time, date, and place of birth are needed to complete a map for an individual. With such a map, you may better understand the planetary archetype under which you have chosen to live, the one you like to visit, and even those with which you are least comfortable. What is Venus? Why is She Descending? Probably the first accomplishment of prehistoric astrologers was the distinction between the "fixed" stars, whose only motion through the sky was a seasonal function of Earth's revolution about the Sun, and planets (the word itself is derived from the Greek "to wander'), which were observed to move through the constellations in cycles that became the basis of astrology. Over the centuries, meanings were ascribed to these planets from observation and from mythological association, and these meanings are the fundamental origins of the interpretations of time that astrologers generate. This book is obviously not the place for an exhaustive exploration of the significations attributed to the ten planetary archetypes (astrologers, for the sake of convenience, categorize the Sun and Moon along with the other eight planets, excluding Earth). Rather, it is intended to affirm and supplement the planetary meanings astrologers have used by demonstrating their applicability in a locational application with numerous, well-known examples. Still, the book would be incomplete without a brief description of the planets' traditional interpretations and associations. Following are brief synopses of each planet's meaning as usually expressed when dominant in a particular location." This brief listing is intended mostly as amemory aid or guide for the reader unacquainted with astrology; any beginning astrology text will present a far more thorough inventory of the psychological and practical matters associated with these most basic astrological archetypes. SUN: Here, you become master of your own fate and attract others' envy, good wishes, favor and admiration. This is the best place to practice theatre, creative arts, leadership, or to seek publicity. Health improves, and rehabilitation, self-actualization and promotion go well. Dangers lie in coming on too strong, arrogance, inconsideration and egotism. MOON: Under these lines, you are completely immersed in sensation and become entangled, vulnerable and involved with others. Nurturing capacity, sympathy, sentimentality, sensitivity and spontaneity are responses to constant environmental change; needs and appetites emerge powerfully. Negatively, it can mean impermanence, dependence and emotionalism. MERCURY: These areas get you in touch with how you best make contact or have intercourse with the outer world. Symbols, words, writing, ideas, education, work skills, communication, travel, mobility and youthful mental creativity are emphasized here. Negatively, there can be lack of commitment, superficiality, amorality and petty criminality (or exposure to it). VENUS: Here, self-perfection and growth encourage relationship. Love and marriage mates are found under Venus lines. Beauty, sensuality, luxury, money, ease and artistic interests dominate; self-esteem and appearance improve. Dangers lie in consumerism, laziness, superficiality, objectification of people and in an immature, make-believe attitude. MARS: This area is where masterful, dominating, courageous, sexual, passionate instincts take over, but there is danger of attacks, accidents, rashness, injury and contentiousness. Still, zest for life increases, engineering, individual athletics, competition or building succeed, and power is found and mobilized to take care of self. JUPITER: Prosperity, success, social acceptance, exposure to the rich and powerful, status and conformity beckon from this zone. You are taken care of and coddled with success in publishing and team sports. Elevated living standards are balanced against possible boredom, bourgeois complacency and pomposity. Lucky breaks in material and career concerns are likely. SATURN: Fierce personal independence and perseverance develop in the face of difficulties in health, power struggles, poor finances and solitude under these lines. Challenge, maturity and hard work are met and accepted. Though they complain, people find it hard to leave these zones once entrenched by the responsibilities and feelings of personal power that come from accepting them. URANUS: In Uranus zones, the surprising becomes commonplace as boredom and social rigidity are shattered. You grow to be able to see who youare, apart from your social role, and rebel against City Hall, rules, conformity, and your own past; causes are adopted. * List taken from "How to Use Your ASTRO*CARTO*GRAPHY Map," text copyright 1980 by Astro*Carto*Graphy. Used with permission.

## Page 14

### Introduction

<!-- chunk-id: acg-src-lewis-guttman-1989.page-0014.chunk-001 -->
<!-- chunk-sha256: 7d6452cdbc877c1cc38f39f695c1adc717ec85913c90e31d4287497edc72fc8e -->
Impermanence and constant change must be accepted, along with possible scapegoating, inability to relate emotionally and alienation. NEPTUNE: Under Neptune lines, ideals are pursued, romanticism flowers, spiritual progress can be made, and photography, arts and music are inspired. But the price is a feeling of ineffable unconnectedness and the delusions, victimization and self-deception that develop from unrealism. Illness, dealings with the underground, mental strain and dependence on institutions portray the less glamorous side of the planet of illusions. PLUTO: Total alteration and new directions in life can be taken under these lines. Exposure to and concerns with death, regeneration and mortality are common. You may feel isolated, alienated and experience violent growth through intense inner upheaval so that society seems vapid or persecutory. Personal depth is gained, but what is old and outmoded in life will be stripped away so that complete rejuvenation and rebirth are possible. How's a Midheaven Different from a Descendant? Each ASTRO*CARTO*#GRAPHY map has 40 lines. Any one of the ten planets can appear on one of four angles or points of unusual sensitivity. Below, the

### meanings of the four angles are briefly summarized

<!-- chunk-id: acg-src-lewis-guttman-1989.page-0014.chunk-002 -->
<!-- chunk-sha256: 1f529a7a332944fce4867d9eb2244972c2bf9f5819bd3a693a1ba320d662621a -->
_ Midheaven In this position, the planet expresses itself socially so that the milieu and environment in which one finds oneself resemble the nature of the planet. One is socially classified by the planet and seen by others as an example of its action and attributes. This is apparently the "prime angle" for the planet, the place where an individual is most apt to come into awareness of the part of his or her inner self the planet symbolizes.

### Ascendant

<!-- chunk-id: acg-src-lewis-guttman-1989.page-0014.chunk-003 -->
<!-- chunk-sha256: 10264a87a547ad16fd2e94c27c413d4f17be6bc3a38116832ef0f8f89ac54f2f -->
On this angle, the force of the planet expresses itself through direct, outward, personal expression. The individual's personality focuses the force of the planet as a lens does light. For example, where Mars occupies the Ascendant, one acts Martian—aggressive, direct, physically active, boisterous, etc. One is identified by the planet's nature.

### Imum Coeli

<!-- chunk-id: acg-src-lewis-guttman-1989.page-0014.chunk-004 -->
<!-- chunk-sha256: e1449582aaae56729d904bef4dd6f4e2e45961dede83d66f0808483e9b5c9f7a -->
At this angle, the force and nature of the planet act to center or ground the individual and identify what becomes the inward foundation of self concept. Issues of the home and family are usually involved here, and the planet's symbols identify the manner in which the individual conceptualizes his/her origins and how s/he deals with these origins. Security, attachment, and belonging are found through the actions of the planet so that one is centered through the planet's nature.

### Descendant

<!-- chunk-id: acg-src-lewis-guttman-1989.page-0014.chunk-005 -->
<!-- chunk-sha256: 76c7e17e54e9127d1cf3f23f495be98a704689b03b895dc91e18c389f6dec333 -->
Here the force and nature of the planet expresses itself by drawing to the individual external circumstances, people, and relationships akin to its own nature. By a usually unconscious projection of need, these situations are attracted and force the individual to oppose, respond to, and eventually assimilate the capacities of the planet inwardly. Difficult planets can be most dangerous in this position since they tend to create external challenges and out of control problems in strongly polarized forms.

<a id="acg-src-lewis-guttman-1989.page-0015"></a>
<!-- page-id: acg-src-lewis-guttman-1989.page-0015 -->
## Page 15

### THE MAPS

<!-- chunk-id: acg-src-lewis-guttman-1989.page-0015.chunk-001 -->
<!-- chunk-sha256: 8da1e6fca94ac88364a78bbc3c2b9b7a8509f208b3009165e0a5e2676f3917b1 -->
Data Sources and Categories In each case, the data used for map generation are the best available at the time of publication, except in those cases where use of speculative times of birth is clearly stated or in which there are differing, contradictory data in widespread use. Where contradictory data exist, the appropriateness of the map drawn for a reported birth time has been used as criterion for selection of that time. The authors wish to thank Lois Rodden, whose dedication to accuracy has made works such as this possible. Ms. Rodden's research is now publicly available through the International Society for Astrological Research (ISAR).3 See Appendix A for source data. The categories were arrived at by rather arbitrary means and are presented only to group maps of somewhat similar themes. Within categories, maps are presented in chronological order to facilitate comparisons between different generations' attitudes toward the planetary principles. Appendix B attempts to list every citation of a particular planet when it appears on an angle in any of the examples. This appendix will be useful in confirming that, for example, entertainers tend to gravitate to Sun and Moon locations, while politicians rather prefer Jupiter or Saturn.

### Abbreviations Used

<!-- chunk-id: acg-src-lewis-guttman-1989.page-0015.chunk-002 -->
<!-- chunk-sha256: 1bb2d9b4592123720cb9ffb9e6f0eab38e703bc2941c2cedfc543e61f9ca76a4 -->
Abbreviations used in the text to denote data from maps are those that have emerged as standard in other writings on ASTRO*CARTO*GRAPHY.

### The abbreviations for planets' lines are Sun: SU Mars: MA Neptune: NE Moon: MO Jupiter: JU Pluto: PL

### Mercury: ME Saturn: SA Venus: VE Uranus: UR Angles' abbreviations

### Midheaven: MH Ascendant: ASC Imum Coeli: IC

### Descendant: DSC

<!-- chunk-id: acg-src-lewis-guttman-1989.page-0015.chunk-003 -->
<!-- chunk-sha256: 8b1ea61d7e7901d43f9354c2da0b5ad8684d2eeb55a75834469d9ee42618bc5e -->
3 Rodden/ISAR Data Project (RID), P.O. Box 38613, Los Angeles, CA 90038. Whenever an actual planet line on a map is referred to in the text, it will always be abbreviated. For example, SU DSC means "Sun on the Descendant" and is the line on the map where the Sun was setting at the individual's birth moment. It is important to distinguish between planet lines and latitude crossings (parans). As noted previously, where any two lines ona map cross, the point of intersection establishes a significant latitude, and anywhere along that latitude the energies of the two planets will seem to be associated. If a direct planetary influence is being described, when the individual resides near the actual planet line it is designated by the abbreviation noted, such as SU DSC. But if the influence of a latitude crossing is being discussed, an asterisk is used in the abbreviation. A location, anywhere in the world, on the latitude at which the Sun Descendant line crosses the Uranus Midheaven line would be designated as "SU/DSC * UR/MH," or more simply, "SU*UR." When the text describes the influence of a planet in the natal horoscope generally or discourses on a planet's abstract significance, the name of the planet is spelled out. In the text, the word chart always refers to a conventional natal horoscope with its 12 houses (using the Tropical Placidus system), aspects, etc. The word map, on the other hand, only refers to the ASTRO* CARTO*GRAPHY map drawn from the birth data. Summary of Conventions Used in Text SU DSC: Refers to the actual planetary line on a map. SU/DSC*UR/MH or SU*UR: Refers to a latitude at which two planet lines cross. Sun and Uranus: Refers to actual planets or their positions in a natal horoscope.

## Page 18

### DOROTHY DAY NOVEMBER 8, 1897; 6:50 PM EST BROOKLYN, NY

<!-- chunk-id: acg-src-lewis-guttman-1989.page-0018.chunk-001 -->
<!-- chunk-sha256: 9c8396b318ae750c9712ada4ba48e140b57e880ea483f88f0520ddb0d1dcc1d4 -->
Neptune and Pluto, planets of which living archetypes are created, were placed precisely on the Ascendant at Dorothy Day's birth in New York, the place where she so fruitfully pursued her life's work. Her map is more extraordinary in that there is only one crossing in the latitudes of the U.S., NE*PL. Probably no one has so uncompromisingly committed his/her life to the fusion of the highest expression of these two planets' principles as she. Dorothy Day combined the religious idealism of Neptune with the social and political activism of Pluto. She has observed that her personal conflict was essentially that of "flesh and spirit." While not born into Catholicism, she converted as a young adult; with a URand SA conjunction at the IC through the Vatican, she tempered unquestioning obedience to the Church with an interpretation of Christ's teachings as demanding social action on behalf of the oppressed. Her SU, ME and VW MA IC lie over France where social utopian Peter Maurin, her philosophical mentor, was born. The misery and poverty of the world always remained in her conscience, and, like Mother Teresa and Gandhi, she insisted that her life have no more material comfort than the lives of the social outcasts she served. She was a lifelong socialist, something the Church obviously disapproved. Her writings and actions influenced several generations, among whom can be numbered the Berrigans, Ammon Hennacy and Daniel Ellsberg. She was a peace and anti-nuclear activist and was jailed more times than she could remember in her 83 years. She refused to "take cover" during nuciear air raid drills in the 1950s. Most of her life was dedicated to vocalizing the plight of the poor through the Catholic Worker, a newspaper she edited, and she organized a nationwide chain of "Hospitality Houses" which provide food and shelter for those she called the "socially and materially incapacitated." She often quoted Dostoevsky: "Love in action is a harsh and dreadful thing, compared to love in dreams."

## Page 20

### TIMOTHY LEARY OCTOBER 22, 1920; 10:45 AM EDT SPRINGFIELD, MA

<!-- chunk-id: acg-src-lewis-guttman-1989.page-0020.chunk-001 -->
<!-- chunk-sha256: b61184301c36ca7729e513e9440a0a427b31871c6f7ab6d4b1fac6c93e5648b3 -->
The "tune in, turn on, drop out" guru of the "60s seems to be doing still what he advocated then and is evidently no worse off. Here is a man who practices what he preaches. He became the spokesperson for millions of disillusioned youth, a generation with Neptune in Libra, who were ready to heed his call. Surely the subtle, hypnotic influence of Neptune should be prominent in this chart above all. And it sits in the Midheaven over the California coast spotlighted by SU ASC, once again illustrating that any planet the Sun shines its rays upon comes into brilliant, overriding focus. The Sun and Neptune together project surrender of the self to mystical, collective ideals. Leary successfully preached an alternative doctrine and attracted huge followings of people disillusioned by the straight world. Through LSD he hoped to make society think love, get in touch with universal currents, and realize that we are all one. 13 Leary did not start out on the West Coast but ratherina Boston Ivy League collegiate environment under his SA MH. Saturn demands tradition, conservatism and respect for authority. Once LSD began to dominate his life, California, the location of his Neptune, drew him to its freedom and structurelessness. Even on the West Coast, though, his ideas were unpopular with the authorities. Upon crossing the border back from Mexico at his UR/IC*ME/ASC crossing, the customs agents vacuumed his pockets and found a few milligrams of marijuana. Realizing that a "jury of his peers" would be unlikely, he fled to Algeria. When he outwore his welcome there, he went to Switzerland, the birthplace of LSD (see map elsewhere in this book). Both locations are under his JU DSC, assuring protection from authorities. But his luck ran out when he went to Afghanistan where he was captured by the CIA under his NEIC line and sent back to California where he did four years at Soledad (NE MH). He has since continued living in California and has written books about space exploration and expanding consciousness.

### DANIEL BERRIGAN

## Page 22

### DANIEL BERRIGAN MAY 9, 1921; 6:30 PM CST VIRGINIA, MN

<!-- chunk-id: acg-src-lewis-guttman-1989.page-0022.chunk-001 -->
<!-- chunk-sha256: fd10d66361024eea906a1edf28a3dbe3ef58aa7d939f2984310d3714f5ef0a34 -->
Roman Catholic Priest and anti-war activist, Daniel Berrigan, along with his brother Philip, actively protested the war in Viet Nam and became a top name on the FBI's enemy list. In 1971, he wrote a documentary of the trial in which he, his brother and seven others (the "Catonsville Nine") were found guilty of destroying draft records in the Selective Service Office in Baltimore, Maryland (URIC, JU MH). He was convicted and spent time in prison but was paroled shortly thereafter. Early in his career he traveled to France where he supported the efforts of factory workers to better their conditions. MA IC with NE DSC suggests a militancy 15 on behalf of idealistic causes as one of the more constructive possible manifestations of these ill-matched planets. Both he and his brother have MO lines in Viet Nam. Daniel has the ASC, attuning him personally to suffering there, while Philip has the DSC. Jane Fonda (q.v.) had the ASC there also, indicating that MO lines generally identify localities in which feelings of human empathy are so strong as to demand personal sacrifice. Both Oliver North and Richard Secord (q.v.) had MO lines in Iran and Nicaragua, respectively. Daniel also has his exact UR-JU opposition on the meridian in Viet Nam, with nonconformist and revolutionary UR on the Midheaven. The two planets appear on opposite angles in the Washington, D.C. area along with the charismatic SU DSC line.

<!-- chunk-id: acg-src-lewis-guttman-1989.page-0024.chunk-001 -->

### PHILIP BERRIGAN OCTOBER 5, 1923; 12:45 AM CST TWO HARBORS, MN

<!-- chunk-id: acg-src-lewis-guttman-1989.page-0024.chunk-002 -->
<!-- chunk-sha256: 63dc49a32a6ef802b69ecef73dbbc2c206a5ad2a312e0e56f4b3ea5161db36dd -->
The first Roman Catholic clergyman ever sentenced to jail in the U.S. for political crimes, Philip Berrigan served three years and two months in prison during the tumultuous '60s for challenging U.S. government policy in Viet Nam. He admitted to the destruction of selective service records in draft board offices in Baltimore and Catonsville, Maryland and was charged with smuggling letters in and out of the Lewisburg, Pennsylvania federal penitentiary where he was incarcerated in 1970 under his NE ASC. During World War II, he earned medals for his tour of duty in France where his VE and SA conjunction rises. After the war, he became a Josephite Father assigned to New Orleans to work with the poor among the black community. "Racism and the Christian View" Sb 17 was a frequent theme in his writings and work. He quickly became an embarrassment to the religious and secular hierarchy in the South so was transferred to a seminary in Newburgh, New York, a blue-collar town of conservatives where he fared no better. Though still on his NE ASC, he was outspoken and increasingly militant in his views. He was then transferred to Baltimore. Very concerned about America's role in Viet Nam, he spent much of his time lobbying in Washington. On October 27, 1967, he and three other peace activists entered the Selective Service Office in Baltimore and began pouring ducks' blood over the files to symbolize the human blood that was being spilled in Viet Nam. After a lengthy and spectacular trial in which he defended his actions as motivated by a higher moral law (NE), he was incarcerated. Since his release, he has been active in peace, environmental and liberal causes throughout the world.

## Page 26

### MARTIN LUTHER KING, JR. JANUARY 15, 1929; 11:21 AM CST ATLANTA, GA

<!-- chunk-id: acg-src-lewis-guttman-1989.page-0026.chunk-001 -->
<!-- chunk-sha256: 7fc8d3b9e66f22e411259c81fe81299d33ee077b83677ee988d8996b2fb8751c -->
AUTHORS' NOTE: This time has been rectified 39 minutes, using the A*C*G map, from his mother's remembered birth time of Noon. Martin Luther King, Jr. was born witha potent cardinal T-square involving the Sun, Pluto and Jupiter, all aspecting the USA's natal Pluto. He first emerged as a Baptist minister in Atlanta, Georgia under his JU ASC. Quickly assuming leadership in the nascent civil rights movement, he became a national symbol for equality, peace, transformation and nonviolence. In 1963, he led 300,000 followers, the largest protest in recent American history, on a peaceful march to Washington, a city which would many years later honor him with a national holiday (SU MH). This event seemed the turning point in this century's effort in the never-ending struggle for racial equality. 19 In the tumultuous, turbulent "60s, popular opinion, fueled by the Uranus-Pluto conjunction, demanded social and political revolution, and King was able to direct this energy towards unifying black people, instilling in them a sense of political and social clout, and furthering efforts towards world peace. For his efforts, he was awarded the Nobel Peace Prize in Stockholm in 1964 under his JU MH. An assassin's bullet ended his life in Memphis, Tennessee (PL IC) in 1968. The arch-conservative former Governor of the State of Arizona refused to observe the national holiday in King's honor, insisting that King should not be equated with American presidents. SA MH, MA IC and the MO ASC sat over Arizona on King's map, echoing Gandhi's (g.v.) planets in South Africa.

## Page 28

### JIM JONES MAY 13, 1931; 10:00 PM CST LYNN, IN

<!-- chunk-id: acg-src-lewis-guttman-1989.page-0028.chunk-001 -->
<!-- chunk-sha256: 57165353b81c56b0abe773cb92d34c65f99ed5b0fa6c3efb2064e1cce8bcf61d -->
The early 1930s were marked by a T-square of Uranus, Saturn and Pluto in cardinal signs. This configuration was amplified and focused in Jim Jones' chart by the addition of Venus, Jupiter and the Moon. Jones was the self-appointed guru and leader of the People's Temple who led some 900 followers to their deaths in the South American commune he christened "Jonestown." Why would so many people surrender their wills to this man? The blind faith of Neptune must be involved somewhere. A SU*NE crossing lies off the coast of California at San Francisco's latitude, a pair evident in Tim Leary's chart as well (see previous P| map in this section). Both attracted unquestioning followers in San Francisco. When he had amassed enough disciples (and their worldly belongings) to finance his trip, he set forth to Guyana. Guyana contains for Jones an exact crossing of the SU IC and MA DSC, an intensely belligerent pair. The SU suggests leadership, charisma and arrogance, while MA on the DSC projects violence and sexuality outward. The two together indicate unmitigated masculinity— aggressive, paranoid and stubborn—particularly since they occupy Taurus and Leo. The SU focuses MA, in this case with tragic results. Jones' natal Mars also forms an exact paran with the ill-reputed fixed star Algol at Jonestown's exact latitude, which is also that of the MO, UR and VE zeniths and their crossings with JU, PL and SA.

## Page 30

### ANITA BRYANT MARCH 25, 1940; 3:10 PM CST BARNSDALL, OK

<!-- chunk-id: acg-src-lewis-guttman-1989.page-0030.chunk-001 -->
<!-- chunk-sha256: 5748ed54509dff40171250126e7cde57179a0abbdd144ae8223258319d52edaa -->
A former Miss Oklahoma (VE and UR MH) and runnerup for Miss America, Anita Bryant emerged publicly as a media representative for the Florida Citrus Commission. An Aries Sun with a powerful 10th House holding career-oriented Mars, she has fared well on her Mars lines. Earlier in her career, she performed in many Oklahoma productions. When she tried for success in Hollywood (PL ASC, SA MH), they thought her "too mature" for her age. Success did come in New York, however, with Arthur Godfrey's talent scouts. In Chicago (MA MH), she did the "Breakfast Club Show" while attending Northwestern University. She married 23 Bob Greene, a disc jockey from Miami Beach (MA MH), and he became her manager, carefully selecting her engagements to emphasize family-oriented values and avoiding the hedonistic nightclub scene. She performed with Bob Hope in Viet Nam and has given acommand performance to the King and Queen of Thailand (MA IC in both locations). Her pro-Christian, intensely anti-sexual sentiments have made her acontroversial figure, and in San Francisco (PL ASC, JU MH) her anti-gay demagoguery has earned her the hatred of thousands. On Miami's latitude, a crossing of her UR and VE with PL assures that controversy will pursue her there as well. Her moralistic, pro-family image suffered when she filed for divorce.

## Page 32

### LECH WALESA SEPTEMBER 29, 1943; 3:30 AM EET POPOVO, POLAND

<!-- chunk-id: acg-src-lewis-guttman-1989.page-0032.chunk-001 -->
<!-- chunk-sha256: 9f9368069be35b8ddc878cab4c37d54241e6b30bc615412cb83a25e3e03d66a6 -->
Synonymous with Solidarity, the rebel Polish trade union, Lech Walesa has become one of the leading newsmakers of the decade and certainly Poland's second most-famous citizen after Pope John Paul II (with whom he has a strong alliance). Since July 1980 when massive workers' strikes in Poland began, he has fought an inspiring personal battle for the rights of the working classes in the face of Soviet satellite bureaucracy and oppression. In 1970, he moved to Gdansk on the Baltic coast (SU*PL latitude crossing) and was fired from his job because of his protests on behalf of workers there. With natally stationary-direct Venus on the Ascendant in Poland, he seems to promote harmony and justice. Phe) JU ASC through central Europe assures good press and the support of governments there, including the Vatican. Of course, with UR MH right through Moscow, his effects on the entrenched Communist bureaucracy were disruptive, revolutionary and entirely unappreciated. But he became a hero to the workers of the world, and protests from around the globe, particularly from Uranian Western democracies, in support of this man made him impossible to silence or ignore. Still, he has been in and out of prison. With PLIC through Washington and UR ASC nearby, he would probably be as unpopular there as he is in Moscow were he to be working for justice for American workers. Since he is the Soviets' problem, Washington cautiously supports his positions. ME*UR and SU*MaA also cross at Washington's latitude.

## Page 34

### ANGELA DAVIS JANUARY 26, 1944; 12:30 PM CWT GOODWATER, AL

<!-- chunk-id: acg-src-lewis-guttman-1989.page-0034.chunk-001 -->
<!-- chunk-sha256: a8afc3983fc92769366fa95633289aa09c8d00cb26e714bae50b0d8455e24446 -->
The century's foremost black female intellectual, Angela Davis was fired from her position as professor of philosophy at UCLA after she was arrested in 1970. She was charged with kidnapping, murder and conspiracy in a shootout at Marin County Courthouse although she was not present during the incident. The pressure to fire her from then-Governor Ronald Reagan stemmed more from her active involvement in the Communist Party than in the crime mentioned; her firing was later ruled unconstitutional. Always an excellent student, Angela attended school in New York under her SU, PL, UR and MA lines ona scholarship and was offered several others for graduate work. She attended Brandeis University, where she fell in love with a German exchange student (born under her MO and JU opposition). Both families opposed the union. While attending the Sorbonne in Paris, she sympathized with the French Algerians (SU DSC and PL ASC) and their racial plight as second-class citizens. Zz], Very taken with Marxist philosophy, she became a student and friend of Herbert Marcuse, political philosophy professor at Brandeis. She received her degree magna cum laude and then went on to study philosophy in Frankfurt, where she took to the German language and philosophers (especially Kant and Hegel) with enthusiasm. Her post-graduate studies were undertaken at the University of California, San Diego, after which she was hired to teach at the University of California, Los Angeles. However, her vocal commitment to the Communist party cost her her job. She was actively supportive of the Black Panther Party, the San Diego Black Conference, and the Communist Party. (Her MA MH passes exactly through Moscow conjunct UR MH.) After the Marin County incident, she was put on the FBI's 10 most-wanted fugitive list. She was captured in New York City, extradited to California and held without bail. Protest marchers from Ceylon to Paris with banners and shouts of "Free Angela!" eventually contributed to her release. She remains an important part of the progressive intellectual community on the West Coast under her VE MH.

## Page 37

### SIGMUND FREUD MAY 6, 1856; 6:30 PM LMT FREIBERG, MORAVIA

<!-- chunk-id: acg-src-lewis-guttman-1989.page-0037.chunk-001 -->
<!-- chunk-sha256: d5881fb99f2dbcaed88dcd5dba0226257a98f6737ba75a02e541a17a622b3ba8 -->
Sigmund Freud proposed the theory of the unconscious, founded psychoanalysis, and has been called the father of modern psychology. He spent his life working in Vienna nearest his SU DSC line and so resembles Jung (q.v.) in this respect. While both had Suns powerful on the angular 7th House and could illuminate for their patients unrecognized regions of the psyche, the principal difference between them is Jung's SU line crossed his PL line while Freud's Sun was closely conjunct natal Uranus and in the same sign as Pluto. Pluto and Uranus both symbolize cathartic transformation, but while Uranus seems connected to ego, ideas and individuality, Pluto reflects the deeper processes of life itself. Freud identified far more strongly with Uranus, living closer beneath its lines, and his famous feud with Plutonian Jung illustrates the discomfort with which he regarded the total upheaval, change and death of the ego symbolized by Pluto. His obsession with the libido can also be traced to this discomfort, but in a time when sexual matters were never discussed, his focus on libidinous instincts, mitigated by appropriate Victorian disgust, was an important step forward for European medicine and philosophy. Four planetary crossings also dominate Vienna's latitude for Freud: MO/ASC*PL/ASC; VE/IC*SA/DSC; NE/IC*ME/DSC, and VE/DSC*PL/DSC. Clearly, the feminine archetype is linked here with unconscious forces so that it is not surprising that so many of his patients were women, oppressed by the limitations of Victorian society. On a trip to the northeastern U.S. with Jung in 1909, both were granted honorary degrees by Clark University (ME MH). Burnings by the Nazis of Freud's work began to occur in Berlin in 1933, so he exiled himself to London though he was loath to leave his beloved home in Vienna. Fond of Greek and Egyptian art and literature, he spent much time exploring the ancient myths of Moses, Israel and Egypt. The last 16 years of his life were severely painful for Freud on account of many jaw surgeries. To relieve the suffering, he used cocaine and was a confirmed addict by the time he died in exile in London (PL and VE DSC) in 1939.

### MOHANDAS GANDHI OCTOBER 2, 1869; 7:00 AM LMT PORBANDAR, INDIA

<!-- chunk-id: acg-src-lewis-guttman-1989.page-0039.chunk-003 -->
<!-- chunk-sha256: 8a3a2bebe34479103fa29f87d6ee652f473db4f364bcdf049f62bcc2b2073a33 -->
Gandhi was the father of modern political and cultural India, an activist, social reformer, writer and political philosopher. Motivated by tremendous personal ambition, he attended law school in England and upon returning to India was assigned to do casework in South Africa. Sensitized by his MO ASC line, the brutal repression he encountered changed his direction in life, and under his SA IC he was humiliated on account of his skin color. This experience dedicated him to spend the rest of his life working for the oppressed and against racism and prejudice. In India, he wrote and published numerous articles in keeping with his ME ASC line there. Moreover, his protest against British colonial rule became associated with the symbol of weaving—a spindle—and he carried one with him for years in open violation of a British Ss 233 49' —— a 33 es ban on production of textiles on the subcontinent, one of the more onerous of colonial restrictions designed to enrich England. As his revolution gathered momentum, prejudice and separatism, the very ideas he hated most, erupted between Muslims and Hindus. The nation he founded was from its inception rent with partisan violence until Pakistan broke off from India and established its own identity under his UR MH line. When the British finally acknowledged their defeat in India, Gandhi visited London where his JU-PL conjunction sits athwart the Midheaven along with VE and MA on the IC. The British, as yet unused to the idea that their time of empire had run out, were incredulous that this little, sunburnt weaver could be causing all this trouble. Loved asa messiah (the actual meaning of Mahatma) in India, he was nevertheless gunned down by an assassin in 1948 as the Mars, Saturn and Pluto conjunction in Leo squared his Scorpio/Taurus opposition.

## Page 41

### ALBERT SCHWEITZER JANUARY 14, 1875; 11:50 PM LMT KAYSERBURG, ALSACE

<!-- chunk-id: acg-src-lewis-guttman-1989.page-0041.chunk-001 -->
<!-- chunk-sha256: 4c50b2d65076969a673dc2b25c758ec5cc042e6e109269658ffacfad3a396a18 -->
A great humanitarian, theologian, writer, philosopher, doctor and musician, Albert Schweitzer sacrificed what would have undoubtedly been a comfortable life in order to devote himself to relieving the suffering of equatorial Africans at his famous clinic. A notable church and concert organist, his performances commanded sizeable audiences under his natal Sun and Mercury on the IC. After he founded his clinic, his reputation and concerts helped to support his medical efforts. He authored a respected biography of J. S. Bach and wrote several texts on the life of Jesus. At age 30, he abandoned his successful career as a 35 musician and religious educator to study medicine. Of all the places in the world to which he could have taken his medical mission, he chose Gabon, a country in central west Africa one degree south of the Equator, and set up facilities there to treat the indigenous poor and ailing. There on his map appear five singularly appropriate planets: SUIC (leadership); MEIC (concern with the common man); MO and NE DSC (self-sacrifice), and JU ASC (medicine). In addition to these lines actually crossing over his famous clinic in Gabon, the Equator is the latitude at which these planet lines cross at three other locations. His example later influenced Larimer Mellon, a socially prominent American, also to return to medical school and to set up a Schweitzer Hospital in Haiti (JU IC, NE MR). He was awarded the Nobel Peace Prize in Stockholm (SU and ME IC) in 19523

### CARL JUNG JULY 26, 1875; LOCAL SUNSET KESSWIL, SWITZERLAND

<!-- chunk-id: acg-src-lewis-guttman-1989.page-0043.chunk-002 -->
<!-- chunk-sha256: 6e10b1c9b465be2e7dae84f0357070cc7bdc802ea753d40ff80ea96f37664089 -->
Carl Jung was born in Kesswil, Switzerland under SU DSC and PL IC. Though his travels would briefly take him to several continents, he spent his entire life living and working very near his birthplace. Three pairs of planetary crossings—all powerfully feminine—affect Zurich: MO/ASC*#NE/ASC, VE/IC*MO/ASC, and VE/ IC*NE/ASC. His work certainly has a feminine flavor to it, especially in contrast to Freud's (q.v.) very masculine Sun-Uranus approach, and the preponderance of his disciples and devotees seem to be women. Jung studied medicine at Basel. He met Freud in Vienna (near Jung's UR DSC) in 1907 and became one of his leading protégés. As his own personality developed, he became increasingly critical of Freud's dogma placing sexual maladjustment at the core of all mental problems. With the publication of The Psychology of the Unconscious in 1911-12, he broke formally with his mentor. Jung went on to develop influential theories of psychological types, such as extravert/introvert; he studied psychic energy and proposed the existence of the "collective unconscious," archetypes, and individuation. His work drew from varied and rich cultural sources—dreams, myths, history, tribal cultures, alchemy, even astrology. It encompassed anthropology, religion, art and literature. Perhaps the most notable distinguishing characteristic of Jung's work was its view of the psyche as an evolving, 37 growing entity, linked somehow to a "collective unconscious." With the SU on the DSC, he drew much of his inspiration from his patients, and PL IC shows a singular example of positive Pluto: ongoing transformation of the "base material" of existence into the alchemical gold of consciousness. He received many honorary degrees from such institutions as Clark University, Harvard and Yale (MO DSC); Oxford (ME DSC); Calcutta and Benares (UR IC, MO ASC, SU IC), and, of course, places of learning near his home in Zurich (PL IC, SU DSC). His ME and VE MH lines are over San Francisco, where there are probably more Jungians than in all of Switzerland. In the 1920s he traveled extensively, as noted in his autobiography. A visit to Tunis and Algiers in 1920 hada profound effect upon him personally (PL IC). In 1924- 25, he visited the Pueblo Indians of New Mexico and Arizona (JU ASC, SU MH) and declared their sacred land to be the spiritual center of North America. The following year he visited Kenya to observe tribal practices and later went to Egypt where he commented on the masculine nature of the culture (MA MH). He was in India twice, the last visit being in 1937, attempting to discovera correlation between the unconscious psychic contents of modern man and more primitive manifestations. Here is where he has the SU IC and MO ASC lines crossing, (see also Gauguin map for the same planetary crossing over Tahiti), a place where he must have felt an integration of the masculine and feminine which was an ongoing symbolic quest in his life and work.

## Page 45

### PARAMAHANSA YOGANANDA JANUARY 5, 1893; 8:38 PM LMT GORAKHPUR, INDIA

<!-- chunk-id: acg-src-lewis-guttman-1989.page-0045.chunk-001 -->
<!-- chunk-sha256: 50e6ccc61c1829b7cacf1d580ae1a3e7608f4fba04807a543788a2fc34c13774 -->
Author of Autobiography of a Yogi, a book which became an inspiration for millions of Westerners seeking to learn Eastern mysticism, Yogananda was born in India during the time of a partile Neptune-Pluto conjunction. This conjunction dominated the Midheaven just to the east of his birthplace. In India (which included Pakistan in 1893), he had the MO ASC and URIC. The Moon inclined him to sensitivity to his environment and strongly attached him to his family, as it would later attach him to his spiritual teacher. UR on the IC fated him to transcend the limitations of his culture and to forge a self-created identity more congenial to California than to India. This blending of energies served him well in the West, where people were attracted to the Moon-Uranus aura he carried with him—the 39 gentleness of his spirit combining with the individuation of his identity. Los Angeles, California is the precise location of his SU ASC line, a place where his energy could be expressed, illuminating all who came to study with him. His Capricorn Sun shines upon Uranus in the Midheaven, offering a structure that promises revolutionary freedom from the established way of doing things. When he arrived in Los Angeles in the '30s in his Indian clothes, sandals and long hair, his nonconformism created quite a sensation. But he went on to embody the concept of self-realization to an entire generation. He who transcended his own culture, caste, and national identity under his UR IC urged others to do so under his UR MH. His Self-Realization (Sun) Fellowship (Uranus) is still going strong in southern California, almost 40 years after his death.

## Page 47

### FRITZ PERLS JULY 8, 1893; 5:30 AM MET BERLIN, GERMANY

<!-- chunk-id: acg-src-lewis-guttman-1989.page-0047.chunk-001 -->
<!-- chunk-sha256: fa33d5a9fd8a99e0f907998f2c5432e1dec257ba38cc48516601ae2f2bd76351 -->
Born undera SA IC line with MA and VE lines nearby, this pioneer of Gestalt therapy in the psychiatric world would eventually have his most profound effects on the avant-garde psychological movement in California. He seemed to be attracted to his Mars and Venus conjunction in terms of localities he chose as residences. Israel, where he made his home for a time, seeking refuge after World War IL, is the site of MA and VE ASC; the additional influence of the MO MH and UR IC assured an unsettled time in that perenially crisis-ridden area. Perls' Moon-Uranus opposition would force this Cancerian to examine his academic, cultural and philosophical roots closely in an attempt to transcend the negative instinctual patterns of the past. (This planetary combination came up in the Yogananda map previously discussed in this section.) Fritz Perls' desire to escape the traumatic memories of his birthplace (SA IC) resulted in years of wandering. 41 ——— es ZA ' =. In the mid-1960s, an incidental visit to the Esalen Institute on the California coastline south of Monterey put him in touch with his roots (JU IC, MA and VE DSC) and with the psychological reform movement that was being born there. Here he was able to practice and perfect his Gestalt therapy, pioneering the freeflowing kind of encounter groups that became so popular during the transformational psychology movement of the '60s and '70s. He had "come home" under his JU IC. There was an uninhibited sensuality at Esalen, and the grey-bearded, elderly European delighted in the freedom and intellectual inquiry. He was treated with great respect: a beautiful house was built for him overlooking the sea and he spent his last years there. His emphasis on touch, sensuality and group therapy were revolutionary at the time and symbolized well the VE and MA conjunction on the DSC. Crossings at Esalen's latitude include SU/DSC*UR/MH (like Yogananda),

## Page 49

### JEDDU KRISHNAMURTI MAY 12, 1895; 12:18 AM LMT MADANAPALLE, INDIA

<!-- chunk-id: acg-src-lewis-guttman-1989.page-0049.chunk-001 -->
<!-- chunk-sha256: 3ed3c4de5b5b3757c3ce6c9211509105f723f211f0a765caf8149009dad5c8f8 -->
Born in India with SU IC and UR MH, Krishnamurti became a self-realized master who was guided, like Paramahansa Yogananda (q.v.), to take his Indian wisdom to the U.S. to feed the spiritually starved masses occupying the sunny California coast. Ojai, California just outside of Santa Barbara is where he ultimately set up his world headquarters. Yogananda began in India with the MO and UR angular and came to California where the SU and UR dominated. Krishnamutrti's pattern was only somewhat different: with the SU and UR angular in India, he found his calling in southern California between SA IC and the SU MH and UR IC lines. Members of the Theosophical Society found him in India and groomed and educated him before taking him back to England to be their "guru," proclaiming 43 him Buddha incarnate. A young man of 16, he acceded to their wishes for a while. But a Sun-Uranus opposition is too independent for any established role. He shocked the world and the Theosophical Society when he declared in 1929 that he was not the incarnation of Maitreya and dissolved the order he had led. SU*MA and MA*UR crossings at London's latitude suggest that this may have provoked nota little antipathy. UR on the ASC is closest to London. From his institute in Ojai, California and in his voluminous writings, he promulgated a Saturnian autonomy and independence within the structure of his brilliant Sun-Uranus insight. His best-known quote typifies his refusal to be part of any institutionalization of wisdom or knowledge: "Truth is a pathless land. Man cannot come to it through any organization, through any creed, through any dogma, priest, ritual, not through any philosophical knowledge or psychological technique."

## Page 51

### ALAN WATTS JANUARY 6, 1915; 6:20 AM BST CHISLEHURST, ENGLAND

<!-- chunk-id: acg-src-lewis-guttman-1989.page-0051.chunk-001 -->
<!-- chunk-sha256: 9d64bfaaddfed5b0bb94e69f0e6e8080e56c9b40e06bbe7e0203afe1e85ca881 -->
In the 1960s and '70s, Alan Watts was the foremost proponent of Zen Buddhism in the West. A serious scholar, he published The Spirit of Zen: A Way of Life, Work and Art in the Far East in 1936, and afterwards was a frequent lecturer at universities and to radio and TV audiences, charming them with his dry, Capricornian humor and willingness to question the fundamental assumptions of Western culture. As a child, he was always interested in Chinese and Indian philosophy. From 1937-39, he was active in the World Congress of Faiths in London near his PL and SA lines and came to the U.S. in 1939, settling in Chicago where he attended Theological Seminary (SU and MEIC). He earned a Master of Sacred Theology degree there in 1948 but later left the Church, preferring 45 to avoid any religious affiliation. During the 60s, when thousands of American youth were looking for cultural and religious inspiration, he became very popular in San Francisco where he taught comparative religion at the Academy of Asian Studies. He was also a popular radio personality in both San Francisco and Los Angeles where he had a call-in show about Zen. Although known and associated for his Zen Buddhist awareness, he was just as interested in general Buddhism along with Taoism and Vedanta. Through India passes the opposite of what was in Chicago: SU MH, ME MH, and MA MH. Moreover, San Francisco lies between SA MH and the MO ASC, two polarities that bespeak the humanism and strict traditionalism of his Zen. The same two planets, much closer, dominate Japan with SA on the ASC and the MO on the IC, establishing a philosophical "home" for Watts and illustrating how important SA can be for a Capricorn.

## Page 53

### POPE JOHN PAUL II MAY 18, 1920; 12:30 PM EET WADOWICE, POLAND

<!-- chunk-id: acg-src-lewis-guttman-1989.page-0053.chunk-001 -->
<!-- chunk-sha256: 4505b156139d6311ae0181b5e21ad14fe88c25e978310b5b0bfc692a5d9c67b2 -->
Pope John Paul II is the first non-Italian pontiff in 455 years and the first Slavic pope ever. He began his seminary studies clandestinely while Poland (UR DSC, SA ASC) was under Nazi occupation. Completing studies in France and Italy (VE and ME MH), he returned home to do parish work. Uranus and Saturn were precisely opposed at his birth on his Ascendant-Descendant axis, symbolizing an irreconcilable dichotomy between individualism and authority, and he may be expected to identify with the latter in his role of infallible successor to St. Peter. Liberation Theology is suggested by his NE-JUIC in Central America along with his rising ME and VE so that his statements about the immorality of systems that create poverty will likely persist. 47 On his first trip abroad as Pope, he visited Latin America, home to almost one-half the Catholics of the world, where he was welcomed by millions under his SU, MO, VE, and ME ASC. Although practically everywhere he goes he is greeted with overwhelming support, in San Francisco (PL IC) he has managed to alienate liberals and gays with his conservative views on sexuality. He visited the Philippines during times of civil unrest, as evidenced by his SA MH and UR IC there. Like Karl Marx, John Paul was born within minutes of an eclipse in Taurus, so issues of money, poverty and capitalism will likely continue to demand his attention and offer him opportunities to leave a powerful legacy to his church. Powerful planets dominate Eastern Europe, which has been in upheaval since John Paul took up residence in the Vatican.

## Page 55

### RAM DASS APRIL 6, 1931; 10:40 AM EST BOSTON, MA

<!-- chunk-id: acg-src-lewis-guttman-1989.page-0055.chunk-001 -->
<!-- chunk-sha256: 6a12fc6c4b7f3a01280793938a704b9f3b8bd0b6e4c883ba602b04d6214c338c -->
In his first incarnation in this lifetime as Richard Alpert, Ph.D., Harvard University, Ram Dass was a co-partner in early LSD experiments with Timothy Leary. As devoted "servant of God," he now spends his life looking and talking much like a spiritual guru, but he quickly rejects this label. The lines on his map are appropriately suggestive of how and where he's chosen to act out his roles. If Neptune (it and Uranus seem consistently significant in spiritual pioneers' charts) symbolizes his special vision and his spiritual ideal, he would seek it out anywhere on earth. He doesn't need to seek out Uranus, since his Sun forms a rare occultation with the planet of iconoclasts. Over India sits Ram Dass' NE MH, and there he is almost an honorary citizen and has been deeply influenced by its ancient spiritual disciplines. The northeastern U.S. contains a mixed grouping, 49 including MA, PL and JU on the ASC and SA on the DSC, suggesting a projected, externalized role for the planet of tradition. He's often joked about the "family curse"—being born Jewish, you just accept the fact that you have a loose noose around your neck for life, and your family holds the rope—but his JU MH right through Israel shows a positive identification with his cultural origins. Attempting then to transcend his family roots and origins, he first tried college, then LSD, then Hindu mysticism, and now he has basically become a humanistic activist with a great sense of humor. He does Jewish jokes, drug jokes, and guru jokes and keeps lecture halls around the country filled with laughing audiences who want to glimpse his special brand of truth; all the proceeds usually go to one of his numerous selfless causes. The one he most consistently funds is a service organization called SEVA, based in Nepal under his NE MH, dedicated to financing eye surgery for the blind. SEVA also operates in Central America under his NE IC and VE MH.
