## Page 57

### BHAGWAN SHREE RAJNEESH. DECEMBER 11, 1931; 5:13 PM LMT GADAWARA, INDIA

<!-- chunk-id: acg-src-lewis-guttman-1989.page-0057.chunk-001 -->
<!-- chunk-sha256: be950c3cbb7654e00c85b192c06cc73c687883046b60633981add1cd5a1cf1c8 -->
The "Age of the Guru" may have officially reached its close with the undignified flight, of Bhagwan Shree Rajneesh from his commune in Oregon in 1985. The charismatic Sagittarian spiritual leader, who proved that freedom of religion is a relative matter in the USA, arrived from Poona, India where he had grown in influence and power under his JU IC line. Purchasing desert land in eastern Oregon, he and thousands of his followers built a city, irrigated, and quickly began to outnumber the suspicious and traditional local folks. The media, redolent with parallels to Jonestown, arrived, and the affair concluded with Rajneesh fleeing, immigration authorities in hot pursuit, while several of his followers were charged with mass poisonings, attempted murder, and various degrees of fraud and conspiracy. At first glance, Rajneeshpuram/Antelope, Oregon seems to be a geographical void—territory without lines, suggesting options open to develop what is needed— until one examines the planetary crossings for the latitude of relocation and the Cyclo*Carto*Graphy (progressed) events of the time. The latitude of Antelope contains a SU*UR crossing, and this is the third guru to come from India to the United States with these planets related (see also Jeddu Krishnamurti and Paramahansa 51 Yogananda maps elsewhere in this text). In this case as in other Sun/planet combinations, the Sun acts as a powerful spotlight to project outward the nature of Uranus. The small-town folks of Antelope weren't noticeably attuned to Uranus' revolutionary, antiestablishment energies. He and his strangely dressed, largely urban and middle-class followers stood out uncomfortably, and a total lack of diplomacy on the part of the "Rajneeshees" made a difficult situation impossible. But this crossing seems inadequate to explain the war that nearly broke out between his followers and local residents, even when it is realized that he arrived in Antelope at the time when he was undergoing the once-in-a-lifetime transit of Uranus to his natal Sun. He somehow picked the SU*UR latitude to more fully experience the transit. The most powerful indication of the problems he encountered comes from progressed factors. In 1985, just as Rajneeshees, Oregonians and the federal government were all about to start shooting, Bhagwan's progressed SU and MA, along with transiting JU, conjoined over Rajneeshpuram. Due to the relative placements of his Sun and Mars at birth, they were destined to conjoin when he reached 54 years of age. But how did he know to travel to Oregon, the one place in the world at which they would be on the IC? At this writing (1988) he is back in Poona, under the watchful eye of the Indian government.

## Page 59

### WERNER ERHARD SEPTEMBER 5, 1935; 11:25 PM EST PHILADELPHIA, PA

<!-- chunk-id: acg-src-lewis-guttman-1989.page-0059.chunk-001 -->
<!-- chunk-sha256: b6eac39f12fea54452665d80c9a40bf09b6fde67179cfb1e7bef649473cd8929 -->
A self-described discipline-freak, Werner Erhard was born on a SA MH line in Philadelphia, Pennsylvania. Although he began his career in sales, he now enjoys a worldwide following due to his unique enlightenment training seminars, so he is placed in this section. While SA MH may have gifted him with excellent entrepreneurial skills, life there with a wife and four children proved a little too routine, so he began his migration west. His first stop was St. Louis (MO DSC) where he worked in automobile sales. That too must have proven uncongenial since he continued west and stopped for quite a while in the Pacific Northwest (UR ASC), accepting a job as sales manager for an encyclopedia company. It was here that he was first successful at combining the business wizardry of his natal SA MH with the unconventionality of the local UR ASC into the unique, eclectic blend of spiritual, philosophical, psychological and entrepreneurial skills that eventually became the multimillion dollar "est" package. He launched "est" in San Francisco (UR ASC on the latitude of the MO/ASC*ME/MH) in 1971. Since his UR DSC passes through India, he may have been inspired by other traveling 20th-century gurus. Several 53 spiritual leaders born in various places throughout the world have arrived in California and amassed large group followings. UR is the angular planet for many of them (see the maps of Yogananda and Krishnamurti elsewhere in this section), so this may be a statement not only about them and their belief systems but about California as well. California, a part of the U.S. for hardly a century, has seemed to consistently encourage Uranian expressions by offering a free-flowing, anything goes social structure. It has attracted many who could not fit into a less freedom-loving milieu. Erhard's map reflects the countless maps of people from all over the U.S. who have flocked to California to somehow break with the rigidity of their midwestern work ethics, their southern dogma or their northeastern intellectualism. The United States is a Uranian nation, born when this planet was about to be discovered. California is the epitome of America's individualism, so Erhard's "est" resonated with thousands who may have had second thoughts about leaving their Saturns behind. Erhard has been particularly successful selling Saturnian discipline in the packaging of this Uranian brand of freedom. His headquarters are now located in the Virgin Islands under his SU, VEand NEIC, and these planets indicate a firmer grasp on establishment values.

## Page 62

### RUDOLF VALENTINO MAY 6, 1895; 3:00 AM MET CASTELLANETA, ITALY

<!-- chunk-id: acg-src-lewis-guttman-1989.page-0062.chunk-001 -->
<!-- chunk-sha256: 3bd4bb97ccca58d299c78c9d587c1496ef7a0cf83686c66c18170134a360558c -->
Born in Italy between the MO DSC and VE IC lines, Rudolph Valentino was destined to inspire adulating hysteria at a level unprecedented in the '20s and '30s. His map locates the same set of planets that are angular in Hollywood—SU, ME, SA, UR—in the Middle East, only reversed. In Hollywood, the SU and ME on the DSC oppose SA and UR on the ASC, while over the Middle East the SU and ME on the ASC oppose SA and UR on the DSC. His romanticized portrayal of the Sheik of Araby combined the autocratic with the exotic and had women fainting over him in the streets. O77. As is often the case, becoming an archetype proved to be a curse as well as a blessing. The blessing was it got him jobs. The curse was he couldn't live up to his screen image and reportedly had very unfulfilling relationships with women. He became another casualty of the Hollywood fame and money machine. Although highly paid, he spent most of his off-screen time drunk, heavily in debt and very isolated. His stage persona was loved and adored (SU, ME). His personal life was lonely and desperate (SA, UR). His PL*VE crossing at Los Angeles' latitude confirms that his attractiveness would isolate him and warns of financial excesses.

## Page 64

### JOAN CRAWFORD MARCH 23, 1903; 10:00 PM CST SAN ANTONIO, TX

<!-- chunk-id: acg-src-lewis-guttman-1989.page-0064.chunk-001 -->
<!-- chunk-sha256: c00fd38d0bdcb3379e8e64a3100508625f7535ce09df7f277c0059305e393ea9 -->
Joan Crawford was the Hollywood film star of the '40s and '50s whose posthumous claim to fame lies in her daughter's unflattering biography, Mommy Dearest, which revealed her as singularly deficient in maternal kindheartedness. Both the MO and SA appear at the IC over California, which is an appropriate planetary signature for a wounded, neurotic soul who would take out her afflictions on her offspring as they probably had been taken out on her. 59 One of her highest achievements in Hollywood came when she was awarded an Oscar for her portrayal of Mildred Pierce, a single mother who worked hard, fought severe opposition from male domination and bought her own business, making it a highly profitable enterprise at the expense of her personal life—another, more positive side of the Moon-Saturn. The VE DSC near Hollywood keyed her in to the aesthetic archetype of her time. She was not only beautiful but set fashion trends (somewhat masculine in keeping with Venus in Aries) at the height of her career.

## Page 66

### CLAUDETTE COLBERT SEPTEMBER 13, 1905; 7:45 AM LMT PARIS, FRANCE

<!-- chunk-id: acg-src-lewis-guttman-1989.page-0066.chunk-001 -->
<!-- chunk-sha256: 38c21046ae1e52e31367c373d3b1bf68de1c539ecc822187c4842c2948f98b34 -->
Born in Paris with NE at the MH, Claudette Colbert became a Hollywood star in an era when European stars brought exotic and seductive qualities to the American screen. She was a major box office luminary throughout the '30s and '40s. Over Hollywood, five planet lines include NE ASC, UR DSC, MO MH, SU IC, and PL ASC, suggesting strong sexual polarity between the Sun and the Moon, fortified with an aura of intrigue and individuality. Like Elizabeth Taylor (q.v.), she achieved stardom far from her European birthplace, 61 having powerful planets on all four angles in the movie capital. But with all the success and notoriety that Hollywood gave her, she always preferred the Broadway stage. She proclaimed that New York nightlife was the best under her VE ASC, and as a child there in the 1920s, she studied art and fashion design, appropriate Venusian choices. In her film career, one of her most successful and notable roles was Cleopatra in 1934 (another parallel to Elizabeth Taylor). Venus in the Midheaven wafted her down the Nile with suitable luxury and feminine indulgence.

<a id="acg-src-lewis-guttman-1989.page-0067"></a>
<!-- page-id: acg-src-lewis-guttman-1989.page-0067 -->

## Page 68

### DESI ARNAZ MARCH 2, 1917; 12:30 PM HAVANA TIME (+5.5) SANTIAGO DE CUBA

<!-- chunk-id: acg-src-lewis-guttman-1989.page-0068.chunk-001 -->
<!-- chunk-sha256: 31a9938fe5c080bab89413ba0357cbd8245d1041536bfbfc03532dff0a2d5f60 -->
Born in Cuba with a Piscean Sun-Mars conjunction near the Midheaven, Desi Arnaz perfectly expressed this combination by portraying a hot-tempered, fiery Latin bandleader on the I Love Lucy series. His close rising Pluto-Moon conjunction hints at alienation from his culture and family, subtly evoking the rootlessness and detachment that began to pervade America in the '50s. His role's relevance is evidenced by the fact that the series is still running in syndication some 30 years later. In Los Angeles, his angular Neptune (IC) manifested in two contrasting expressions. Being a Pisces, Nep- 63 tune's angularity brought him in touch with his acting, music and stage performance potentials and struck a responsive chord in all who saw him. But the less desirable side of Neptune, especially for a Latin male with a Sun-Mars conjunction, is that there had to bea certain amount of self-deprecation and suppression of ambition. He "plays" the passionate Sun-Mars person that he is, but in reality, Lucille Ball and Los Angeles must have slowly watered down all his fire until he felt confined by the system in which he was working. Recent biographers have alluded to ongoing problems with alcohol. The Desilu Partnership dissolved in 1962 when Lucy bought out Desi for $2.5 million. With her dominating Leo presence, she reigned over the growth of the corporation until she later sold it for $17 million.

## Page 70

### YUL BRYNNER JULY 11, 1920; 6:15 AM LMT VLADIVOSTOK, U.S.S.R.

<!-- chunk-id: acg-src-lewis-guttman-1989.page-0070.chunk-001 -->
<!-- chunk-sha256: f926a348198014a47c5f9f6015912c008732beceae16f1b0a6754e6a286c93cb -->
Yul Brynner grew up in China where his Manchurian father owned silver and lead mines. His privileged childhood and youth included attending boarding school in Paris, studying philosophy at the Sorbonne, and working the Paris nightclub circuit, all under his JU DSC, an influence symbolically associated with opulence at others' expense. He supported himself performing in circuses throughout France, Italy and Switzerland until an accident ended his career under his UR ASC and SA DSC. 65 He then turned to theater with the idea of directing. After the war, he came to the U.S. where success on Broadway ultimately led to Hollywood. His memorable film roles include the king of Siam (between SA IC and PL ASC) in The King And I and his portrayal of Pharaoh Ramses III in The Ten Commandments where PLand SUIC influences dominate in Egypt. In Hollywood, his NE MH allows him to seem the embodiment of collective needs, while his ME MH hints at an "ordinary person" beneath the glamour. The MO DSC adds sensitive appeal, though MA ASC establishes a macho persona. The combination seems vulnerable but sexy, other-worldly but reassuringly ordinary.

### MERV GRIFFIN JULY 6, 1925; 4:45 AM PST SAN MATEO, CA

<!-- chunk-id: acg-src-lewis-guttman-1989.page-0072.chunk-002 -->
<!-- chunk-sha256: 1ec9c158ccb7e8ebc7aaba87abb8841f82118f9181e99e7e8a335e30e1876c64 -->
Merv Griffin's birth near San Francisco under the MO DSC, UR MH and PL ASC impelled him to find a geographical alternative to the emotional upheavals suggested there. On the East Coast, his SA IC and NE ASC took him into show business. Since SA and NE hint at great sensitivity and perhaps low self-esteem, their presence in New York suggests his identity as a successful entrepreneur as well as the idea that he may be "hiding" behind his show-business personality. He recently sold his highly successful, long-running TV show to Coca-Cola for undisclosed millions. Merv's map is somewhat striking in that all 10 planets pass through the U.S., and there is a powerful clustering of lines on the West Coast, the base of his operations 67 for many years. There his Cancer Sun conjoins PL ASC, and both oppose the MO and JU DSC. The Cancer planets are personable, warm and intimate; Capricorn demands a shrewd, entrepreneurial and presentable image. All of these conflict with UR MH, a planet which rules the electronic media. The West Coast business conglomerate which he controlled must have seemed to be a mixed blessing since with UR MH and PL ASC there were most likely constant power plays and high-finance skullduggery. Yet before the camera, he maintained the appearance of the warm, familiar Cancer Sun personality. He pioneered giving TV exposure to astrologers, psychics, trance channelers, Eastern mystics and meditation techniques long before Shirley MacLaine or the "New Age" became fashionable, a manifestation of his powerful Neptune.

## Page 74

### RICHARD BURTON NOVEMBER 10, 1925; 2:30 PM BST PONTRHYDYFEN, WALES

<!-- chunk-id: acg-src-lewis-guttman-1989.page-0074.chunk-001 -->
<!-- chunk-sha256: 531a2e2e6b823f3dfe7d156e9c008187a1e7af2e378b08f3d3b3a6bdf797f69f -->
Richard Burton was born with UR on the ASC, VE on the MH, and NE and the MO on the DSC, suggesting extraordinary charisma, communicating power and individualism. Powerfully placed Uranus and Neptune inclined him to portray archetypal characters, beginning at the London Theater where he played the demanding Shakespeare repertory. Natally, his Sun- Saturn conjunction set impossibly high standards, while the Moon's wide conjunction to Neptune plagued him with sensationalism and alcohol. Burton's on-again, off-again marriage to Elizabeth Taylor (born in London where he has the MO DSC with VE MH nearby) kept his name in the tabloids for years but also resulted in opportunities for high-paying 69 movie parts. Perhaps his most famous role, though a failure at the box office, was in the lavish production of Cleopatra. It was in Egypt, not far from his SU DSC, SA DSC, and MA DSC, that he first met Elizabeth, these planets perhaps foreshadowing the nature of their relationship. Their whirlwind affair reportedly delayed the filming for months and drove production costs even higher than they already were. In Hollywood, his Sun-Saturn conjunction in Scorpio rose exactly, and there he continued to play mature, serious roles. But NE MH furnished the tabloids with scandalous tales of marital battles and alcoholic excess. The filming of Night of the Iguana, under Burton's MO MH and ME ASC in Mexico, provided such tawdry gossip that his reputation as a serious actor never completely recovered. Burton died at the age of 59 during his second Saturn return.

## Page 76

### MARILYN MONROE JUNE 1, 1926; 9:30 AM PST LOS ANGELES, CA

<!-- chunk-id: acg-src-lewis-guttman-1989.page-0076.chunk-001 -->
<!-- chunk-sha256: b354f8370e4d4647431e1f3061061138b0794b90ff4855d4758237af7bfcd714 -->
Probably no one needs to be reminded who Marilyn Monroe was. Her tragic suicide in 1962 ended her individual life, but ensured immortality for her image. The two planets that symbolize the feminine archetype, the Moon and Venus, are most angular over Los Angeles, Marilyn's birthplace and career center. Neptune, planet of archetypes and illusions, is also close by. It is fitting, then, that she should come to represent all that is "Woman" through a mass projection that made her into America's cinematic love goddess. She 71 wanted desperately to be considered a serious actress and studied with drama coach Lee Strasburg in New York (MA DSC). But as the perfected image of Venus, she was not "allowed" to own her Mars, which passes near Boston and Washington, D.C. She has been linked to the personal life of former President John F. Kennedy, whose VE line passes through her birthplace. Two of her husbands were sports figure Joe DiMaggio and playwright Arthur Miller, born under her VE and MA lines, respectively. A UR*PL crossing at Los Angeles' latitude hints at a rebellion from the role others demanded she accept.

## Page 78

### GRACE KELLY, PRINCESS GRACE OF MONACO NOVEMBER 12, 1929; 5:31 AM EST PHILADELPHIA, PA

<!-- chunk-id: acg-src-lewis-guttman-1989.page-0078.chunk-001 -->
<!-- chunk-sha256: d5c07a21bbddff313167a6b0f63921e98fe417eb26395331ed696be0d5c627f7 -->
Born in Philadelphia on a VE ASC line, Grace Kelly was drawn to acting in New York where she studied drama under her ME ASC line. While ME lines are good for writing, they also identify good places for education since the powers of the mind are in sharp focus. During the filming of Hitchcock's To Catch A Thief on the French Riviera, she met Prince Rainier of Monaco. They married shortly thereafter and so actualized as closely as possible the Cinderella/Prince Charming fairy tale that culturally dominated the pre-feminist 1950s. She gave up a very successful, if short-lived, Hollywood career for a chance to be a princess, live in acastle and raise three children. As Cinderella, her life was entirely taken up by her family and her involve- 73 ment in Monaco's social and political functions, a life which was cut short by an automobile crash in that same location in 1982. The ME MH line passes nearest to Monaco, and its inappropriateness calls this birth time into question, but a case can be made for her ongoing concern with the well-being of her subjects, and it's likely that in the snobbish, glittering milieu of Europe's playground Grace Kelly seemed relatively commonplace. An indication unaffected by birth time is the exact crossing of SU*NE on the latitude of Monaco. A SU*NE crossing unites visibility and myth in an aspect unusually appropriate for a princess living out a fairy tale romance. Under the Sun-Neptune combination, the illusion is often grander than the reality, however, so this princess' untimely exit reflected an inner discontent with the role her life had presented her.

## Page 80

### JAMES DEAN FEBRUARY 8, 1931; 9:09 PM CST MARION, IN

<!-- chunk-id: acg-src-lewis-guttman-1989.page-0080.chunk-001 -->
<!-- chunk-sha256: 76df10a850cf7eaadbd17e7c77dba7c6ed7c3fc29f8cb628559ee9e8c282d3dd -->
As actor and cult hero for American youth in the '50s, James Dean's meteoric rise to fame was cut short by his passion for life and intensity. Born in Indiana, his family moved to southern California when he was four years old. At the death of his mother, he was sent back to relatives who lived ona farm in Indiana (VEIC, JU MH). Though he felt loved by his aunt and uncle, he experienced deep pain and rejection at being orphaned so early in life. He excelled in sports, exhibited a reckless rebelliousness, and enjoyed motorcycles and terrifying teen pranks. He joined his high school drama guild and participated in poetry readings. After high school, he spent a short period of time with 79 his father in California. In Hollywood (NE ASC), he was encouraged by his mentor, James Whitmore, to study at the Actors Studio in New York (PL MH, SA IC). His first role in East of Eden firmly established him as a serious actor and attracted a youthful following. With Neptune angular over Hollywood, he continued his hero/victim roles in future films and in his private life. Earlier, he had been introduced to Yoga as a means of physical as well as mental discipline, and he continued this practice throughout his acting career. Auto racing was also a passion. While driving his Porsche to an auto meetin Salinas, California, he collided with another car and was killed instantly. His death, at the apex of youthful potentiality, earned him archetypal status as a puer aeternus symbol—never to experience the moderation imposed by maturity or aging.

## Page 82

### ELIZABETH TAYLOR FEBRUARY 27, 1932; 7:56 PM BST LONDON, ENGLAND

<!-- chunk-id: acg-src-lewis-guttman-1989.page-0082.chunk-001 -->
<!-- chunk-sha256: 7c5646e94ff9cdf62ad0bda0d7bad2b8a2d7e00c61edd578b15d9587a1327f00 -->
Elizabeth Taylor began her legendary film career as a teenager in National Velvet and blossomed into a classically beautiful film goddess who became the archetype of glamour for an entire generation of Americans. While no lines occur over her London birthplace, four planets angular over Hollywood describe her image perfectly: SU MH gives regality and charisma; MEMH hints at an unpretentious "ordinary person" behind the glitter; MA and NE, the closest planets, impart sexual scandal along with the innate capacity to receive and reflect back an entire society's projections. In the days when it was customary to marry every time an encounter became sexual, her marriages and divorces kept her constantly in the tabloids. She served her public well in a time when divorce was not available to "decent" people. She met Richard Burton while filming Cleopatra, float- 77 ing down the Nile. Her SA IC goes right through Egypt. Their resulting love affair reportedly delayed shooting of the film tremendously and drove the production costs up into the millions. Still, the film did poorly at the box office. Near Egypt, specifically the Mediterranean areas of Italy, Greece and Libya, she has a VE-UR conjunction on the DSC crossed by PL MH, and these appropriately describe her affair with Burton. Her Pluto zenith occupies the latitude of Puerto Vallarta where her increasingly sordid affair with thenalcoholic Burton began to go beyond what the public could find amusing. On Hollywood's longitude, she has an exact MA MH- NE IC opposition, two planets which are often the signature of sensational, sexy film stars, and those who may have problems with addictions. Recently, the MA-NE influence has manifested more positively in her courageous fund-raising work for AIDS research, demonstrating how maturity can temper even the most problematical aspects.

### YOKO ONO FEBRUARY 18, 1933; 8:30 PM JST TOKYO, JAPAN

<!-- chunk-id: acg-src-lewis-guttman-1989.page-0084.chunk-001 -->
<!-- chunk-sha256: b5fd0cb3133f971e1b1c8dc742a2f2bfb5acf96bf3f50eb682a85049ab4c81fa -->
Tokyo, the birthplace of Yoko Ono, is affected by a MOVE latitude crossing, appropriate for an artist. It also identifies the feminine archetype that inspired John Lennon to immortalize her in "Woman," one of his last great pieces. Yoko's marriage to John Lennon, not her artwork, brought her worldwide fame. Their relationship caused quite a scandal, and John's fans never liked her very well. It was thought that their relationship caused the dissolution of the Beatles, and in this she manifested her close Venus-Saturn conjunction. She and John made headlines in several places in 72 addition to Liverpool, where she has an exact VE MH line. Their "open honeymoon" in Amsterdam (SU MH) and their "bed-in for peace" in Toronto (SA and VE ASC) were typical of how they worked together and dealt with the public and press, interweaving Venusian concepts of love, sexuality and peace. The West Coast of the U.S. never seemed to appeal to her, and URIC identifies it as a center of Lennon's fans' antipathy. They chose New York City when it was time for them to settle down, concentrate on their family and live amore normal life. There, Yoko has the MO MH along with the SU and VE ASC. Lennon's VE near Tokyo (her birthplace) and her VE near Liverpool (his birthplace) illustrate a reciprocal Venus alignment often found in the maps of deeply committed lovers.

## Page 86

### RICHARD CHAMBERLAIN MARCH 31, 1934; 6:20 PM PST BEVERLY HILLS, CA

<!-- chunk-id: acg-src-lewis-guttman-1989.page-0086.chunk-001 -->
<!-- chunk-sha256: fd614a36382b5450d9efc331e4e2a825ce53189ece607450dd7e25f3bc8c57a5 -->
Born in Los Angeles with the SU and MA on the DSC, Richard Chamberlain was a natural for film work in which he portrayed extremely masculine roles. After starring as a surgeon for several years in the hit TV series Dr. Kildare, he left Hollywood to pursue his career in the London theater. He became the first American actor to play Hamlet successfully in London since John Barrymore. He delighted the critics and went on to play other Shakespearian leads on stages in New York, Washington, Los Angeles and Seattle. When he went back to television, he played an English 81 sea captain shipwrecked in Japan (PL ASC, SU MH) who, under the tutelage of the supreme warlord Toronaga, gradually develops into a true medieval Samurai. This miniseries, based on James Clavell's best seller, Shogun, has been one of the most-watched TV serials ever aired. Subsequent roles included a priest torn by his erotic desires in The Thornbirds, set in Australia (GSU MH, MA MH, JUIC, MOIC), for which he also received much acclaim. Chamblerlain also starred in King Solomon's Mines, which were probably located near his ME ASC and NE DSC. His exact VE-SA conjunction goes through New York, suggesting more mature, sophisticated roles there which diverge from the macho image of the Aries Sun-Mars conjunction.

## Page 88

### SHIRLEY MACLAINE APRIL 24, 1934; 3:57 PM EST RICHMOND, VA

<!-- chunk-id: acg-src-lewis-guttman-1989.page-0088.chunk-001 -->
<!-- chunk-sha256: a2a08454686c6afe7572d07021df3a7d7d6eeff1ceb7b202e8bb52354b749e77 -->
The most striking characteristics of Shirley MacLaine's natal horoscope relating to her recent activities and role in society are the Moon-Neptune conjunction in Virgo and the 12th House and the Sun-Mars conjunction in Taurus and the 8th House. The Moon and Neptune show a fascination with the mystical and with inner realities, while the Taurean Sun-Mars combination gives business acumen and practicality. Her inner questioning and self-reliance, grounded by strong values, motivate her to proselytize psychic phenomena and metaphysical principles to large masses of people. Her Ascendant, the astrological indicator of identity and life purpose, is at 29 Virgo, activating the United States birth Neptune near the same degree, linking the country's ideals and spiritual consciousness to her own. 83 According to her autobiography, she initiated her metaphysical researches in Hong Kong where, under her ME ASC and JU DSC, she picked up a book that was to ultimately change her life. In a recent interview in Body, Mind and Spirit magazine, she acknowledged using her Astro*Carto*Graphy map to locate her new center, which is planned in southern Colorado where her MO-NE conjunction occupies the ASC. Though this combination could imply practical problems in business, the location should maximize her intuitive capacities and put her even more deeply in touch with her vision, idealism and message for the larger society. SA DSC near Hollywood advises that she is strictly business on stage, though SA's placement in Aquarius directs this professionalism toward goals larger than her personal interest. She probably responds best to no-nonsense, pragmatic directors and producers.

## Page 90

### ELVIS PRESLEY JANUARY 8, 1935; 4:35 AM CST TUPELO, MS

<!-- chunk-id: acg-src-lewis-guttman-1989.page-0090.chunk-001 -->
<!-- chunk-sha256: 6a1c37a04b3605f3984bbfca91c2b4f04e2d773d2ad13e228cb5c94759411a7e -->
The first "King of Rock and Roll" grew up near Memphis, Tennessee—an absolute blank area of his map. At the latitude of his birth, a SU*UR line crossing hints at the rebellious and transforming effect he would have on his generation through his self-expressive music. MA and NE MH are closest to Memphis and define a Hollywood "bad boy" image: scandalous sex and addictive patterns. They also hint at the redefinition of male sexuality his music and liberated pelvis foreshadowed. His battle with drugs, alcohol, overeating and the other hazards of superstardom are suggested by this planetary combination, which he shares with Elizabeth Taylor and James Dean (see maps elsewhere in this section). 85 His JU ASC and SA IC lines cross exactly over southern California and Las Vegas, thus explaining his powerful marketability in both Hollywood's film industry and the nightclub circuit. While the JU ASC continually promoted and inflated him, the SA IC line represented a place where, out of his element, he grew more lonely, paranoid and obsessive, withdrawing through the use of drugs, alcohol or whatever else could ease his personal pain and suffering. Apparently, the middleclass, secure respectability of the JU and SA locations were fatal to his intensely masculine Sun squared Mars personality; he couldn't reject the complacency that killed him because of the low self-esteem indicated by his SA and MO IC near Las Vegas (see also Janis Joplin's map in this section). Elvis' map unarguably illustrates that Jupiter is not always good for everyone, especially Saturn-ruled Capricorns.

### JULIE ANDREWS OCTOBER 1, 1935; 6:00 AM BDT WALTON-ON-THAMES, ENGLAND

<!-- chunk-id: acg-src-lewis-guttman-1989.page-0092.chunk-002 -->
<!-- chunk-sha256: 8004405f1a36022b96913fb6875e581afb9ca4ed2765619251ec6228a19c638d -->
Although she's had numerous Hollywood film roles, Julie Andrews is probably best remembered for her portrayal of Maria in The Sound of Music, which was filmed in Austria under her SU ASC line. Originally from England, she came to Los Angeles to do film work as a young woman. At that time her marriage broke up, but there, under her VEIC and MA DSC, she met Blake Edwards with whom she has had a personal and professional relationship, starring in many of his films. Since the Descendant placements often identify where other people or partners play out the particular planet's role, in Los Angeles it is through her relationship that she is psychologically assimilating and absorbing the masculine potentials symbolized by Mars. In many of 87 Hollywood film actors' charts, DSC lines are prominent where spouses or agents serve as vehicles through which the star becomes known. New York has a crossing of the SU IC and PL ASC, suggesting it is a powerful but lonely place for her. Success and opportunities for public self-expression— exactly what performers are looking for—often manifest under Sun lines, but in this case, the Sun's energy is focused through Pluto, identifying New York as a transformative locale—introspective, severe, a place where personal power struggles may overshadow the successes. She has adopted two Vietnamese orphan children who were born under her SU MH line in keeping with the Sun's symbolism of self-expression, creativity and the parental archetype.

## Page 94

### JANE FONDA DECEMBER 21, 1937; 9:14 AM EST NEW YORK, NY

<!-- chunk-id: acg-src-lewis-guttman-1989.page-0094.chunk-001 -->
<!-- chunk-sha256: c89212c38b2282e62fb10021c2bfa062ee729a6e0f8a6097dde7c78a0abba212 -->
While placed under the heading of "Entertainers," jane Fonda has as many facets to her personality as she's had movie parts. She's been a successful Hollywood sex kitten, political activist, serious actress, aerobics instructor, entrepreneur, and wife and mother. This Fire sign woman (Sagittarius Sun, Leo Moon) never stops. Her VE ASC and SA IC are closest to Hollywood, appropriately reflecting the connection to her famous father, Henry Fonda, and her physical beauty. Early in her career, she went to Paris under the influence of her JU MH and PLIC, and there met and married Roger Vadim, a French producer who created many of her films. These early film roles tended to exploit her femaleness, displaying the same welltoned body she now can successfully market in exercise videos at age 51. While her films did fairly well, her campaign to publicly protest the U.S. role in Viet Nam really made headlines. She was under illegal governmental surveillance for several years. The Nixon Administration, operating from her JU ASC and PL DSC, considered her a real threat to their idea of democracy and the American people. She was arrested at a Cleveland airport (UR IC and ME ASC, see also Timothy Leary's map previously in this text) for possession of illegal drugs, which, 89 in fact, were vitamin pills and prescription tranquilizers. The obvious unfairness of this persecution lent credence to her cause. But many of her fans think she went too far when she traveled to Hanoi and allowed herself to be used by the North Vietnamese government. Her MO ASC there shows a genuine, passionate commitment, as well as a childlike naiveté. (See also the Berrigans, North and Secord for other politically passionate MO lines.) After Viet Nam ceased to be an issue, she withdrew from the public arena, emerging a few years later as a serious actress whose production company sought to make marketable films with messages. For the most part, they have succeeded, though her most popular effort also involved Viet Nam (MO ASC) —her role in Coming Home won her an Oscar. Her subsequent films, especially The China Syndrome about a nuclear power plant meltdown, were regarded by many in Hollywood as too political. But the nation and Hollywood were stunned when the Three Mile Island catastrophe (on her UR*ME crossing latitude) occurred the same week the film was released. Making films and marketing exercise videotapes and clothing are what occupy most of her time back on her VE ASC and SA IC. If she divorces her California politician husband, Tom Hayden, she is likely to resume a more direct political activisim.

### JOHN LENNON

<a id="acg-src-lewis-guttman-1989.page-0096"></a>
<!-- page-id: acg-src-lewis-guttman-1989.page-0096 -->
## Page 96

### JOHN LENNON OCTOBER 9, 1940; 6:30 PM BST LIVERPOOL, ENGLAND

<!-- chunk-id: acg-src-lewis-guttman-1989.page-0096.chunk-001 -->
<!-- chunk-sha256: dc00fe582c44988888fc3cdf62ec0b1e89561b486e1349445b6f6c33af42ea77 -->
John Lennon truly became a legend in his own time. Born under the rare Saturn-Jupiter-Uranus conjunction, he had powerful effects in three places where it was angular at his birth. Early rock and roll fans in Liverpool (SA ASC, JU ASC, UR ASC) and then Hamburg (PLIC, MO MH) brought the Beatles to the attention of European audiences. A 1964 tour to the U.S. and the release of "I Want to Hold Your Hand" created a worldwide furor. The Beatles' appearance on the Ed Sullivan Show touched off "Beatlemania." The SA-JU- UR conjunction which occupied his ASC in England came to the IC in New York with the additional influence of the MO ASC assuring popularity with the masses. John Lennon was labeled the "intellectual" Beatle. His opinions, which mirrored the social and political inquiry of the times, became lyrics to one gold album after another. Lennon soon grew weary of being a "star" 91 and having people faint, no matter how absurdly the Beatles performed. In 1967, his assertion that the group was more popular than Jesus Christ led to mass burnings of Beatle albums by fundamentalists in the South and Midwest, but it only fed his popularity among the youthful counterculture. With five planets in Venusruled signs, he opposed Britain's involvement in the Nigerian Civil War (PLIC, MO MH, SA ASC, JU ASC, UR ASC) and its support of the U.S. involvement in Viet Nam (SA MH zenith, JU MH zenith). He became a symbol of the revolutionary peace movement. He met and married Yoko Ono (VE ASC and NE ASC are on either side of Tokyo), whom he acknowledged as his spiritual teacher, and evolved from his pop star persona to a more soul-searching, introspective artist. They set up housekeeping in New York City, raising their son, Sean. During the late '70s, Lennon enjoyed gardening, housekeeping, cooking and parenting. He was just beginning to go back into album-making and musical peformance when an assassin's bullet ended his life outside his home in 1980 at age 40.

## Page 98

### ANN-MARGARET APRIL 28, 1941; 4:30 AM MET VALSJOBYN, SWEDEN

<!-- chunk-id: acg-src-lewis-guttman-1989.page-0098.chunk-001 -->
<!-- chunk-sha256: 8063155c1650359d9fbf72696255501eadf6bb55c543743f4c3eb880e1827ae1 -->
Ann-Margaret was born in Sweden very close to her planetary "vortex," one of two locations on the globe where all the planet lines converge. Through Sweden she has her Taurus Sun, Venus, Saturn, Jupiter, Moon and Uranus and Arian Mercury on the Ascendant, certainly a sign of sturdy stock. While still a young child, her family relocated to a town near Chicago (NE MH). From that time, she yearned to be in entertainment. She made her debut with the Ted Mack Amateur Hour at age 16. Many show business jobs in Chicago, followed by others in Kansas City, Las Vegas and Los Angeles, started her off on her successful career. A 22-foot fall from a stage platform while performing in Lake Tahoe (SA DSC) threatened not only her career, but her life. Her six-planet stellium in Taurus conferred on her the determination not to let this stop her—she was totally recovered and back 93 on-stage performing again within three months. With the MO DSC in Hollywood, her early film roles successfully marketed her as the image of a sex goddess or pin-up girl. Three planets on the DSC in Hollywood added emphasis of working through or with a partner with a strong persona, as many other stars with planets heavily occupying the DSC in Hollywood have demonstrated. Her marriage to Roger Smith in 1967 created just such a relationship. He took over management of her career, remolded her image, and was insistent upon carefully scrutinizing every potential role. With JU DSC and UR DSC in Hollywood, she might be expected to mature into more serious roles, ones that deal with moral or philosophical issues, and this has come to pass. She holds awards from the Italian Film Institute for her contributions in international filmmaking and a Presidential Citation for two trips she made to Viet Nam to entertain the troops.

## Page 100

### JANIS JOPLIN JANUARY 19, 1943; 9:45 AM CWT PORT ARTHUR, TX

<!-- chunk-id: acg-src-lewis-guttman-1989.page-0100.chunk-001 -->
<!-- chunk-sha256: c895c16c5320c72c01c907ee9144178386aabc8848b94d9e93ae36668e38671c -->
Born in the blue-collar town of Port Arthur on the Gulf of Mexico, Janis Joplin never related much to her surroundings. Saturn and Uranus were conjunct at her birth with SA IC the most angular placement. (See Elvis Presley and Gandhi elsewhere in this book.) Like Elvis Presley, another Capricorn Sun opposite Pluto, fame, glory, money anda meteoric rise to superstardom were not enough to overcome the deep, early wounds often pertaining to class issues that a SA IC can sometimes imply. At 17, she ran away from home, and at 27 (her Saturn return), she ran away from life, dying of an overdose of heroin. In these 10 years, she got what she once asked for—to live 10 years of superhype rather than die at 70 in a rocking chair. 95 Once she left Texas and arrived in California, she discovered other people who felt as she did, and who looked like she did. In Texas, she was the stranger ina strange land with URIC. In Los Angeles, she felt she fit (SU ASC), and other people imitated her. She established San Francisco as her home base (JU DSC anda JU*NE latitude crossing) but traveled extensively doing concerts and personal appearances. She earned the distinction from critics and fans of being the only white and the only woman who could sing blues and soul so naturally, so intensely. She loved to shock people with her appearance, her behavior and her addictions. In Florida she was arrested for screaming obscenities at a police officer (MA MH). Bette Midler's portrayal of Janis in the film The Rose gave the public a chance to see the sensitive little child behind the hard rock facade.

## Page 102

### MICK JAGGER JULY 26, 1943; 2:30 AM BDST DARTFORD, KENT, ENGLAND

<!-- chunk-id: acg-src-lewis-guttman-1989.page-0102.chunk-001 -->
<!-- chunk-sha256: 849d5cda3345d0ef881d21f6fae5ba3f7a3a9286162107ac9234feb87921e3ef -->
A Leo with four planets (SU, JU, ME, PL) in Leo on the IC, Mick Jagger has truly become a myth in his own time, powered by enough energy and charisma for five people. If Elvis was the undisputed king of American rock, then Mick Jagger has held the title in Britain during the last two decades. Like most Leos of his generation, the playful, creative essence of the sign is darkly tinted with Pluto's intensity, and with moralistic Jupiter caught between the Sun and Pluto, bourgeois values didn't stand a chance. Mick Jagger symbolizes as much as anyone the entire Pluto in Leo generation, or at least its darker side. 97 Arriving in the USA in the mid '60s, he and the Rolling Stones carried with them the label of Britain's "bad boys of rock and roll," emphasizing Jagger's Pluto and Leo planets descending in New York. They've toured the entire globe, both entertaining and causing minor hysteria in their wake. The role of bad boys in the "flower power" generation turned sour at the Stones' concert at Altamont near San Francisco where Hell's Angels, invited to provide "security," beat several people to death under Jagger's SA DSC. In spite of incidents like that and the drug-related deaths of members of his own group, Jagger's high-wired energy, enthusiasm and spirit has kept him and his music popular. His only durable liaison, Bianca Jagger, came from Nicaragua, where Jagger's PL DSC is closest.

## Page 104

### LIZA MINNELLI MARCH 12, 1946; 7:58 AM PST LOS ANGELES, CA

<!-- chunk-id: acg-src-lewis-guttman-1989.page-0104.chunk-001 -->
<!-- chunk-sha256: 4f063cdd4364fe6358095afbe29303c61547a73cb272e37fedc7101b3069474b -->
Child of legendary parents Judy Garland and Vincent Minnelli, Liza grew up in their shadows and has sought throughout her life to discover and to become herself. Caring for her neurotic mother for much of her youth in Hollywood, Liza claims she skipped childhood, assuming many of the responsibilities of running the household and paying the bills. This is appropriate to her SA conjunct the MO, occupying the angle of the home, the IC, in Hollywood. This SA-MO combination scars deeply in childhood but develops extraordinary capacity to accomplish since no amount of success ever seems to fill the void this aspect creates (see also Joan Crawford in this section). MA conjoins closely also, conferring energy and, probably, a taste for difficult male partners. JU on the DSC assures success and help from others. No lines are near New York, but the SU MH and UR ASC are the closest, and a ME/ASC*VE/ASC crossing 99 occupies its latitude, so she prefers the Broadway stage to Hollywood. She has not done particularly well with her Hollywood film career with the exception of Cabaret, which was filmed in Germany (UR MH), and Arthur, filmed in New York. She has become practically synonymous with the song "New York, New York" and receives enthusiastic ovations wherever she performs it. She's at her best in cabaret performance although she is a triple crown winner (Oscar, Emmy, and Tony). In July 1984, after a 20-year battle with drugs and alcohol, Liza checked into the Betty Ford Center in California for detox, counseling and recovery, still dealing with her difficult psychological inheritance. She claims to have been healed and remains as popular and dynamic a performer as ever. Her first marriage to Australian-born (PL DSC, JU MH) Peter Allen ended in divorce as did second marriage to film producer Jack Haley, Jr. Third husband Mark Gero and she make their home in New York.

## Page 106

### DAVID BOWIE JANUARY 8, 1947; 11:50 PM BST BRIXTON, ENGLAND

<!-- chunk-id: acg-src-lewis-guttman-1989.page-0106.chunk-001 -->
<!-- chunk-sha256: 2c38a86b5acfdb970f9b888f470d186e5d8fca4248398a33a0f6d7844693d010 -->
Rock star of English origins, David Bowie was born near London with the SU IC, MA IC, MEIC and NE ASC angular and four planetary crossings at London's latitude—JU*MA, MO*UR, JU*SU and SAVE. He calls himself an actor rather than a rock star, and after a successful career in music, he made several popular Hollywood films. He began his career studying art but eventually was drawn to the glitter and flash that marked pop music entertainers in the '70s. He was born with Neptune on the Ascendant, which suggests something other-worldly and mysterious about him and encourages his fans to project their fantasies onto him. The importance of his Neptune is further supported by the fact that it is on the zenith at the Equator. Neptune on the Ascendant conflicts with a Sun- Mercury conjunction in Capricorn, the sign of realism, 101 of tangibles and of entrepreneurial skills. What he created from the combination was a spectacular show of talent, costuming and staging in his acts, blending art, psychedelics and outer space phenomena to the beat of rock and roll, all supported by an efficient business organization. He was an instant success on both sides of the Atlantic. In Hollywood his first major film, The Man Who Fell to Earth, portrayed him as an androgynous extraterrestrial, a persona he was fond of adopting in his rock acts as well. Hollywood sits midway between his ME DSC and UR lines, UR ASC being an appropriate planetary signature for this stranger in a strange land, and ME suggesting androgyny, youth and widespread appeal to ordinary people. He has toured the world, has a dedicated following and has achieved an enduring creative success. He has lived in New York near his MO-PL ASC.

## Page 108

### FREDDIE PRINZE JUNE 22, 1954; 4:09 AM EDT MANHATTAN, NY

<!-- chunk-id: acg-src-lewis-guttman-1989.page-0108.chunk-001 -->
<!-- chunk-sha256: 29c2bd0a917b91fadadee7f3b6feac57807dcf83dabca5a0e5d76de6f9dd0573 -->
A survivor of a childhood in the Harlem/Washington Heights section of New York, closest to PLIC, Freddie Prinze's was a rags-to-riches story with a tragic ending. His mother was Puerto Rican, and his father was Hungarian. As a teenager, he worked the New York stand-up comedy circuit before he was signed as a comedian for a show in Los Angeles, which eventually led to a TV contract. In the midst of a very successful TV comedy series of which he was a co-star, one that insured further career success and opportunities, he shot and killed himself. No one really understood why, though conspiracy theorist Mae Brussel believed his liberal politics and high visibility may have caused 103 his assassination by right-wing forces. The three planet lines over Los Angeles are MA MH, JUIC and SUIC, planets that together usually symbolize an excess of masculine energy often leading to some form of violence, either overt or projected. Bhagwan Rajneesh, Jim Jones and Lyndon Johnson (see maps elsewhere in this book) all exemplify the external manifestation in different forms of similar excessively yang potentials. Perhaps because Prinze's Mars was retrograde, his was directed against himself. The SA*VE crossing at Los Angeles' latitude hints at romantic disappointments and shuts off opportunities for feminine aspects of his personality to develop. MA retrograde and SA*VE suggest problems with selfimage, particularly in regard to his masculine identity.

## Page 111

### WOLFGANG MOZART JANUARY 27, 1756; 8:00 PM LMT SALZBURG, AUSTRIA

<!-- chunk-id: acg-src-lewis-guttman-1989.page-0111.chunk-001 -->
<!-- chunk-sha256: 02578b915e78f96a34e05f6577df4f5b7bdf3802749838b877a64b90091c7989 -->
In the 18th century, few people traveled very far from their birthplaces. But Mozart toured extensively though he lived permanently either in Salzburg or Vienna. His traveling began at a very young age as his father paraded the child prodigy before the courts of Europe, becoming his son's road agent. Uranus stands out most prominently in both his natal chart and relocations. Since Mozart is an Aquarian with Uranus in exact opposition to the Ascendant at birth, its significance suggests that residence under the Sun's ruler lines can unleash powerful creative forces. Uranus did not help him interface very well with society, however, and his attempts at conformity were awkward at best. Only his musical genius allowed him to get away with his idiosyncratic behavior, behavior 107 suggested by the fact that on the latitudes of Salzburg and Vienna Mozart was born with five planetary crossings: MO/DSC*MA/ASC; JU/IC*ME/DSC;

### MO/MH* VE/ASC; PL/MH*VE/ASC; and JU/IC#SU/

<!-- chunk-id: acg-src-lewis-guttman-1989.page-0111.chunk-002 -->
<!-- chunk-sha256: 4e7ac15f92ecf2cc068e776663eda44cacc88e4f459e65fa0f4e026d0f08ce23 -->
DSC. Unable to play the political games required ofan artist in his day, his music was truly recognized and appreciated only after he drank himself to death at age

### 35. His exact Moon-Pluto conjunction in the 4th

<!-- chunk-id: acg-src-lewis-guttman-1989.page-0111.chunk-003 -->
<!-- chunk-sha256: 33720f01f634dbee43e07f6146a2a0c0c8f73f48d9ce70dbb72f76fd97dedd0a -->
House describes some of the deeper, blacker aspects of his relationship with his ambitious father. This same Sagittarian MO-PL conjunction on the DSC along with UR MH and MA ASC in New York City received a transit from Uranus during the 1985-86 period. At this time, Amadeus became botha Broadway and Hollywood hit, resurrecting Mozart at age 230 and demonstrating that one's horoscope responds to transits long after the individual it symbolizes has gone elsewhere.

## Page 113

### PAUL CEZANNE JANUARY 19, 1839; 1:00 AM LMT AIX-EN-PROVENCE, FRANCE

<!-- chunk-id: acg-src-lewis-guttman-1989.page-0113.chunk-001 -->
<!-- chunk-sha256: 151df28894d882985821dea06352531eebb77b745a5483a66c747b212581cc2f -->
Having endured a lonely and maladjusted childhood, Cezanne failed to gain admission to art school and thus required continued meager support from his father. His introversion seems indicated by crossings of PL*NE, SA*MO, SA*NE and MA*SA within one degree of Paris's latitude. His first one-man show occurred when he was 56, but before his death he had become one of the most important influences in 19th-century art. His success came with the first wave of French Impressionism that swept the art world at the end of the century prior to its acceptance by the mainstream much later. 109 NE on the IC at his birthplace suggests an early life situation of isolation and privation, but it also intimates a high degree of artistic sensitivity. With the Sun and Venus, creative expression of the most subjective moods and feelings is assured as well as Cézanne's artistic command of light. A consistently preferred astrological key word for Neptune has been "impressionable" or "impressionistic," and many of the French Impressionists of the 19th century were born with it angular (see the following maps of Renoir and Gauguin). In particular, Venus-Neptune combinations indicate unusual sensitivity to nuances of color and form and suggest romantic, idealized subjects.

## Page 115

### PIERRE AUGUSTE RENOIR FEBRUARY 25, 1841; 6:00 AM LMT LIMOGES, FRANCE

<!-- chunk-id: acg-src-lewis-guttman-1989.page-0115.chunk-001 -->
<!-- chunk-sha256: d8fcbc84646143dc7e11edda9ec37390aeeb3a131ee441780fef67cd90213023 -->
Renoir was one of the earliest French Impressionist painters of the 19th century and has as the only planet angular through France NE ASC, which he shares with most of the other Impressionists. (See also maps of Cézanne and Gauguin in this section.) Witha Pisces Sun, he demonstrates the creative potential of his Sun's ruler lines. Over Rome, he hasa crossing of the SU ASC and JU MH, so he was favored by European aristocracy. When he She ee 111 visited Italy, he was greatly influenced by the works of Raphael. His paintings were colorful, portraying French middleclass life of the time, and he most enjoyed painting women and childen in keeping with his MO/DSC+ VE/DSC crossing on Paris's latitude. While not angular in France, his birth chart contains an exact conjunction of the Moon, Venus and Pluto, an aspect that would tend to an intimate exploration of the feminine character and charm. Appropriate to his dedication to his painting, Pluto suggests an almost fanatical pursuit of his art. He so loved his work that even when crippled with arthritis, brushes were tied to his hands so that he could continue.

## Page 117

### PAUL GAUGUIN JUNE 8, 1848; 10:00 AM LMT PARIS, FRANCE

<!-- chunk-id: acg-src-lewis-guttman-1989.page-0117.chunk-001 -->
<!-- chunk-sha256: 6a301204a5ca1fd9a5515e7715ae4f28bfd6625ecb1006033e7aa5e7990ebee6 -->
Born in France under a NE DSC line, Gauguin's lineage contained Peruvian Indian ancestry, linking him to his NE MH zenith in Peru. His Neptunian sensitivity resulted in his becoming increasingly disillusioned with urbanized European life, ultimately embarking him upon a nomadic life style. He spent much time in Brittany, a less-populated and more backward, rural part of northwestern France. He was inspired by the other French Impressionists of his time (see also Cézanne and Renoir maps previously in this section). He befriended Vincent Van Gogh and the two remained close until the latter's death. 113 In mid-life he left behind a successful business, wife, and five children in France to go to Tahiti. There, his SU IC identifies a place where his deepest artistic instincts are catalyzed by his environment and seek expression. The MO DSC suggests a place where the feminine archetype inspires creativity. Additionally, he has three planetary crossings affecting TahitiSU/ MH*MO/ASC reiterates the lines present there, a potent creative combination; PL/MH*JU/ASC; and PL/DSC*MA/MH. In Tahiti, he painted very sensual, richly colored portraits of ripe fruit, native women, palm trees and family life. He also fathered several children there, again suggesting the fertile union of the masculine and feminine. He died inthe Marquesas Islands at age 55 under his SA ASC.

## Page 119

### PABLO CASALS DECEMBER 30, 1876; 8:00 PM LMT VENDRELL, SPAIN

<!-- chunk-id: acg-src-lewis-guttman-1989.page-0119.chunk-001 -->
<!-- chunk-sha256: b5829168d1eaa7f92ce0352d80c600eca0095acd58078679e2677eaec5d52cf7 -->
Born with NE, ruler of music, on the MH and UR on the ASC, this accomplished musician was just as much a political revolutionary as he was a cellist. He has lived in voluntary exile outside of his native Spain for most of his life to protest the suppression of liberty by the Franco government. He has resided in France, England and his beloved Puerto Rico (VE DSC, SA MH, UR IC). For almost a decade, he declined public performances because he felt he could not honestly accept fees from nations which in his mind had abandoned the Spanish people to Fascism. Neither his love 11M, 49' LAS of music nor his desire for worldly success ever led him to compromise his intense political consciousness. He refused to play in Russia after 1917 as he refused to play in Nazi Germany and Fascist Italy in the '30s. Casals also protested to the U.S. for its treatment of Fidel Castro. He moved to Puerto Rico in 1957 (his mother's birthplace) and developed a deep love for the island and its people. Eventually, he came out of his self-imposed exile and began performing again, first at the White House for John F. Kennedy and later at the United Nations. He taught some summers at the Marlboro (Vermont) School of Music under his UR IC. He was Conductor of the Conservatory of Music in San Juan.

## Page 121

### ISADORA DUNCAN MAY 26, 1878; 2:20 AM LMT SAN FRANCISCO, CA

<!-- chunk-id: acg-src-lewis-guttman-1989.page-0121.chunk-001 -->
<!-- chunk-sha256: 12b1536c641de34fd544cee132e2d22f69efabc353716111b053bc197ffe27dd -->
Isadora Duncan's mother was a piano teacher, and her father deserted the family before her arrival, suggesting a deprived childhood in keeping with the MO ASC and SA ASC just west of San Francisco. Still, with VE ASC and MA IC just to the east, she always believed she was an artist favored by the gods, that she was Aphrodite incarnate. She began dancing in childhood and by age 17 was ready for a professional career. She went to Chicago (SU ASC) to perform and was immediately spotted by an agent and given a job in New York (UR IC). She briefly studied ballet there and in London but began to feel that ballet was too rigid, so she pioneered the freeform, expressive dance for which she is famous. Her unusual art form and freedom of expression were totally rejected by American audiences who expected something European, classical, and traditional. 117 In Europe under her NE MH and UR ASC she became an immediate success and was widely acclaimed for her originality. She opened a school of dance in 1904 at Grunewald near Berlin (PL MH). There she met, fell in love and conceived a child with Gordon Craig, an English stage designer. She did not believe in marriage, he did. Their busy careers made it impossible for them to continue their relationship, so she returned to France where she met Paris Singer, an American. They became lovers and lived together under her Uranus line, defying conventional morality. Her children were killed in an automobile accident in France, and she never quite emotionally recovered. Later, she married a Russian poet 15 years her junior. She was killed in a freakish automobile accident as she approached her 50th birthday, again under her UR ASC line in the south of France.

### GEORGIA O'KEEFFE NOVEMBER 15, 1887; 7:30 AM LMT SUN PRAIRIE, WI

<!-- chunk-id: acg-src-lewis-guttman-1989.page-0123.chunk-002 -->
<!-- chunk-sha256: a61028139991d0d68e213159e007837c5c19b35ea387f78292a10808a0090c08 -->
AUTHORS' NOTE: This map was drawn for a speculative time of birth based on facts about her life. Like her life story, her birth date and place are well documented in the many biographies about her, but no time appears in any of them. Georgia O'Keeffe was one of the great American artists of the 20th century. She grew up ona farm in Wisconsin (ME ASC and NE DSC) and began painting as a child. Showing some interest and some talent, her mother encouraged her to continue with her art lessons. She studied fora time at the Art Institute in Chicago (MA MH), but her enrollment there was short-lived due to family financial difficulties. After spending some time in New York studying and painting (VE MB), she was offered a job ina small town in west Texas (SU and MO ASC) teaching art to schoolchildren. While it was a far cry from what she had hoped at the time, she saw this as the only real opportunity available fora woman artist and used her time there productively. She referred to this part of Texas as her spiritual home many years later. Through friends and acquaintances, particularly her lifelong confidante Anita Pollitzer, she and her work were introduced to the famous New York photographer, gallery owner and patron to young American artists Alfred Stieglitz, who was immediately impressed. He photographed O'Keeffe, had showings for her in his gallery, and eventually became her agent, husband and mouthpiece to the world. This arrangement worked well for the very private, double- Scorpio O'Keeffe, who shunned personal publicity and chitchat, and it continued until Stieglitz's death in 1946. 153€ 26' 119 Bs 18" While O Keeffe's artwork showed much promise and merit, it was so different from anything else then being shown that without Stieglitz's patronage and salesmanship it is doubtful that she would have been so successful so soon. They were together for over 40 years, but the relationship suffered several interruptions, due to Georgia's inability to withstand Stieglitz's active New York social life and her own fierce need for independence. One summer she vacationed in Taos, New Mexico (SU, MO and JU ASC), and soon after, she discovered Abiquiu where she knew she had found her home. She, like the other artists that had come to New Mexico, discovered the brilliance and uniqueness of the light that made painting so special. Using New York City for business purposes and New Mexico for artistic and creative fulfillment, Georgia began a 30-year commute. The summers allowed her to paint to her heart's content, and it was here she did her "bones" series that became so famous. Observing the effect that the clarity of New Mexico's light had upon her increasingly more impressive and saleable artwork, Stieglitz honored O Keeffe with an annual show in New York City (VE MH) each winter. After Stieglitz died, O'Keeffe built a permanent home at Ghost Ranch in Abiquiu and lived there full-time. This was also a period when she began to travel, and she made her first trip to Europe in the early 1950s. Europe did little to impress her with the exception of Spain where she immediately fell in love with the work of Goya. In Italy (MA DSC), she was repelled by what she called the "vulgarity" of Italian art and architecture with its ornate frescoes and giant cherubs. Her style was simple, no frills, and to the point. She continued to paint in her adopted New Mexico home until her death at age 98.

## Page 125

### RUDOLF BING JANUARY 9, 1902; 8:00 AM MET VIENNA, AUSTRIA

<!-- chunk-id: acg-src-lewis-guttman-1989.page-0125.chunk-001 -->
<!-- chunk-sha256: 7abb9d80cee09672931d6b380a31f993050c3c2b83d78e7832ebad1345782bec -->
The century's most outstanding operatic impresario was born with a five-planet stellium in Capricorn widely inconjunct Neptune. Capricorn shows tremendous entrepreneurial, management and leadership ability, but it was in the musical fantasy world of Neptune that Rudolf Bing made his mark as a business manager. He held positions as director of major opera companies in England, Germany and Scotland and helped found the Edinburgh Opera Company (UR ASC). He was 121 knighted by Queen Elizabeth II in 1971. As director of the Metropolitan Opera Company for two decades, his name became synonymous with opera in New York City. While there are no lines, New York lies at the exact midpoint of VEIC and MA IC. Assuming this is an accurate birth time, this exemplifies an occasionally seen phenomenon where an individual with a highly stressed chart (i.e., multiple oppositions and/or squares, particularly if involved with outer planets) finds success away from all lines and crossings or near those of the unaspected, inner planets.

### PE LEST LEONTYNE PRICE FEBRUARY 10, 1927; 6:15 AM CST

### LAUREL, MS

<!-- chunk-id: acg-src-lewis-guttman-1989.page-0127.chunk-002 -->
<!-- chunk-sha256: 5456743cc7473fafcfb01c6cef4a6d1063f8648d6023602fceb072a07da1eda7 -->
The odds against a black woman born in Mississippi in 1927 singing on the stage of the Metropolitan Opera could probably have been calculated in astronomical sums prior to Leontyne Price's meteoric career. Few peopie prosper where a grand square is angular, but Price proved strong enough to overcome discrimination (SA MH and the MO IC) with artistic brilliance (SU, ME, VE and JU on the ASC opposite NE DSC). The line closest to New York City is NE DSC, illustrating that music and her attitude that her gift comes from God are among the few paths that avoid the self- 1238 delusions and other pitfalls of that planet. Many of the same planets are angular in La Scala, Vienna, Washington and San Francisco where she had sensational debuts. Her many planets in New York recall the chart of Willie Mays (q.v.) who overcame similar obstacles to self-expression. Saturn and the Moon angular together might be called the "I'll get even aspect." In addition to Leontyne Price and Willie Mays, the pair is important in the charts of

### Gandhi, Isadora Duncan, Joan Crawford, Margaret

<!-- chunk-id: acg-src-lewis-guttman-1989.page-0127.chunk-003 -->
<!-- chunk-sha256: c23787161c9929f8ba614f4a9cbebc049226f8fa185da525802c3e9a7e9c7c32 -->
Thatcher and Liza Minnelli (q.v.). They suggest deprivation in childhood and a resultant ability to rely on oneself, as well as the formation of an implacable desire to succeed over any odds.

### HONORE DE BALZAC

## Page 130

### HONORE DE BALZAC MAY 20, 1799; 11:00 AM LMT TOURS, FRANCE

<!-- chunk-id: acg-src-lewis-guttman-1989.page-0130.chunk-001 -->
<!-- chunk-sha256: a0793f663ad6de40b800d9401b07561d00406bf2060a3fbc2a03daefdf1fd10b -->
Balzac, born in France under his PL DSC, was a man of extremes who alternately experienced poverty and wealth, starvation and indulgence during his lifetime. His lonely and miserable childhood was mitigated by the development of a great love and understanding of literature. All but neglected by his family, he was passed from one boarding school to another. He was an avid reader and studied law as well. Befitting his PL*MA crossing at his birthplace's latitude, and as a desperate measure, he began writing sensational, slightly pornographic novels, publishing them under a pseudonym. Living in a garage, he was ridden with debts which were increased rather than relieved by 127 his business ventures (ME MH opposite NE IC). So, he wrote with feverish activity, sleeping only a few hours a day. Impelled by these constant financial problems, he produced a vast collection of novels over a 20-year period, the best known of which is The Human Comedy. This work is a microcosm of French society of his time, satirizing in merciless Plutonian detail individuals from every class and profession. It was unique in its depiction of contemporary French street life. Despite great success and notoriety in France, his books were banned in relatively prudish Victorian England. After a 17-year relationship with a Polish noblewoman (JU and SU MH), they married in the Ukraine, but, ironically, he died a few months later.

## Page 132

### KARL MARX MAY 5, 1818; 2:00 AM LMT TRIER, GERMANY

<!-- chunk-id: acg-src-lewis-guttman-1989.page-0132.chunk-001 -->
<!-- chunk-sha256: 5ab41abb34333c93243ff5621315e990c94f4f752dce0e582b47cf78623c6fcf -->
Born in Germany under a UR MH line, Karl Marx proved the proverbial strength of the pen over the sword by writing books that literally changed the world. And he wrote them under his ME IC line in Edinburgh and London. His ideological vision of the perfect state has focused the forces of change for the entire political world for nearly two centuries after his birth. Uranus, directly overhead at his birthplace with Neptune nearby, suggests totally individualistic idealism, and his birth in the era of the last Uranus-Neptune conjunction expressed its potential to unify concepts of self-direction and service to a greater whole. In these last years of this Uranus-Neptune cycle, the Communist world is in transition and significant re- 29 organization, and Marx's vision seems at last to be accommodating modification by reality. How different countries have applied Marx's ideas is illuminated by his birth map. Over Moscow, where the Bolsheviks interpreted his concepts as the answer to all economic and political problems, JU sits at the MH along with Marx's SU and MO at the ASC. Here, like Zeus, he is chief among the immortals. In Leningrad, the SU, MO, MEand VEall occupy the ASC. By contrast, his theories have caused generations of fear and paranoid reactions in Washington, D.C., and there sits a crossing of SA at the IC and ME at the DSC, suggesting at least bad reviews for his books. In Peking, where a 3,000year-old civilization was completely reorganized by violent application of his ideas, PL directly occupies the MH, crossed by MA at the ASC.

## Page 134

### GERTRUDE STEIN FEBRUARY 3, 1874; 8:00 AM LMT ALLEGHENY CO., PA

<!-- chunk-id: acg-src-lewis-guttman-1989.page-0134.chunk-001 -->
<!-- chunk-sha256: 3a2ec31eefa92f87b6983c5491be005cf79e7d66908bd8b22ad0555fbb057ef4 -->
With an Aquarian Sun, Mercury, Venus and Saturn in the 12th House opposite Uranus, this was a woman who lived life on her own terms. But she felt she couldn't do it safely in her own country. She fled to the inteliectual underground of Paris (MO IC, SU*PL latitude crossing) in the '20s where a number of other American expatriates lived at the time. She was a journalist, poet, and avant-garde playwright, and with her lifetime lover, Alice B. Toklas, she held a glittering and notorious weekly "salon" at which 131 the most advanced intellectual ideas were discussed among such promising talents as Picasso, Cocteau, Hemingway and Virginia Woolf. The two women's Cookbook contains subtle social satire as well as recipes for hashish candy. Her most famous remark about a place—Oakland, California: "There's no there there'"—seems apt, as "there" she had NE on the IC. With her Aquarian inner planets opposed by Uranus and crossed by PL IC in America's heartland, it can be understood why she didn't fit in, and she offers another example of someone who chose not to live directly under multiple lines focusing conflicting outer planet energies.

## Page 136

<!-- chunk-id: acg-src-lewis-guttman-1989.page-0136.chunk-001 -->
<!-- chunk-sha256: 3f4b4d50b0b00c6c08bdf351e5a092e573a80e1ab01495c59bc3dfc8fb08243c -->
210 87

### D. H. LAWRENCE SEPTEMBER 11, 1885; 6:45 PM LMT EASTWOOD, NOTTINGHAMSHIRE, ENGLAND

<!-- chunk-id: acg-src-lewis-guttman-1989.page-0136.chunk-002 -->
<!-- chunk-sha256: 49bd7df2a83549dac5ae1d05ceda2687961b5e2fc3d30a5416e5c475d67ed5bb -->
English novelist, poet and artist born with Uranus descending and Pluto square Mercury, D. H. Lawrence was an inspired nonconformist whose literature was a vehicle for him to express both his genius and daemon. His work, which celebrated the erotic, was considered pornographic in Victorian England. With a close Moon-Venus conjunction in Libra, Lawrence had a strong attachment to his mother and, of course, to the archetypal feminine ideal. Asa child, he almost died of pneumonia, and he suffered from chronic poor health most of his life. Consequently, he spent much of his time searching for the ideal place to live. He married Frieda von Richthofen of Germany (MO-VE DSC), sister of the World War I flying ace. They were married in Italy. He got more in touch with his male nature in Taos, New Mexico (SU and JU MH), which in the '20s and '30s was a gathering place for the artistic souls who weren't in Paris. People like Georgia O'Keeffe, Mabel Dodge Luhan, and Dorothy Brett all favored Lawrence, the latter two doting over him shamelessly. Mabel 133 built a house for him and Frieda to encourage them to stay. He did much of his best writing there and began to achieve recognition though his journals reveal what modern standards would consider egotism, selfcenteredness and arrogance. Taos proved to be a bit too cold for him in the winter, so he annually traveled to Mexico during those months. During his travels to Australia (Thirroul), he wrote Kangaroo (ME ASC, PL MR). After leaving Taos, he moved to Spotorno, Italy where several intense domestic squabbles, followed by a fire, occurred (NE/DSC* PL/DSC). Near Florence (VE DSC) he wrote Lady Chatterley's Lover and published it there since it was banned in England and the U.S. Although he loved the south of France and Italy, his health went from bad to worse, and he died in France of pleurisy in 1930 under his SA IC. His ashes were returned to the ranch in Taos where he, Frieda and Brett had been sucha quarrelsome, creative and joyful threesome. The D. H. Lawrence Museum is presently located just outside of Taos.

## Page 138

### PEARL BUCK JUNE 26, 1892; 12:30 PM EST HILLSBORO, WV

<!-- chunk-id: acg-src-lewis-guttman-1989.page-0138.chunk-001 -->
<!-- chunk-sha256: 8e2cf9e22bc2bdda9a1a9e13972c06fe5cc625d18b34bd64add9ec58f44c4ea5 -->
Pearl Buck was recognized as one of the greatest and most original American authors of her time—a time when few women authors received credit for their work. She was born between her SU and ME MH lines of evangelist parents, who were soon thereafter assigned to missions in China. She grew up tutored both in English and Chinese and as a Cancer-born Sun was able to feel affection for and assimilate a great deal of her bicultural environment. 135 As a young woman, she returned to the U.S. and received an M.A. in English from Cornell University under ME MH line. Back in China, she taught at the University of Nanking near Shanghai, and it was there under her VEIC and UR DSC that she defied convention with her tender, sentimental and humanistic writing about the stability and beauty of Chinese life. Through the major cities of eastern China pass Ms. Buck's ME IC, VE IC, JU ASC, and UR DSC lines. In 1932, she received a Pulitzer Prize for The Good Earth, and in 1938, she won the Nobel Prize in Literature for the same work, accepting it under her JU IC near her

### NE DSC.

## Page 140

### ERNEST HEMINGWAY JULY 21, 1899; 8:00 AM CST OAK PARK, IL

<!-- chunk-id: acg-src-lewis-guttman-1989.page-0140.chunk-001 -->
<!-- chunk-sha256: c411b870bb3e786dc457d255022865c7dc586940e29798b445dd313308a39bab -->
Ernest Hemingway may have been "born to wander," having UR on his IC in his Chicago birthplace. The son of a doctor, his early interest in journalism led him toa job as a reporter in Kansas City between his ME ASC and UR IC lines. But maturity left him lonely and somewhat misunderstood; he seemed driven to live life to the fullest—surprising, shocking and interesting the world as he went. He loved Europe and, like many other expatriates of the time, he settled in Paris in 1922, though he frequently moved about the Continent. In 1923, he attended his first fiesta in Pamplona, Spain. The locales of his bestknown writings are in Spain, Italy and France under his ME MH lines. His natal Mars is squared by Saturn and Pluto, so the 137

### 1DAx BD

<!-- chunk-id: acg-src-lewis-guttman-1989.page-0140.chunk-002 -->
<!-- chunk-sha256: be46dd120f031fe1b96a2c698cf37e2e99f206ad61a6238199d15f65749dae00 -->
issue of masculinity could never be settled satisfactorily. In his quest for adventure, he became a Red Cross ambulance driver and was wounded in Italy, not far from MA MH. During the late '20s and early '30s, he came back to live in the U.S. but still made three trips to Spain during the Civil War. Hemingway loved the masculine flavor of Batista's Cuba and settled there in 1942-43. During the war, he patrolled for German submarines off the Cuban coast under his SA IC, PLMH and MA ASC. He was in Paris for the D-Day landing and liberation. He was injured in two airplane crashes in Uganda (NE DSC, MA MH zenith) in 1954. In 1953, he won the Pulitzer Prize and was awarded the Nobel Prize for Literature in 1954. In 1959, he boughta house in Ketchum, Idaho under his JUIC and ME ASC lines. Two years later, he committed suicide with his own shotgun, close to his SA*MA crossing latitude.

## Page 142

### ALFRED HITCHCOCK AUGUST 13, 1899; 3:15 AM BST LEYTONSTONE, ESSEX, ENGLAND

<!-- chunk-id: acg-src-lewis-guttman-1989.page-0142.chunk-001 -->
<!-- chunk-sha256: 947637c25c1472dc15117dedcdf7a39bf3253de0532db0537878783020db4c33 -->
Hitchcock earned immortality as the master filmmaker of the macabre. Unlike contemporary thrillers, which attempt to outdo one another in graphic, gory excess, Hitchcock's works subtly built suspense with mysterious plot twists and masterful psychology. His restraint and morbid interests are aptly symbolized by an exact SA MH-PL IC opposition over California with ME DSC lending symbolization skills. His natal VE ASC in England made him seem mild-mannered and pleasant, 139 an ironic contrast to the nightmarish quality of Psycho, The Birds, and his other well-known works. JU DSC is closest to New York where he produced his successful TV series which is still in syndication some 30 years later. Since the usually accepted astrological interpretations of Saturn and Pluto together would give almost anyone cause to reconsider living under their influence, Hitchcock's success should point out the possibility of using such seemingly negative influences in a creative and self-actualizing manner.

## Page 144

### MARGARET MEAD DECEMBER 16, 1901; 9:00 AM EST PHILADELPHIA, PA

<!-- chunk-id: acg-src-lewis-guttman-1989.page-0144.chunk-001 -->
<!-- chunk-sha256: ecaeaa9610fcbc23c1d14db0ad51771878af2feea4b5918031369d419d311d25 -->
Margaret Mead grew up in Philadelphia and completed most of her schooling in New York where MA, JU and SA on the ASC conferred the power and self-assurance to venture into intellectual areas considered closed to women of her era. At Columbia University, she studied anthropology and upon graduation became Curator of Ethnology for the American Museum of Natural History in New York for some time. She is best known for her work in New Guinea where she explored and documented aspects of tribal, ritual and family life that until then were unknown in the 141 Western world. Growing Up In New Guinea was the first work ofits kind and broadly influenced all subsequent anthropology with its humane, non-judgmental style. It was considered quite revolutionary to discuss sexual mores so openly (especially for a woman), quite in keeping with iconoclastic PL MH, UR, ME and SUIC, all of which pass through New Guinea on her map. Their position on the IC indicate that she was changed by being there far more than she affected the people she observed and described. UR and PL lines also cross at the island's latitude. Her basic assumption that all humans in essence behave alike was carried through in her Coming Of Age In Samoa (VEIC), a similar piece about tribal and family life there.
