## Page 146

### JACK KEROUAC MARCH 12, 1922; 5:12 PM EST LOWELL, MA

<!-- chunk-id: acg-src-lewis-guttman-1989.page-0146.chunk-001 -->
<!-- chunk-sha256: 3532731324451e0e64f6cb953ffd3bf2d17387428688ae89d07bd1aa5a3e17c4 -->
Born of French-Canadian parents, Jack Kerouac grew up in Lowell, Massachusetts near his MO ASC and MA IC crossing with UR DSC nearby. These planets aptly symbolize his lifelong struggle to maintain his nonconformist identity apart from his mother and background, a struggle that propelled him toward intensity and extremity. When he attended Columbia College in New York on a football scholarship, the emphasis shifted to UR, and where it occupies the DSC he befriended Allen Ginsberg, Neal Cassady and the other "beats" whom he was to immortalize in his writings. Cassady, whom Kerouac idealized as Dean Moriarty, came from an alcoholic father in Denver (Kerouac's NE ASC), and after Cassady's death in 1968, Kerouac lost all control of his own drinking. 143 His stablest period seemed to occur in northern California and the Pacific Northwest under his JU IC line. Not much attracted to Jupiter's comfortable pleasures, however, he preferred the intense, manic search for "kicks" that kept him on the move, a point of consciousness that was among the first to observe and record the transformation of how America saw itself. Many of the literary "beats" have the Moon, Mars, and Uranus mutually aspected, but few have ended as sadly as Kerouac, who drank himself to death in his mother's house in St. Petersburg, Florida at the latitude of the MO*UR crossing in 1969. His best-known work, On The Road (1957), is a romantic paean to working-class Middle America, astrologically symbolized perhaps by his ME DSC and NE ASC lines in central USA. His vision of the 1950s rings with the childlike innocence and poetry of those planets.

## Page 148

### JAMES CLAVELL OCTOBER 10, 1924; 9:50 AM EST SYDNEY, AUSTRALIA

<!-- chunk-id: acg-src-lewis-guttman-1989.page-0148.chunk-001 -->
<!-- chunk-sha256: 361e852434e8359e8f48b7517684abd6c90f900dc12175631f4840376037f7d6 -->
Known for his epic novels and film productions of such best sellers as Shogun and Tai-Pan, James Clavell was born in Australia of British parents. With the Moon and Uranus natally on his IC, a life of travel and multicultural interests was foreshadowed. His father was a captain in the British Royal Navy stationed in Hong Kong, and Clavell was schooled in England under his SU IC. During World War II, he was captured in Java (SA ASC) and taken to one of the most severe Japanese POW camps that existed near Singapore. Few survived this camp on the latitude of his PL/DSC*SU/

### MH, very close to SA ASC. Of his POW days, he says

<!-- chunk-id: acg-src-lewis-guttman-1989.page-0148.chunk-002 -->
<!-- chunk-sha256: 4fb7c7827e211c971757e32a9f22162b505335c474f7c5f35d6b627a98f08a45 -->
"The Japanese tore the heart out of me when I was 18. It was a school for survivors. It gave me a strength most people don't have. So long as I remember Changi, I 145 know I'm living 40 borrowed lifetimes. What else can they do to me?" After the war, Clavell immigrated to the U.S. His first stop was New York, then Hollywood, as he had ambitions to write and direct films. The MO-UR conjunction on his IC at birth rises here with JU in the MH, so he quickly scored several film successes, including cowriting The Great Escape, a film about POWs in Germany during World War II, and To Sir With Love, a film about a black teacher in a rowdy London high school. To Sir became a smash hit in the '60s. After several other films, his most outstanding success came with the publication and subsequent TV miniseries of Shogun, his epic story of an English shipping crew who arrive in Japan (VE and NE MH, MA IC) to find vast cultural differences. He followed this with the publication of Tai-Pan, a novel based on the settlement, politics, and economics of Hong Kong. He has residences in England, California, and British Columbia.

### ELISABETH KUBLER-ROSS JULY 8, 1926; 10:45 PM MET ZURICH, SWITZERLAND

<!-- chunk-id: acg-src-lewis-guttman-1989.page-0150.chunk-002 -->
<!-- chunk-sha256: 46f94e17ef55f6aac164b4ddb2f3acfc9a6a62a4aa0769c5f1653fc427e9a753 -->
Born one of triplets to a Swiss physician, Elisabeth followed in her father's footsteps and completed medical training. JU ASC, NE DSC and VE IC in Zurich assure compassion for the suffering of others, mysticism and healing capacities. As a Cancer born with the Sun conjunct Pluto at the exact latitude at which these two planets are simultaneously angular (SU*PL "paran"), she became interested in caring for the terminally ill. She left Switzerland to settle in the United States. Over New York City, both UR IC and VE DSC predominate, indicating a transformation in her self-image which affects society, as well as an unusually real and direct mode of relationship. 147 Dissatisfied with the way she saw modern medicine and its technology treat the dying, Kubler-Ross pioneered humane and nurturing programs in hospitals all across the country that were to become the theoretical basis for most modern hospice training programs and manuals. She lectured at universities, hospitals, and health care centers as part of her mission to educate doctors, nurses, hospital staff and families in her methods which enable the terminally ill to die with dignity. Her books have become best sellers, and she has become the acknowledged leader of the hospice movement. The map also shows zenith circles of the SU, PL, MO and VE at nearly exactly the same latitude, indicating linkages (parallels) between the planets of life, death, nurturing and harmony.

## Page 152

### ORIANA FALLACI JUNE 29, 1929; 11:00 PM MET FLORENCE, ITALY

<!-- chunk-id: acg-src-lewis-guttman-1989.page-0152.chunk-001 -->
<!-- chunk-sha256: 130bfdfcfd9f8f36722aded51970e50546afc7ec36f3916aaf7e930586680166 -->
Oriana Fallaci has been called the most adept political interviewer in the contemporary world, having covered political and social upheavals on five continents. She is a recipient of the St. Vincent Prize, Italy's Pulitzer equivalent, for her analysis of the Vietnam War, but she is even better known for her intimate, Barbara Walters-type interviews with leading world figures. Of Spanish and Italian descent, she was born in Florence, Italy under her ME IC line and on the latitude of a crossing of ME/DSC*MO/IC. Growing up in war-torn Italy in an impoverished family, she has known all the horrors of war, bombings, terror and hunger. She chose to be a journalist for financial reasons and got her start on the night beat reporting for a Florence newspaper. 149 Later she traveled and has covered everything from revolutions to royal weddings. She is known for succinct reportage covering NASA's space program at Cape Canaveral (JU DSC) and at the Houston Space Center (MA-NE MH, VE DSC), and the war in Viet Nam (SA DSC, ME ASC). In Mexico City, she was wounded at the 1968 Olympics while covering a government massacre of protesting students (MA-NE MH, VE DSC; on the latitude of UR/MH*PL/ASC lines crossing). She has never married legally but has admitted she feels a "soul connection" with Alexandros Panagulis, a Greek poet and resistance movement leader. New York's upper east side is her North American home, and JU DSC there and all along the eastern seaboard assures success in publishing as well as the patronage of important people.

## Page 154

### GLORIA STEINEM MARCH 25, 1934; 10:00 PM EST TOLEDO, OH

<!-- chunk-id: acg-src-lewis-guttman-1989.page-0154.chunk-001 -->
<!-- chunk-sha256: 49ffabac5b1b5e533b89fa3df93589384cd46e5c50bb26252e2fc0b2008a57a6 -->
A birth signature of SA and VE IC in Ohio are fitting symbols for Gloria Steinem, who has come to be known as a feminist (VE) institution (SA). Her grandmother, Pauline Steinem, was president of the Ohio Woman's Suffrage Association from 1908-11 and suggests a family heritage of SAIC. This placement also indicates the years of hardship she endured in her youth. After the divorce of her parents, she cared for her mother, who suffered from chronic anxiety and depression, while living in a basement tenement in Toledo. Steinem attended Smith College, graduating Phi Beta Kappa (MEIC), and wona fellowship to study in India 151 (PLIC, MO IC) during a period of social unrest. Returning to New York near her Mercury line, writing became her passion and profession. She took an early active stance in the feminist movement and helped found and edit Ms. magazine which established a solid forum for women authors, political and social activists and artists. With MEIC over New York and a PL*JU crossing at New York's latitude, she became a highly visible, articulate and charismatic voice of the movement. For 15 years she sat at the helm of the magazine until it was sold in 1987 to an Australian communications conglomerate (JU IC) which retained her services as consultant. She continues to redefine the role of women in contemporary American society through writing, business and by example.

## Page 157

### FRANKLIN D. ROOSEVELT JANUARY 30, 1882; 8:45 PM LMT HYDE PARK, NY

<!-- chunk-id: acg-src-lewis-guttman-1989.page-0157.chunk-001 -->
<!-- chunk-sha256: c4dd3c88ff8150dba9c198615fef552730d492b48c4fb7a57d18137d306faa1a -->
Franklin D. Roosevelt was elected to and served more than three terms as U.S. President, holding the office longer than anyone else in history. An Aquarian with Uranus close to the Ascendant, he was first elected ata time when the country was in the Great Depression following the Wall Street crash of 1929. Necessity impelled him to institute many governmental reforms and radical responses to the economic problems of the time, and for these he came to be regarded as a genuine hero in those dark days when the country so desperately needed one. But Roosevelt was scarcely a proletarian. Born into a wealthy, upper-class family, he toured Europe eight times before the age of 16. His political life began with his election to the New York State Senate at age 28. While vacationing in New Brunswick, Canada (UR ASC, MA MH) in 1921, he was stricken with poliomyelitis and was paralyzed from the waist down. By unremitting effort, he recovered partial use of his legs, and, supported by his wife Eleanor, he resumed political 155 life and won the New York gubernatorial race in 1928. In the 1932 U.S. presidential election, he beat incumbent Herbert Hoover and quickly asserted control over the devastated economy. The Japanese invasion of Pearl Harbor catapulted Roosevelt and the nation into World War II. Some historians have suggested that he allowed the attack to occur, fearing that only in the face of open aggression would the war gain sufficient public support. In Pearl Harbor, he has the same planets angular that he had in New Brunswick but on different angles: UR IC and MA along with the MO ASC. In Japan, he had SA ASC, while in Berlin his powerful UR held the MH. JU and PL DSC cross exactly over London, suggesting an odds-defying success. He was supportive of the "Good Neighbor Policy" in Latin America, and with PL MH over Mexico City, he increased American influence in that region. At Warm Springs, Georgia under UR ASC, he founded his institute for the rehabilitation of paraplegics. He died there just before the war ended.

<a id="acg-src-lewis-guttman-1989.page-0158"></a>

## Page 159

### HARRY S TRUMAN MAY 8, 1884; 4:00 PM LMT LAMAR, MO

<!-- chunk-id: acg-src-lewis-guttman-1989.page-0159.chunk-001 -->
<!-- chunk-sha256: e1574230f01c237f320b0b4b3beabfb09e7da0e84822ec648b1e2eee49a308d1 -->
Born in 1884 with a fairly wide Saturn-Pluto conjunction, Harry Truman inherited the presidency upon the death of Franklin Roosevelt at a time when the American Empire reached its height of worldwide influence. After World War II's devastation, the gross national product of the U.S. equaled that of the rest of the world combined. Appropriately, JU MH sits near Washington, reflecting imperial hegemony and military victory. To end the war as quickly as possible, Truman ordered nuclear bombs to be dropped on Japan—Hiroshima and Nagasaki—which ended one war, but began another one that would rage for generations to come; since 1945, world powers have been irrationally creating and accumulating nuclear materials, producing toxins and explosives capable of ending life on the planet many times over. Over Japan sits, appropriately enough, Truman's SA- PL conjunction on the ASC, crossing near belligerent MA on the IC. MA IC particularly dominates Korea, which turned out to be the site of the next U.S. active 197) military involvement, one which Truman also led. Truman's MA DSC line passes through Teheran, crossed again by SA and PLIC. He was the first ina long line of U.S. presidents cursed by involvement with this country as he covertly propped up the Shah of Iran (q.v.), secretly intervening to defeat a popular movement toward democracy in 1952. The consequences of this act would keep returning to haunt the U.S. people and future presidents. After the war ended, he was outstanding among world leaders in pushing for the Nuremburg Trials, insisting that the Nazi slaughter of six million people could not be passed off as typical war atrocities. A people-oriented MO MH with VE DSC not far off pass through Eastern Europe, while the SU and NE IC pass through Israel, whose creation was an idealistic vision to which he contributed. He oversaw the Marshall Plan which sent millions of dollars to rebuild Europe after the war and also helped to establish NATO. The "cold war" took hold during his administration, reminiscent of both his natal Saturn-Pluto conjunction and the recurrence of that conjunction while he held office.

## Page 161

### DWIGHT D. EISENHOWER OCTOBER 14, 1890; 5:19 PM LMT DENISON, TX

<!-- chunk-id: acg-src-lewis-guttman-1989.page-0161.chunk-001 -->
<!-- chunk-sha256: f17833c5e787c9b2572d33d367d3506452f6810430fe50b688c9d0a4ef68b2b0 -->
Prior to serving as 34th President of the U.S., Dwight ("Ike") Eisenhower had become America's war hero as Supreme Commander of the Allied Forces in World War II. His German ancestors (MO IC) immigrated first to Pennsylvania (MO DSC), then to the Midwest. Born with the SU, MA, URand ME angular over Texas, Ike was destined for a life of leadership, military success, unexpected changes and popular appeal. His presidency was characterized by his JU MH over Washington, he and Truman being the two leaders to serve at the zenith of the American empire during the years following World War II. The MO DSC suggests his immense popularity, based on his homey, familyoriented and unpretentious image. He attended West Point in New York in 1911 and was graduated in the top third of the class (JU MH, VE DSC). He served in the Panama Canal Zone (MO DSC), and he then accompanied Gen. Douglas MacArthur to the Philippines where he served for four years. As Commander-in-Chief of the Allied Forces in North 159 Africa, Ike led the invasion in November 1942 (SU and UR IC). He subsequently commanded the invasions of Italy and Sicily (MO and URIC). His place in history was secured as commander of the D-Day Force in France (SU and URIC, JU DSC). There, the Allied forces succeeded in establishing a beachhead in occupied France and began to push the Nazis back. He became an instantaneous hero in the U.S. and throughout the European allied nations. Following the war, Ike was appointed as Army Chief of Staff by Truman. After retiring from the Army he became president of Columbia University (JU MH, VE DSC). He also helped to organize NATO. In his first entry into politics, he easily defeated Adlai Stevenson in 1952 and became the governmental institution of the '50s, presiding over America's "easy chair" period and cold war Communist paranoia. As President, he increased spending for military defense, especially that directed against the U.S.S.R. With SA ASC near Moscow, he projected the fears of the nation onto the Soviets. SA MH and PL DSC near Japan indicate the state of that country—devastation and rebuilding—during his term.

## Page 163

### LYNDON B. JOHNSON AUGUST 27, 1908; 4:18 AM LMT GILLESPIE CO., TX

<!-- chunk-id: acg-src-lewis-guttman-1989.page-0163.chunk-001 -->
<!-- chunk-sha256: 528096481ce20b0c38449b92430b76c3a363cda9f28b720d2844b0c74be1d0df -->
Born in Texas, LBJ became the 36th President of the USS. after the assassination of John F. Kennedy. During the early years of his presidency, the country remained in such a state of shock that there was little collective will to question his undertakings. By the time opposition coalesced, he had escalated the war in Viet Nam to irreversible proportions. His presidency took place during the era of the rare Uranus-Pluto conjunction when social turbulence and antiestablishment activism surfaced in American culture. The great undeclared war going on in Viet Nam during his administration was matched by a great cultural and generational conflict which transformed America's ideals and self-image at a time when Uranus and Pluto aspected the national Neptune. Johnson responded with massive government support for civil rights and more equitable economic distribution. Five planets 161 are on the ASC in Washington: SU, MO, ME, MA and JU. On the other side of the world, these same five planets occupy the DSC in Southeast Asia, directly over Viet Nam. The SU, MA and JU DSC most closely affect the region, a combination redolent with masculine, fiery, macho and extravagant energy, planets that seem inseparable from a militaristic, arrogant self-righteousness. MA DSC runs precisely through Saigon, reiterating the path of Mars in the USA Declaration of War chart. These lines, rising in Washington and setting in Viet Nam, symbolize the effect LBJ had on the American treasury—withdrawing its assets to build guns, tanks, bombs, and fighter planes to be destroyed, along with 50,000 American lives, in an impossible war. The MA ASC line is near the Dominican Republic where Johnson sent in the Marines to thwart an overthrow of the U.S.-client dictator. The Sun, planet of ego, along with belligerent Mars and imperial Jupiter seem a combination inclined to militarism despite good intentions, and to extravagance in any case.

## Page 165

### RONALD REAGAN FEBRUARY 6, 1911; 8:25 AM CST TAMPICO, IL

<!-- chunk-id: acg-src-lewis-guttman-1989.page-0165.chunk-001 -->
<!-- chunk-sha256: 5f970b2f67d8a7e077e82a7a9d05555ca26c98fbc41ac38d774f39e5423da5ef -->
The most astrologically significant of the many controversies that surround Ronald Reagan has been that of his time of birth. Over 20 times have been proposed. Astro*Carto*Graphy maps have been tried for every possible time; one for 8:25 AM gives him Pisces rising and seems so startlingly accurate that it is the one delineated below. Ronald Reagan, 40th President of the U.S., was elected in November 1980 and held the office for two terms. He served as Governor of California (UR ASC), and before that he enjoyed a successful career as an actor. The Sun (ASC) nearest Los Angeles indicates a place of strong ego and self-expression. He was both an actor and president of the Screen Actors Guild in that location. PLIC in Nicaragua defines the violent obsession and paranoia he demonstrated about that tiny nation during his entire administration. MA MH in 163 Washington warns of two operations on his colon, minor facial surgeries and a gunshot wound, and gave the retired actor, longing for another starring role ina western, an opportunity for trigger-happy responses to any potential disagreement. SA MH in Moscow identifies the Jungian "shadow"—what he fears most. In his many dialogues with Gorbachev, he yielded little ground, exemplifying Saturn by his cool, programmed caution. The Saturn line is also the nearest to Beirut. JUIC in Iran along with SU DSC and MO MH suggest opportunity and serve to illustrate how Reagan's election was assisted by the Carter hostage crisis in that area. But beginning in late 1986, Reagan's natal JU IC in Iran was transited by PLIC, which natally sat over Nicaragua, linking those two countries just as they became associated in the scandal that bears their names. MEand NE (public relations) lines are found in Grenada, Iceland, Libya and Manila. The VE ASC line goes through first wife Jane Wyman's birthplace.

## Page 167

### RICHARD M. NIXON JANUARY 9, 1913; 9:35 PM PST YORBA LINDA, CA

<!-- chunk-id: acg-src-lewis-guttman-1989.page-0167.chunk-001 -->
<!-- chunk-sha256: 6b77a313e91127e88e28993f60cf97f35489d29ca7918da9a9aa7c6b39f6c754 -->
Coming from California's conservative Republican Party, Richard Nixon was inaugurated 37th President Fic oN O 189548) 130 50 oo. PX | w\ 24 of the U.S. in January 1969. He had made one of the . most remarkable comebacks in American political history after losing to John F. Kennedy in 1960. In his early career as Vice President under Eisenhower, Nixon assisted in convicting Alger Hiss and bringing the House Un-American Activities Committee into the forefront of the American political scene by hounding liberals mercilessly, driving several to suicide, and creating a national anti-Communist hysteria. The SU ASC, NE DSC and SA IC are all near Moscow. As President, he inherited the Viet Nam War and a 165 country in turmoil and might have gone down in history as an able, though unpopular, president. But his own insecurity led him to violate the law and the dignity of his office by authorizing the crimes exposed in the Watergate scandal. Astrologically, the crowning irony of Nixon's life is that so staunch a Capricorn should reach the peak of his career under the one line Saturnians can't handle: NE, which culminates over Washington and occupies the IC in Viet Nam. "Peace with honor" and "I am not a crook," his two most memorable dissimulations, say it all. The People's Republic of China was a different story, however. Over Peking sit SA ASC and UR MH, planets far more congenial to his Capricornian personality. Nixon's friendly goodwill trips opened doors and established ties with that country for the first time since World War II.

## Page 169

### GERALD FORD JULY 14, 1913; 12:43 AM CST OMAHA, NE

<!-- chunk-id: acg-src-lewis-guttman-1989.page-0169.chunk-001 -->
<!-- chunk-sha256: b5ae5570ed699c9892659101681e2e84a206f227be227856077401a73ec4e1e2 -->
Gerald Ford was the 38th President of the U.S., succeeding to that office when Richard Nixon was forced to resign on account of his dishonesty and failing support among the American people. Ford was a pleasant and agreeable enough fellow but neither a very inspiring nor impactful president. His MEIC is closest to Washington, identifying someone who is liked by the masses of "ordinary people" as well as a capacity to wheel and deal. VE ASC also passes near Washington, conferring popular yet inconsequential ideas and appearances. Mercury and Venus seem descriptive enough for his administration—no great enemies, no great accomplishments. While energies on the East Coast seemed agreeable enough, the West Coast was a different story. Not one, 167 but two direct attempts were made on his life on occasions when he visited San Francisco, the place where PL IC sits. By progression, his MA IC line, natally located west of Hawaii, had moved to conjoin his PLin San Francisco during 1975 when travel there was s0 risky for him. One can only conjecture why the violence (MA) and near-death experiences (PL) did not have graver outcomes. In the Rocky Mountains, his JU MH line identifies his favorite recreational area where he mastered gravity on the ski slopes. His SA MH and PL MH command both ends of the Persian Gulf, while his MA DSC passes through Viet Nam, closely paralleling the MA DSC line in the USA Declaration of War chart. It was Gerald Ford who administered the ultimate defeat and withdrawal from that troubled land, a land where the ME that occupies his Washington IC is on the MH and zenith.

## Page 171

### JOHN F. KENNEDY MAY 29,1917; 3:17 PM EST BROOKLINE, MA

<!-- chunk-id: acg-src-lewis-guttman-1989.page-0171.chunk-001 -->
<!-- chunk-sha256: e8840a24c798556fd0cd2f4f1118f28d3314f670a576cf9e5ca566b08f94d809 -->
The 35th President of the U.S., JFK assumed office in

### 1961. Like all presidents since 1840 who took office

<!-- chunk-id: acg-src-lewis-guttman-1989.page-0171.chunk-002 -->
<!-- chunk-sha256: a75fc13390644638a639e87edf34deeba25a66e44844f39c43d47ac58bf64b5d -->
under the Jupiter-Saturn conjunction, he died before completing his term, gunned down by an assassin's bullet in Dallas. JFK's birth map establishes Saturn as the planet closest to his birthplace—SA MH, which is Saturn's most positive and powerful position. Exactly over Dallas, however, is PL MH, and under it his life, and the nation's innocence, were ended at the hands ofa hired gunman; JFK became the national archetype of the martyr. Additionally at the latitude of Dallas, there are four planetary crossings: SU/IC*UR/ASC,

### NE/IC*MA/ASC, UR/IC*MA/DSC and MA/MH*

<!-- chunk-id: acg-src-lewis-guttman-1989.page-0171.chunk-003 -->
<!-- chunk-sha256: fc426126610513320a41a64b223401430f3e7fe666c80f34eb71964f87eddd06 -->
UR/DSC. Strikingly, all contain Uranus and/or Mars— sudden, unexpected violence. JFK's inauguration map shows similar correspondences. At the moment he legally became President, SA MH sat overhead in Dallas with JU MH only a few miles away. 169 VE DSC sits over Berlin where he was better liked than any American president had been previously and where he cemented the alliance between the former adversaries. He was the first president to involve the U.S. directly with Viet Nam, and that locale is actually the midpoint of PLand SA at the IC.MA ASC dominates nearby Taiwan. Cuba lies midway between his PLMH and SA MH lines, the locale of his ill-fated "Bay of Pigs" scenario, and his Saturn zenith lies over the position at sea where the U.S. Navy stood ready for action had Castro not backed down and returned the Soviet nuclear missiles he'd installed. ASU MH over California explains his cinematic appeal and charisma, while VE MH and MO ASC lend credence to his rumored liaison with Marilyn Monroe, America's projected feminine archetype, and apparently his as well. Recently, historian Taylor Branch asserted that J. Edgar Hoover's knowledge of John F. Kennedy's involvement with an East German (VE DSC) woman gave Hoover blackmail power to restrain the President from implementing many of his reformist ideas.

## Page 173

### PIERRE TRUDEAU OCTOBER 18, 1919; 7:03 AM EDT OUTREMONT, QUEBEC, CANADA

<!-- chunk-id: acg-src-lewis-guttman-1989.page-0173.chunk-001 -->
<!-- chunk-sha256: 44a9cfca06f3d78cae979bbc660f313ae1a66f0f4a13da3a31344f1abd9c8198 -->
Canada's urbane, elegant 15th Prime Minister headed the government from 1968 to 1984. He symbolized for Canada the enlightened middle-class liberalism of the 1960s. Trudeau achieved a brilliant academic record while a student at an elite French-Canadian Jesuit prep school in Montreal (SU ASC) but was suspended several times for disciplinary reasons. His political interests awakened and matured while he was attending Harvard University (SU ASC). He later studied in Paris and London (SU MH) where he evidently developed the egalitarian potentials of his Libra Sun. He entered Hungary, Poland and Yugoslavia illegally and was expelled from Yugoslavia (PL DSC). During a period of conflict in Jerusalem (NE DSC), he was mistakenly arrested by the Arabs as an Israeli spy. Attendance at an economic conference in Moscow (PL DSC) resulted in his being placed on McCarthy's "Communist" blacklist. This subsequently caused him to be denied entry to the U.S. and forbidden to hold 171 any teaching positions in Canada during the paranoid and repressive 1950s. By the late 1960s, however, progressive views had come into fashion, and Trudeau became Prime Minister of Canada in 1968. While in office, he worked to reform human rights laws in Canada by liberalizing prohibitions on divorce, abortion, and homosexuality. His own marriage created some scandal since his wife Margaret, born on Pierre's VE ASC, smoked marijuana and was 20 years his junior. Completely bilingual and bicultural himself, perhaps Trudeau's greatest contribution to Canada was his pluralistic view of two harmoniously cooperating cultures working together while still retaining their individual identities. This vision, along with his preference for non-intrusive government, was embodied in the new Canadian Charter, perhaps the most liberal constitution of any Western democracy, which he considered his most important legacy to Canada. In his chart, Jupiter, Neptune and the Moon in the 10th House possibly inspired such broad-minded ideals.

## Page 175

### REZA PAHLAVI, THE SHAH OF IRAN OCTOBER 26, 1919;8:19 AM IranST TEHERAN, IRAN

<!-- chunk-id: acg-src-lewis-guttman-1989.page-0175.chunk-001 -->
<!-- chunk-sha256: 1ebea026d0fc32ffa68c8bb64663cd04ae06b2589351350727b37f9c610772e9 -->
Succeeding his father in 1941 as Shah of Iran, Reza Pahlavi was formally crowned in 1967 with the congratulations and approval of his sponsors, the U.S. government. Born with Saturn conjunct Mars in his Midheaven opposite Uranus on the IC, the Shah exerted great energy to bring his Islamic nation into the 20th century (UR IC), a task not easily accomplished on account of Iranians' inherent suspicion of Western ways. He also strove to preserve the traditional, brutal, absolute power of his father (SA and MA MB). After his early studies with palace tutors, he was sent to Switzerland for finishing school where he excelled scholastically as well as athletically. He later attended French military academy. While his father was sympathetic with Germany during the War, the Allies sought relations with Iran to secure its strategic location and oil reserves. Young Reza Pahlavi, the defacto ruler, set up alliances that artfully balanced the demands of the U.S. (SUIC, NE and JU ASC), the U.K. (PL MH), and the U.S.S.R. (SU ASC, JU MB). After the war, all three countries maneuvered for control of Iran's oil and government leadership, but the U.S. won with an infusion of capital and a CIAengineered coup d'état that toppled the popular secular government of Mohammed Mossadegh, a follower of Gandhi, in 1953. It installed the young Shah on the Peacock Throne and promised to keep him there, pro- 173 viding carte blanche for U.S. arms. The Shah tried to maintain friendly relations with the U.S., the Soviet Union, Israel (JU MH), and the Arab nations (JU and NE MH),a task not easily accomplished. He married Princess Fawzia, sister of King Farouk of Egypt (JU and NE MH in Cairo), but no heir to the throne resulted. He then married Soraya, partly of German ancestry, but remained childless. His third marriage to Farah Dibah of one of Iran's leading families produced an heir. In January 1979, followers of Shi'ite Muslim leader Ayatollah Khomeini along with millions of Iranians, tired of decades of iron-fisted, accelerating SA-MA dictatorship, overcame the formidable Iranian Army and drove the ailing Shah into exile. In his last decade of rule, he had degenerated into a medieval despot whose police terrorism and torturers set the stage for his overthrow. When the U.S. granted him sanctuary, the Ayatollah's angry armies retaliated by seizing the U.S. embassy, an act whose ramifications cost Jimmy Carter the presidency. Significantly, Carter has JU and MA in Iran and SA in Washington, an inversion of the Shah's own placements. (See also Ronald Reagan's and Harry Truman's maps previously in this section.) Pahlavi's last trip was to Egypt (JU and NE MH) where he died in 1980.

## Page 177

### GEORGE BUSH JUNE 12, 1924; 12:00 PM EDT MILTON, MA

<!-- chunk-id: acg-src-lewis-guttman-1989.page-0177.chunk-001 -->
<!-- chunk-sha256: 998c66e525e227ae69df7e44b1a845c84ce7d072444e2274c7768a1de7286499 -->
As a Republican Congressman from Texas, George Bush was a protégé of Richard Nixon, who appointed him ambassador to the U.N. and head of the CIA. He grew up in Greenwich, Connecticut and was schooled in Massachusetts (ME MH, JUIC). He served in the Navy at Corpus Christi, Texas (NE ASC, MA DSC), then in the Pacific where his plane was shot down near Chichi Jima in the Bonin Islands. After four terrifying hours in the water, he was finally rescued by a submarine. Four planets cross these tiny islands—PL IC, MO DSC, VE IC and SA DSC. After the war, he decided to work in the Texas oil industry and became involved in the Texas political scene. His company was quite successful and acquired interests in Latin America, the Caribbean, the Middle East, Japan, Australia and Western Europe. A strong supporter of Nixon in the Viet Nam conflict (MA ASC and JU MH over Southeast Asia), he also encouraged China's entry into the U.N. despite much opposition. The SU IC, JU MH and MA ASC all pass through Peking, so as President, Bush may channel development resources towards markets there. A Gemini Sun, Bush has ME MH dominating the eastern region of the country (not unlike Gerald Ford) with JUIC a strong influence. JU opposite ME suggests an irreconcilable dichotomy between the privileged elite and "ordinary folks," so Bush's years as President may see the continued divergence of social classes. His election 175 may not be good news to our neighbors to the south, either. In keeping with the policy of the Reagan administration, his MA DSC and NE ASC pass through Central America. As former head of the CIA, it can be assumed that Bush has ample experience in using tax dollars to blow up anyone resisting his own brand of control in that region. A birth time of 11:45 would put MA DSC directly through Panama and Manuel Noriega's embarrassing dictatorship. Neptune suggests that despite macho posturing, he has little real comprehension of what is going on there. With Mars, this planet often symbolizes covert war, drugs and scandal. The same MA line passes through Santiago de Chile where the CIA, two years before Bush was appointed its head, engineered the bloody coup that destroyed the democratically elected government and murdered thousands in a downtown sports arena. It also passes through the arena in New Orleans where Bush was nominated and began to overcome his "wimp" image. Every American president of the last four decades has had some important connection to Iran, and Bush's map seems to be no exception. There sit the SU DSC, the MO MH and JU ASC (almost identical to Reagan's influences there). The complete story of Carter, Reagan and Bush's bargaining with the Ayatollah for the hostages in 1980 may never be fully known. As of this writing, the Iran- Iraq War, fueled by arms the U.S. has funneled to Iran since Reagan took office in 1981, seems to have wound down. But the Bush presidency will probably continue some sort of covert support to Iran, most likely through Israel, as payment for unspecified past favors.

## Page 179

### JIMMY CARTER OCTOBER 1, 1924; 7:00 AM CST PLAINS, GA

<!-- chunk-id: acg-src-lewis-guttman-1989.page-0179.chunk-001 -->
<!-- chunk-sha256: c461f92fd44a068b033930b6299561dd139e69d8b384cdf7e5a5a87824bdd5b3 -->
39th President of the U.S., Jimmy Carter, a peanut farmer and ordained minister, was born in Plains, Georgia where no planets are powerful. The closest planet influencing his birthplace is SA ASC, which becomes exactly angular in Washington. There he hada typically difficult time fighting uphill battles, first with an unsupportive Congress, then with a confrontational media. By the time his four-year term ended, he looked as if he had physically aged 25 years, and he was trounced by Ronald Reagan, despite his incumbency. Carter is a Libra Sun, Libra rising, and has Saturn in Scorpio placed in the 1st House. Carter's natal Venus is conjunct Neptune, an aspect that promotes peace and suggests a deep sense of compassion, identifying him asa leader who would avoid confrontation whenever possible. This conjunction falls on the DSC closest over Israel and Egypt, suggesting Carter's forgotten accomplishment—his effecting of the most comfortable relationship these two countries have enjoyed with each other in recent memory. While Mars may be merely assertive and energizing for some people, Venus-ruled Librans might find it WA intolerable. In Teheran, this influence identifies where Carter was forced to assume a macho, assertive posture and where, like so many other presidents, he involved the U.S. in military activity. Also, Carter has JU MH crossing the MA ASC at Teheran, interjecting the element of religion and putting the planet symbolizing victory squarely in the enemy's camp. A SA*PL latitude crossing in Algeria affects Teheran, producing in that exact latitute and longitude for Jimmy Carter a meeting of four powerful warlords—Mars, Jupiter, Saturn and Pluto. The situation called for (and the American public seemed intent on having) something that was difficult for this Saturn-inhibited Libra to produce—a trigger-happy response, like that of LBJ in Viet Nam. Khomeini's frenzied troops sealed Carter's doom at the polls, an ironic reversal and reminder of Harry Truman's map and earlier intervention in the affairs of Iran. Carter's SA MH passes near Paris, where he allegedly tried to bargain freedom for the hostages prior to the 1980 election but was outbid by George Bush, who, on behalf of Reagan, promised arms for the upcoming Iran-Iraq war. In return, the Iranians promised Reagan not to release the hostages until after the inauguration. This MA/JU/SA theme in Iran seems one in which the American presidency is still deeply enmeshed.

## Page 181

### MARGARET THATCHER OCTOBER 13, 1925; 9:00 AM BST GRANTHAM, ENGLAND

<!-- chunk-id: acg-src-lewis-guttman-1989.page-0181.chunk-001 -->
<!-- chunk-sha256: ab41ed16c3093373640809a93d4392e270a46fb89704332eaa5659e4b1b912a1 -->
Margaret Thatcher, the Prime Minister of England known as the "Iron Lady," was born with SA ASC and MO MH directly over Britain. (Joan Crawford, "Mommy Dearest," had the same planetary signature over Hollywood.) Thatcher has become Britain's longestserving Prime Minister in this century. She remains very popular, despite her extremely conservative policies that have dismantled the welfare state, curtailed civil rights, and transferred immense wealth to the entrepreneurial classes. Britons seem to like their figureheads to personify Saturn. (Queen Elizabeth II (g.v.) also has Saturn over Britain.) Thatcher is often criticized as too authoritarian and 179 ruthless and is credited with alienating the working class and unemployed whose lives have deteriorated markedly under her economic policies, policies that have transferred wealth and prosperity upward. Her handling of the 1982 uprising in the Falkland Islands made her a heroine to Britons (MA and the SU ASC, UR DSC) and demonstrates that not only men like to make war under their MA lines. Since the map of the United Kingdom has SU and MA lines at the Falklands, Thatcher demonstrates the principle that individual leaders' maps can activate potentials in national charts, even centuries after their inception. NE MH through Ireland suggests the nemesis of the strong Saturnian—confusion, lack of clear-cut issues, and an invisible enemy—and reminds one of Richard Nixon's (q.v.) fall under his NE MH. Ireland may be her undoing.

## Page 183

### JOHN TURNER JUNE 7, 1929; 2:30 PM BDT RICHMOND, SURREY, ENGLAND

<!-- chunk-id: acg-src-lewis-guttman-1989.page-0183.chunk-001 -->
<!-- chunk-sha256: be94bdb1b0bdc062443c9cc2306fad8bd0624bd5ddda893553689a44b79fb3f3 -->
In June 1984, John Turner succeeded Pierre Trudeau (a hard act to follow) as Canada's 17th Prime Minister, holding that position for one of the shortest times ever in Canadian history. His father was from Britain where a SA IC line suggests identification with tradition, and his mother was born in British Columbia where NE on the IC suggests a more spiritual, idealistic influence. He attended the University of British Columbia where he excelled in sports. While training for the Olympics, he had an automobile accident which prevented his participation in 1948 though he went on to achieve 181 national championships in sprinting. He attended Oxford on a Rhodes scholarship and studied French law at the Sorbonne. In Quebec, he became a member of the Bar. Turner, who heads the Liberal Party, is a political rival of the NDP's Ed Broadbent and Tory Prime Minister Mulroney. He is friendly with the British royal family (SA IC). In 1988, Turner lost another election for Prime Minister against his two rivals at a time when his public leadership of the liberals was challenged by inner dissent and literary exposés unfriendly to him. His candidacy was based on his rejection of a free-trade agreement with the U.S., and at the exact latitude of the U.S./ Canadian border his SU-MO/IC crosses NE/DSC.

### GEORGE MOSCONE NOVEMBER 24, 1929; 4:00 AM PST SAN FRANCISCO, CA

<!-- chunk-id: acg-src-lewis-guttman-1989.page-0185.chunk-001 -->
<!-- chunk-sha256: 1bd3f5dd30ccbac53aed69dedcc35167760fb9efd27cd597354b75e5f292308e -->
Before winning the mayoralty of San Francisco the same year Harvey Milk and Dan White were elected to seats on San Francisco's Board of Supervisors, George Moscone had been a very powerful California State legislator and would probably have succeeded Jerry Brown as governor. Assassinated along with Harvey Milk by Dan White (see both maps elsewhere in this section), Moscone's death dealt a powerful blow to 183 Democratic Party cohesion in California, particularly since Leo Ryan, another powerful leader, was killed the same month at Jonestown. As the birth time is a round hour, the PL MH may have actually been much closer to San Francisco, similar to other political assassinations (see Martin Luther King, Jr. and John F. Kennedy maps elsewhere in this text). At the time of his assassination, transiting Pluto formed a close, 90-degree aspect to Moscone's natal Pluto, and this aspect was exactly paran at San Francisco's latitude.

## Page 187

### BOB HAWKE DECEMBER 9, 1929; 3:30 AM CST BORDERTOWN, AUSTRALIA

<!-- chunk-id: acg-src-lewis-guttman-1989.page-0187.chunk-001 -->
<!-- chunk-sha256: ebf131bbd0f0595fd0b3673f8a7ab34a774bbce5f8ccd3e9f8d344ebe8285ab5 -->
Bob Hawke was a labor union leader who became Prime Minister of Australia in March 1983. He appeals to both the "ordinary" folk of Australia and to business interests with his pragmatic idealism. His interest in labor unions and civil servants' and workers' rights have made him highly popular with these groups, and he has fought against Australian racism and xenophobia, opening trade and investment opportunities to the world economy. When he was 10, Hawke's family moved to Western Australia (Perth) where his father was a minister. In 1952, he left Australia to study at Oxford (MO MH, PL ASC) asa Rhodes scholar. He became an agnostic as a result of a trip to southern India as the Australian delegate to the World Congress on Christian Youth

### (VE IC, JU MH, NE ASC, MO DSC).

<!-- chunk-id: acg-src-lewis-guttman-1989.page-0187.chunk-002 -->
<!-- chunk-sha256: 1aa9f2d3d1ce8a61cc19d46d869923f6f760f90cc94728c26fe2551ee788ea9c -->
185 While his relationship with Reagan was officially friendly, he was one of the most outspoken of his country's leaders against Australia's habit of blindly following the U.S. His SA MH passes through Washington, and his PL MH lies over a top-secret, feared and resented nuclear base in Western Australia. He is also vehemently opposed to South Africa's apartheid policy (SA DSC in Johannesburg) and is against the French nuclear tests in the Pacific (PL ASC through Paris). He was quite fond of Golda Meir and has been a staunch supporter of Israel. In Canberra and Australia's population centers, Hawke has VE ASC. His early image of a drinking, womanizing gambler has been moderated by his business acumen and charming, irreverent charisma. His leadership has consciously advanced a more positive image for Australia, both at home and abroad, and national selfesteem has risen exponentially under his influence.

## Page 189

### HARVEY MILK MAY 22, 1930; 1:30 AM EDT WOODMERE, NY

<!-- chunk-id: acg-src-lewis-guttman-1989.page-0189.chunk-001 -->
<!-- chunk-sha256: 54a8f32c3470ba8439a97c5f10ba1e19336d049255978156ec931969ebc06333 -->
Harvey Milk was the first openly gay person to be elected to public office in the United States, serving as City Supervisor in San Francisco. He grew up in New York on his NE DSC line. His life there was undistinguished, save for his reportedly being discharged from the Navy for being gay. He then moved to San Francisco, situated on his MA IC line with UR IC and JU DSC nearby. There, on the sexual and self-assertive MA line, he became an activist, and after three tries running for City Supervisor, he finally won despite everyone's belief it couldn't be done. During his short term of office before being assassinated, he was able to change the method by which City Supervisors were elected to one in which they ran in 187 districts rather than city-wide; this gave more power to the neighborhoods. He championed rights of minorities, tenants, the elderly and gays, making deadly enemies of almost all conservative and monied forces. He formed a strong coalition with Mayor George Moscone (q.v.), who represented a more conservative, mainstream Democratic constituency, and with Congressman Leo Ryan from San Francisco. All three of these men were killed within a month of one another. Milk's assassination is explored further in the map of his assassin, Dan White, elsewhere in this book. He foresaw his own assassination, leaving several taped messages with friends that clearly stated his opposition to the appointment of a conservative to fill his vacated seat. The liberal activist who succeeded Milk, Harry Britt, has continued his populist commitment through three terms on the Board.

## Page 191

### EDWARD M. KENNEDY FEBRUARY 22, 1932; 3:58 AM EST DORCHESTER, MA

<!-- chunk-id: acg-src-lewis-guttman-1989.page-0191.chunk-001 -->
<!-- chunk-sha256: b731aa2ee031e9e4186e3a8996f50c8f1293dae3678b4578d4cbff9c9d9abfd0 -->
Sole survivor of the four Kennedy brothers who were to be so intimately involved with the American political scene of the 20th century, Edward (Teddy) has served as Democratic Senator from Massachusetts forever. He grew up in Boston but spent his childhood floating between Boston, New York, London and Palm Beach, Florida. He honorably served in France and Germany during World War II (PLIC) and studied international law at The Hague. Later, he spent time in North Africa as a reporter. After studying law, he was admitted to the Massachusetts Bar in 1959. He almost died in a 189 Massachusetts plane crash. Backing a staunchly liberal political forum, he strongly but unsuccessfully opposed the U.S. involvement in Viet Nam, which lies under his SA DSC line. He has always supported Northern Ireland where both VE and UR rise. He condemned Pakistan for genocide in Bangla Desh (UR MH). The incident at Chappaquiddick has seemingly permanently soured his chances of ever becoming President. The East Coast of the U.S. where Senator Kennedy has led his life is singularly bereft of lines, suggesting perhaps inaccurate birth data or offering comment on his seeming incapacity to follow in the footsteps of his illustrious brothers.

## Page 193

### MARIO CUOMO JUNE 15, 1932; 9:00 AM EST JAMAICA, NY

<!-- chunk-id: acg-src-lewis-guttman-1989.page-0193.chunk-001 -->
<!-- chunk-sha256: 7a1469e007474652a43a7f1d38a1821f5a2c9f95c9bbf42a7e2ad42a1ba7de0c -->
The Democratic Governor of New York, Mario Cuomo has emerged as an important spokesman and national figure in the Democratic Party since he took his oath of office on January 1, 1983. Despite his protestations, he was thought to be a major contender in 1988's presidential election. Wise man that he is, he let the other contenders fight without him, but he may be heard from in 1992. No wonder he's well liked—over New York and influencing Washington sit JU ASC and the MO IC, a personally and politically satisfying combination. The MO IC reinforces the strong connections he feels to his home, family and ethnic origins, grants popular appeal, and keeps him at the center of attention. JU ASC personally elevates his image to one of unchallengeable authority, generosity and confidence. It can also 191 have religious and philosophical implications. Before he entered politics, Mario Cuomo was a minor league player in that most Jupiterian sport, baseball— first for Pittsburgh, then in Georgia. There, after an accident hospitalized him under his SA DSC, he quit baseball. While a popular political idol in his home state, he may not always be viewed that way elsewhere. In Italy (his family's origins) lies lunar antagonist SA IC, symbolizing a past he, or perhaps his family, has buried but one that could surely be resurrected by his political enemies at some future time. In 1992, he might well be running against the former head of the CIA. SA also shows the achievement of transcending his immigrant heritage. Itis also angular in Central America, perhaps suggesting insoluble problems. NEMH and NEDSC straddle Moscow and Tokyo, respectively, and warn that lack of experience in foreign policy might make him easily misled in either place.

## Page 195

### RICHARD SECORD JULY 6, 1932; 3:00 AM EST LA RUE, OH

<!-- chunk-id: acg-src-lewis-guttman-1989.page-0195.chunk-001 -->
<!-- chunk-sha256: 6bce6ade3f93f262ab2119459de8ec863184194123587a149b56c9a7a3838b5a -->
Along with Lt. Col. Oliver North and Admiral John Poindexter, Richard Secord was one of the chief architects of the Iran-Contra connection that created the scandal in 1986-87 (see Oliver North's map elsewhere in this text). Interestingly enough, he has VE MH placed over Teheran where he insisted he sought to develop relationships with "moderate elements," while the MOIC is directly over Nicaragua, a place of sticky involvement and reactivity. This is the exact reverse of Oliver North, who has the MO MH over Iran and VE MH over Nicaragua. 193 They both bamboozled the American public, sidled through purposefully limited public hearings, and survived having their personal lives scrutinized through a microscope on national TV. They came out smelling like Venusian roses when, perhaps, they might better have been locked up. JU IC over Washington insulated Secord from harm and hints at very influential friends at the highest levels of government. Nevertheless, his trial (upcoming as of this writing) will continue to focus negative attention on the Reagan-Bush administration.

## Page 197

### MICHAEL DUKAKIS NOVEMBER 3, 1933; 6:00 PM EST BROOKLINE, MA

<!-- chunk-id: acg-src-lewis-guttman-1989.page-0197.chunk-001 -->
<!-- chunk-sha256: e485c112a990c2b24571553779ad9881bcc498f8d2a2c53a3378b6f78d9bf1f6 -->
It seems no coincidence that in the same year that the Soviet Union initiated its most radical transformation since its revolution, Pluto transits its natal Sun in Scorpio. And there arose in the United States a presidential candidate whose Sun also occupies the middle degrees of the sign of birth, death and regeneration. From early on in the 1988 campaign, Dukakis drew parallels between himself and John F. Kennedy. Born

### a few miles apart, their maps share one major similarity

<!-- chunk-id: acg-src-lewis-guttman-1989.page-0197.chunk-002 -->
<!-- chunk-sha256: 6cd4800ee3e58dd15025642414c80910136a6037ad74962e266f4096ec7bf390 -->
Saturn is in the Midheaven in almost exactly the same place, a longitude that includes New York, Washington and Havana. Moreover, with his Sun squared Saturn (which he shares with the U.S. birth chart), should he ever become President it would likely be during a time when the polarization of age and youth and rich and poor are extreme, perhaps as results of economic or other circumstantial limitations. Presidents often make war under their Mars lines; if this is to be the case here, Dukakis would have only one iheds) 31939 major trouble area in the world: Iran. His Mars lines pass through such unlikely places as Canada, Australia and Brazil, but the IC is close to Teheran, suggesting a Dukakis presidency would continue the strange "fatal attraction" U.S. presidents seem to have for the area. Jimmy Carter's map (q.v.) is especially similar. MEDSC and the MO ASC on the horizon at Washington indicate that a Dukakis administration would be more open and perhaps more populist. His ethnic roots in Greece lie under the MO MH line, showing a strong attachment to his family's mother country. The MO DSC and ME ASC in Japan indicate warm relations, but the NE MH warns that as President he could be taken in by seeming cooperation and not look deeply enough for hidden interests in negotiations with the financial giant of Asia. NE ASC also runs through the oil-rich Middle East, perhaps clouding his otherwise eagle-eyed Saturnian vision in dealings there as well. UR MH and SA DSC in England suggest that were he President difficulties in getting along with conservatives there would work to change important military and economic alliances. Meanwhile, his home state of Massachusetts will still benefit from his careful management and Saturnian restraint.

## Page 199

### EDWARD BROADBENT MARCH 21, 1936; 8:00 AM EST OSHAWA, ONTARIO, CANADA

<!-- chunk-id: acg-src-lewis-guttman-1989.page-0199.chunk-001 -->
<!-- chunk-sha256: d09f603c3286a0c9b1e4e6f0d1ab968cd04a598769228fd70ff511068b06e65c -->
The leader of Canada's New Democratic Party, Ed Broadbent is considered by many in Canada to be not only a "nice guy" but an effective leader as well. Canada, unlike the U.S., has three major parties. The NDP is the most liberal and receives 80% of its financial support from individuals instead of from the usual corporate/ institutional axis. With PL on the IC exactly at Ottawa along with UR on the ASC in Ontario, this man seems destined to be in the defense of individual rights in an increasingly institutional society. This planetary signature speaks of intense political and social activism, a strong stand on populist issues such as nuclear power, military spending, and human rights, and some very challenging, progressive opinions and attitudes on EME Canada's fundamentally conservative government structures. With the same PL and UR lines passing through Washington, D.C., we might expect some outright challenges to the U.S. government's domination of Canadian policies, especially in regard to the nuclear issue, the deforestation of Canada, acid rain and trade concerns. He has powerful lines in other English-speaking countries around the world, too: his PL ASC passes through South Africa, suggesting a militant attitude against the government, while PL DSC through New Zealand identifies some threats to smooth relations there. VE and the MO IC and JU ASC lines in Australia's population centers suggest a warmer reception in that nation's more working-class, labor political climate. His progressed SU ASC line crossed his PLIC in Ottawa in 1987 and saw a new surge of interest in the NDP.

## Page 201

### JERRY BROWN APRIL 7, 1938; 12:34 PM PST SAN FRANCISCO, CA

<!-- chunk-id: acg-src-lewis-guttman-1989.page-0201.chunk-001 -->
<!-- chunk-sha256: f373cb11aecebafa0ee8ff3cd220f0e503a5767110a49f41a3a372bb556f671d -->
Democratic Governor of California from 1975-81, Jerry Brown's love-in with the California electorate was ended by aconcerted media assault on his personality when he ran for the Senate in 1982 and lost. In his youth, his ambition was the priesthood rather than politics. Accordingly, he entered the Jesuit novitiate in Los Gatos, California where he underwent highly disciplined training, which included prolonged periods of silence, meditation and philosophical study. After deciding to re-enter the world, he attended law school at Yale and became interested in the civil rights movement in the South. He did much legal defense work in Mississippi (JU DSC) until that state's governor asked his father (then Governor of California) to order Jerry back home before he got into serious trouble. He traveled in Mexico and Central and South America. In 199 his Los Angeles law firm, he worked on the "California- Chile" Program (JU DSC). Asa leader in the liberal wing of the Democratic Party, he adamantly opposed the U.S. involvement in Viet Nam (JU ASC). He also supported Caesar Chavez' work and marched with him. His SU-SA (ASC) conjunction is close over Japan where he has pursued his religious interests which have focused on Zen Buddhism. Brown recently traveled there and spent five months under rigid discipline ina Zen Buddhist monastery and ten days in Calcutta (JU ASC, MA IC) with Mother Teresa, donating time at her mission. He expressed an opinion that world leaders would do well to follow her example. Since no lines occupy Washington, it will be hard for him to shake the "flakey" image imposed there on all things Californian. Moreover, since NE ASC is closest to the District of Columbia, his political base may be realistically limited to the West.

## Page 203

### BRIAN MULRONEY MARCH 20, 1939; 7:47 AM EST BAIE-COMEAU, QUEBEC, CANADA

<!-- chunk-id: acg-src-lewis-guttman-1989.page-0203.chunk-001 -->
<!-- chunk-sha256: 5cc0ce6880dd2d5ddc4766e8fd30cc8b25496c11d5e71617b37710e9f73ea3a4 -->
Brian Mulroney became the 18th Prime Minister of Canada in 1984, having risen quickly from the business sector. He is the first conservative leader to come from Quebec in almost a century, succeeding John Turner, a liberal, whose tenure was one of the briefest in Canadian history. Mulroney, of Irish descent (SU MH), hasa particularly strong focus on foreign affairs and has advocated a controversial free-trade agreement with the U.S. (UR ASC is nearest Washington) which some Canadians fear will result in their being overwhelmed by cheap American products and culture. He is a staunch supporter of NATO and the U.S. and chided the Trudeau 201 government for sniping at Washington. He hosted Ronald Reagan in Ottawa and supported Reagan's Grenada (UR ASC) campaign as well as placement of cruise missiles in Canada. Well-spoken and bilingual, he has proven himself a consummate politician, working to unite the bickering provinces in the Meech Lake Accord. He is a critic of the Soviets (UR MH) anda champion of Israel (UR MH, VE DSC). He met his wife in Montreal, her birthplace (UR ASC). In 1988, he prevailed in an election against his two liberal rivals, and in so doing assured closer trading relations between his nation and the U.S. With Saturn, Uranus and Neptune firmly in Capricorn at this time, the three leading English-speaking nations have aligned themselves firmly in the conservative camp. Only Australia remains liberal.

<a id="acg-src-lewis-guttman-1989.page-0204"></a>
<!-- page-id: acg-src-lewis-guttman-1989.page-0204 -->

### PL DSC JESSE JACKSON

<a id="acg-src-lewis-guttman-1989.page-0205"></a>

## Page 205

### JESSE JACKSON OCTOBER 8, 1941; 3:30 PM EST GREENVILLE, SC

<!-- chunk-id: acg-src-lewis-guttman-1989.page-0205.chunk-001 -->
<!-- chunk-sha256: 9fe81916d5f0c71906e9f873cb4983cf63932f17a386d9705ceef0721e171073 -->
"What does Jesse want?" The all-white, all-rich, all- WASP pundits of the political parties and media kept asking one another this as the upstart preacher gathered delegates, votes, momentum and political power. Any astrologer could have foretold that the Saturn-Uranus conjunction of 1988 at the solstice point of power and establishment was going to produce some surprises, so no one should have been so astonished. By the time Jesse got to Atlanta (where Martin Luther King, Jr. lies buried, but not at all dead), he had amassed enough power so that what he wanted was no longer in doubt—under his SA-UR IC and close to his PL DSC, he wanted to change the way the country is run. To move the lines closer to Atlanta, a rectification to some 20 minutes earlier might be appropriate. This would also put his SA ASC, UR ASC, VE DSC, and MO ASC lines in South Africa where he has steadfastly opposed the apartheid regime (see Gandhi's map elsewhere in this book). The same rectification would move the NE IC line to Moscow and to Lebanon, where Jackson achieved 203 notoriety bargaining for hostages, succeeding where official representatives of the U.S. government had failed. JU ASC would pass through London and Paris, while the SUIC and MA MH in Iran suggest confrontation. But it is in the American South and in Central America that Jackson's five planet lines are apt to have the most effect. Chicago, with the given birth time, finds the SA, UR, VE lines angular. As rectified, the MO IC becomes closest, defining a home base and family interests. In New York, where an anti-Jewish epithet resulted in considerable embarrassment, MA*PL and PL*UR crossings insure continued controversy. Jackson's chart forms intensely powerful aspects with the USA July 4th nativity—his Sun conjoins the USA's Saturn, and his Uranus-Saturn conjunction with his opposing Venus all square the USA's Moon. He shares a late Virgo Neptune and is able to hook into populist ideals, national sympathy and point out our collective "shadow" —the parts of American social life we would rather not be forced to view. His influence may well see a shift of power from the Eastern establishment and California technocrats to the New South—and to our neighbors to the south.

## Page 207

### OLIVER NORTH OCTOBER 7, 1943; 10:14 AM CWT SAN ANTONIO, TX

<!-- chunk-id: acg-src-lewis-guttman-1989.page-0207.chunk-001 -->
<!-- chunk-sha256: a12429876a90fb4990146ad8a666aa4004d7b48c4f48136d982f8a015cd02b67 -->
Lt. Col. Oliver North became a sort-of national antihero in the waning days of the Reagan administration, proudly admitting he betrayed the trust of the American public and broke its laws. During the 1986-87 Iran- Contra Affair, he secretly sold arms to "terrorist" Iran and then diverted the profits to Reagan's Contra terrorists in Nicaragua, a group for whom he demonstrated an almost maternal concern when confronted with his actions during televised hearings. He inspired affection and intense loyalty from his beneficiaries, while no one mourned or remembered the 15,000 Nicaraguans (mostly noncombatants) over whose deaths he presided. Looking at Iran, we find his 205 MO MH, while in Nicaragua he has VE MH. Since he certainly didn't promote peace or populism in either place, we are left with psychological conjectures about the American macho male's relationship to and fear of his feminine aspect and are reminded that "Hell hath no fury as a woman scorned." Americans saw him as the good guy and sympathized with him. After all, he was just doing his job. The Moon and Venus will charm the judge and the whole Congress when backed up by popular appeal. (See also Richard Secord's map elsewhere in this text, as well as those of Jane Fonda and Daniel and Philip Berrigan for other examples of MO lines identifying places where intense sympathies motivate defiant personal action.) North's trial in 1989 did produce convictions on several grave charges, but the murderous policies he implemented remained unquestioned.

## Page 209

### DAN WHITE SEPTEMBER 2, 1946; 8:13 AM PST BELLFLOWER, CA

<!-- chunk-id: acg-src-lewis-guttman-1989.page-0209.chunk-001 -->
<!-- chunk-sha256: ae962febd088ce7c7bc6da88688f4d9b3be0efd5b54f12e3b5bcba12ab0d70bf -->
The assassin of Harvey Milk and George Moscone began his life in a Los Angeles suburb, within a degree of his MA ASC. Fittingly, he exemplified the American male archetype though the proximity of NE ASC hinted at lack of a true self behind the macho posturing. He excelled at football and went on to a successful career in firefighting. After moving to San Francisco, he ran for City Board of Supervisors, backed by police and conservative forces, and won a seat the same year as Harvey Milk. Now firmly under his NE ASC line, things went badly, 207 and he was quickly overshadowed by his gay rival on the Board. It will be remembered that Harvey Milk (q.v.) had moved from his NE to his MA, the reverse of Dan White. Envy and frustrated homophobia led to White's double assassination of Milk and Mayor Moscone in November 1978 at the exact moment of a Mars-Neptune conjunction. In atrial that nearly everyone considered to have been rigged, White was sentenced to only three years for the double murder, despite a California death penalty statute that militated the gas chamber for political assassinations. A short time after his release, White took his own life in a manner appropriate to Mars and Neptune: carbon monoxide poisoning in his automobile.

## Page 212

### GENERAL GEORGE PATTON NOVEMBER 11, 1885; 6:38 PM PST SAN MARINO, CA

<!-- chunk-id: acg-src-lewis-guttman-1989.page-0212.chunk-001 -->
<!-- chunk-sha256: 4ecbd52af104796e62699a4f60be1a6ac119e3c92f5fc51dd9dd76d58248ab20 -->
George Patton was born on a ranch in California under his MA IC and PL ASC lines, a fitting combination for a military strategist. He attended school in Pasadena and the Virginia Military Academy (MO DSC). Patton was an accomplished athlete who took part in the 1912 Olympics in Stockholm (MO IC and six planet crossings). He served in the Cavalry all over the U.S.—Illinois (JU IC), Virginia (MO DSC), Texas (SA ASC, VE DSC), Kansas (SA ASC) and California (MA IC)—before journeying to France to learn French sabre methods. Under Gen. Pershing, he headed the World War I Tank Corps in 1917. Wounded there, he was awarded a Distinguished Service Cross (JU ASC, VE IC). After the war, Patton returned to the U.S., commanding posts in Maryland, Hawaii (MO MH), Georgia (UR IC) and California (MA IC). In Ft. Benning, Georgia, he gained the reputation of developing the toughest, most-feared outfit of the whole Army (MO DSC and UR IC). In Indio, California, he trained his troops in the desert under tougher conditions than existed in 211 North Africa to prepare them for that campaign and was subsequently placed in charge of the North African invasion by Gen. Eisenhower. There he commanded successful campaigns in Morocco (JU ASC), Tunisia (VE, SA and UR) and Sicily (SA, the MO). He was both loved and feared by his troops, being very tough on them and yet inspiring affectionate loyalty. He was nevertheless almost court-martialed for his harsh treatment of wounded soldiers in Sicily. It is said that he experienced déja vu on a battlefield in Italy (MO IC, SA MH), having remembered it from a Roman soldier incarnation. His marriage to Beatrice Aver of Boston produced three children, all of whom pursued careers in the Army. One is struck by the numerous MO locations at which Patton's life took important turns. As both his feminine planets, the Moon and Venus, are opposite natal Saturn and square Uranus in his chart, a psychological interpretation comes to mind as well as the gruff, comradely caring he always shared with his troops.

## Page 214

### AMELIA EARHART JULY 24, 1897; 11:30 PM CST ATCHISON, KS

<!-- chunk-id: acg-src-lewis-guttman-1989.page-0214.chunk-001 -->
<!-- chunk-sha256: 76b779cf0eccbdde89790ab0807229cb2d8a749cf5a53b10a5707c39708791ec -->
Amelia Earhart, possessed to some degree by the archetype of the puer aeternus (Peter Pan), delighted in being airborne where she could experience a feeling of freedom and detachment usually denied to the earthbound women of her generation. Born in 1897 in Atchison, Kansas of German heritage, Amelia spent most of her early life in the Midwest. As a young adult, she made a trip to California near her exact MA-JU conjunction's DSC line and was introduced to flying; she immediately signed up for lessons. Like Lindbergh and Cousteau, it is difficult to associate her with specific localities since she's been everywhere. In 1928, aviation history was made when Amelia Earhart became the first female to cross the Atlantic from Newfoundland to Wales. In 1932, she again made aviation history as the first woman to fly the Atlantic solo from Newfoundland to Ireland, and she set a time record at that. In 1935, she flew her first solo flight from Hawaii (SA and UR MH, ME DSC) to the mainland and also flew nonstop from New Jersey to Mexico City. On July 2, 1937, Amelia and her crew set out on a 213 2,556-mile trip from Lae, New Guinea (six planets traverse this small island country) to Howland Island in the mid-Pacific. There, on a two-mile long by onemile wide island only 15 feet above the sea, she was to land and refuel at the U.S. Coast Guard landing strip. Weak radio messages were received by the Coast Guard that fuel was low with no landing in sight. The last message was received at 8:45 AM on July 2, and despite numerous government and privately funded searches, no trace of the aircraft or its passengers has ever been found. In 1959, speculations surfaced that the plane hit Saipan in the Marianas (due north of Lae) and that the crew had been executed by the Japanese. A memorial to her remains on Howland Island. The Cyclo*Carto*Graphy map for this time period is full of activity in the area. Transiting PL DSC crossed the Equator precisely at Howland Island near the sites of recent searches. In addition, at the time she took off, her progressed Mercury, progressed Mars and transiting Saturn were in conjunction, the zeniths of which were all on the Equator. Additionally, her progressed Sun had just contacted her exact natal Mars-Jupiter conjunction over New Guinea, suggesting the profound activation of the risk-prone and adventuresome potentials of that combination.

## Page 216

### GILES G. HEALEY MARCH 23, 1901; 7:45 AM EST NEW YORK, NY

<!-- chunk-id: acg-src-lewis-guttman-1989.page-0216.chunk-001 -->
<!-- chunk-sha256: 0cee52094f490d0ff480cf03e17bba75be35f9b3cfbf207b12f12e86933742f7 -->
In 1946, Giles Healey was making a movie in Mexico for the United Fruit Company. The film centered on the Lacandon Indians, their way of life, and the ancient Mayan ruins in their Yucatan environment. In his background research work with the Lacandons, Healy befriended several individuals. Earning their confidence through his interest in their language and customs, he heard rumors of an ancient city in the nearby unexplored forest. His friends eventually led him through the 215 swampy jungle to a place where, under centuries of foliage, he discovered the Mayan city of Bonampak. Bonampak was a group of temples, one of which contained almost perfectly preserved frescoes depicting in considerable detail scenes of Maya life. This was one of the most extraordinary finds in archeology as the murals, in addition to their artistic value, constitute an important history of the Maya, a history that had otherwise been obliterated by the Conquest. Directly over Bonampak, whose discovery made Giles Healy renowned for life, lies his JU MH with crossings of the SU/MH»JU/DSC and VE/IC*UR/ASC.

## Page 218

### CHARLES LINDBERGH FEBRUARY 4, 1902; 2:30 AM CST DETROIT, MI

<!-- chunk-id: acg-src-lewis-guttman-1989.page-0218.chunk-001 -->
<!-- chunk-sha256: 2e33d1f872313ccba084e3bd3332cfddbfa64d52ed78fe8040d39ae60ab28151 -->
The only child of a Swedish father and English, Irish, and French mother, Charles Lindbergh made aviation history in 1927 by completing the first solo transatlantic flight. Lindbergh was so fascinated by aviation that he quit school early to attend flight school in Lincoln, Nebraska. His first job in aviation was as an airmail flyer from St. Louis to Chicago. He met his wife, Anne Morrow, while on a goodwill mission to Mexico City (at Neptune's zenith latitude) where she lived with her father, the U.S. Ambassador. Lindbergh was instrumental in bringing aeronautics into the 20th-century mainstream in Latin America. He also pioneered the Alaskan route to Asia and the Labrador-Iceland route to Europe, which is still being used today and which follows his UR ASC closely. Like Jacques Cousteau, he is difficult to pin down to specific places since he spent much of his life flying all 217 over the world. However, New York and Paris stand out as the beginning and end of his historic transatlantic flight. UR occupies the ASC in New York and the MH in Paris. In Paris, Lindbergh also has the MO MH angular along with four planetary crossings at Paris' latitude: VE/MH is crossed by PL/ASC, the MO/DSC, and NE/ASC. He arrived there and received adulation of the entire French nation. Lindbergh's map shows PL DSC and UR ASC dominating the eastern part of the U.S. where in 1932 he suffered the tragic kidnapping and murder of his first child for which Bruno Hauptmann (q.v.) was later prosecuted. Hauptmann's birthplace was in Germany where Lindbergh's NE IC passes. Lindbergh's involvement with and affection for the German aviation techniques and pilots along with his opposition to U.S. involvement in World War II resulted in his being blacklisted in Washington and forbidden to participate in active duty or be employed by any government-contracted aeronautics firms during the war. After the war, he continued to promote air travel throughout the world. He died in 1974.

## Page 220

### JACQUES COUSTEAU JUNE 11, 1910; 1:15 PM LMT ST. ANDRE-DE-CUBZAC, FRANCE

<!-- chunk-id: acg-src-lewis-guttman-1989.page-0220.chunk-001 -->
<!-- chunk-sha256: 0eb6af0fc59bf95825def3814ef3ba9bdcda9b038e99286ffd26991db3776a7b -->
Born with a 9th House Gemini Sun and with Jupiter on the Ascendant, Jacques Cousteau began traveling at a very early age. He has been all over the world and has sailed on and explored all of the earth's oceans. His love affair with the sea began with his training in the French Navy in Shanghai during which he cruised all along the Indochina coast under his NE DSC, the first of many meetings he was to have with the ancient god of the sea. With MA DSC and UR ASC nearby, both of his arms were broken there in an automobile accident. Cousteau began his diving career at Toulon on the Mediterranean coast, doing spy work for Italy during the war under his NE MH. In June 1943, he developed the first aqualung. 219 Cousteau loved the sea. His early expeditions uncovered art treasures froma sunken Roman vessel off the Tunisian coast (NE MR). He has created a corporation of 16 worldwide research organizations devoted to all aspects of oceanography. The parent company, headquartered in Santa Ana, California under his SU and PLASC, funds its endeavors through the retail sales of diving equipment. He has written books, made films, and done extensive TV work, distinguishing himself as the world's most famous oceanographer. A factfinding expedition to the Antarctic contributed much previously unknown information. The Cousteau Society in Bridgeport, Connecticut (on his MO ASC) is an ecology-minded group devoted to the education and conservation of the world's sea life. He has homes in both Paris and Monaco but spends more and more time aboard the Calypso.

## Page 222

### WILLIE MAYS MAY 6, 1931; 10:30 PM CST WESTFIELD, AL

<!-- chunk-id: acg-src-lewis-guttman-1989.page-0222.chunk-001 -->
<!-- chunk-sha256: 2ea92f332c02e640ee8ae8834de1b4bdcf265f1cd6b054e2d79567cca23378c3 -->
Born in Alabama, Willie Mays had six planets appearing on angles over New York City. Although he began his career in the segregated Negro National League, he went on to an unprecedented career in New York as an outfielder and devastating hitter for the Giants. He broke so many baseball records that he was named Player of the Decade for the '60s. His fans in New York loved him so much that they would applaud him vigorously every time he came to bat. When the New York Giants moved to San Francisco, Mays went with them. Eventually, he was traded to the New York Mets, and while it was a whole new team, it 221 4725 154 was in the city that he belonged. His fans welcomed him back to New York with standing ovations, and though he was older and no longer able to break records, he still led the Mets to a National League Championship in 1973, his last year in baseball. He was one of the first black major league baseball players, and his map is reminiscent of that of Leontyne Price (see map elsewhere in this text) who has a similiar set of planets over New York City. His angular planets there are: JU and PL DSC, the MO and SA ASC, and ME and the SU IC, among which Jupiter most closely affects New York. The MO-SA conjunction is closely angular there, two planets frequently in evidence where prejudice must be overcome through enduring, persevering excellence.

## Page 224

### MUHAMMAD ALI JANUARY 17, 1942; 6:35 PM CST LOUISVILLE, KY

<!-- chunk-id: acg-src-lewis-guttman-1989.page-0224.chunk-001 -->
<!-- chunk-sha256: c4b45d9937cb5fc146a6b3893ef0f92805f5c8a041d40af193f2a465c10c91b9 -->
Born Cassius Clay, Muhammad Ali was an Olympic gold medalist and the first boxer ever to win the World Heavyweight Championship three times. In a time of growing black pride, he used his success as a platform for his political and religious convictions—even when they interfered with his athletic career. After he converted to Islam, he refused to serve inthe Army in Viet Nam, a decision that forced him into three and a half years of professional exile between 1967-70. A federal court in Houston (MA MH) found Ali guilty of violation of the Selective Service Act, sentenced him to five years in jail, and fined him $10,000. He has fought all over the world, both winning and 229 losing, mostly winning. His Madison Square Garden victories occurred under SA MH, a planet often prominent in charts of those discriminated against who make good. On New York's latitude, there occur crossings of MA*MO, JU*NE and SU*SA as well. His Olympic title in Rome (1960) combined MA DSC and MO, ME and VEIC. His victory over George Foreman in Kinshasa, Zaire was a return to SA, this time the DSC, at the latitude of crossings of the SU/IC*MA/ DSC and MA/IC*SU/ASC. He's surprised many by winning matches when many thought he was too old to fight or no match for the opponent. He had training camps at Deer Lake, Pennsylvania and Berrien Springs, Michigan and lives in Chicago where he oversaw business enterprises in Chicago and Cleveland, all under his VE DSC line. He has conferred with heads of state in the Soviet Union, Africa and Europe.
