## Page 226

### BILLIE JEAN KING NOVEMBER 22, 1943; 11:45 AM PWT LONG BEACH, CA

<!-- chunk-id: acg-src-lewis-guttman-1989.page-0226.chunk-001 -->
<!-- chunk-sha256: 5c8356a3981e0ec75bff4d11bb0f9fbfa3f3338677a7c30c267cd88011c7a59b -->
The first woman athlete to win $100,000 in one year, Billie Jean King succeeded in obtaining equal prize money for women tennis players at the 1973 U.S. Open. A feminist and lesbian, King shocked the sports world by openly declaring her sexual preference. In 1973, she was challenged by Bobby Riggs, then senior male tennis champion, whose comment, "A woman belongs in two places—the bedroom and the kitchen— in that order," qualified him as leading contender for world-class male chauvinist. They played out the ageless battle of the sexes on the tennis court in Houston where five planets are angular for King: (ME MH, UR IC, JU DSC, PL DSC, SU MH). With back-up like that, papas) needless to say, she won. Since then, she has often made national headlines. She became the victim of a media circus when she was legally blackmailed by an East Coast ex-girlfriend in 1981 (SA IC). As holder of the all-time record of 20 victories at Wimbledon (JU IC) with crossings of MA*PL, MA+*JU, PL*MA and NE*UR, King has also won four U.S. singles titles in Forest Hills, New York (SA IC, crossings of ME*PL, PL*ME, UR*PL). She has won one Australian title (PL MH at the latitude of NE*MA, JU*MA) and one in France (four crossings at Paris' latitude: MO*ME, MO*UR, VE*PL and NE*ME). She has become a leading voice in encouraging girls' participation in sports in the schools.

## Page 228

### ARNOLD SCHWARZENEGGER JULY 30, 1947; 4:10 AM MEDT GRAZ, AUSTRIA

<!-- chunk-id: acg-src-lewis-guttman-1989.page-0228.chunk-001 -->
<!-- chunk-sha256: 872947ec05ab94c14af393aae8599e7f6557e5f5c4759487091039941e9a85d3 -->
Arnold Schwarzenegger will probably go down in sports history as the world's greatest bodybuilder. He has won every possible title, including "Mr. Olympia," the bodybuilders' highest honor, seven times. His wide VE-ME conjunction occupies the ASC at his Austrian birthplace and is somewhat closer to exact there. The narcissicism of VE seems obvious—some bodybuilders refer to their sport as sculpture with the self as the raw material—but what of Mercury? Mercury seems, in addition to being the planet of ideas, communication and studies, to be the symbol of the working class, and Arnold certainly came from an unpretentious background. Moreover, bodybuilding, like boxing earlier in this century, offers a fast track to fame and success for those few able to endure its 22d, rigors. Physical superiority is a class issue, one tied to an image of the body as an object rather than as one's self. His two most unexpected and spectacular wins (his first amateur Mr. Universe and his first Mr. Olympia) took place in New York under his URIC, transforming him from an unknown Austrian athlete to a world figure. ME always seems attracted to JU (and vice versa), so it isn't surprising that after he "made it," he settled in Hollywood where JU and VE assure success in a new career as an actor in action movies. He has enjoyed financial rewards and some self-indulgence. It was there he married Maria Shriver of the Kennedy clan. During Jupiterian George Bush's presidential campaign in 1988, Schwarzenegger campaigned for the Republican, making unarguably clear his new allegiance to privilege.

## Page 230

### MARK SPITZ FEBRUARY 10, 1950; 5:45 PM PST MODESTO, CA

<!-- chunk-id: acg-src-lewis-guttman-1989.page-0230.chunk-001 -->
<!-- chunk-sha256: 87bfe710d46168749853593251ea534da5f29f0eabfc054a31a654b3833777f5 -->
Mark Spitz became an instant celebrity after winning seven gold medals in the 1972 Olympics in Munich, which is situated precisely between his MA MH and SA MH lines. He had prepared with a grueling training period following a disappointing showing in Mexico City (MO IC, SA ASC) at the 1968 Olympics. He was born in California near his SU DSC, but his family moved to Hawaii when he was young. There he got into the habit of swimming at Waikiki Beach every day. His NE-MA conjunction there falls on the IC. Since Neptune was ancient god of the sea and Mars rules sporting events, it is not surprising that Mark would lay 229 the groundwork for a career in competitive swimming. The family then moved back to California and he continued to swim. His father, like Chris Evert's, really pushed him to win. He attended Indiana University (SA ASC) because of their excellent team. In 1965, he performed in a Tel Aviv swimming competition with others from around the world and won four gold medals (UR DSC, NE MH). He set records in California, Winnipeg, London, Berlin, and Lincoln, Nebraska. But his map shows little to confirm his spectacular wins in Munich, an event which catapulted him to superstar status overnight. Athletes occasionally adjust their birth dates for competitive reasons, so better data may emerge at a future time.

## Page 232

### EVONNE GOOLAGONG JULY S13 951;6:56 AM EST BARELLAN, AUSTRALIA

<!-- chunk-id: acg-src-lewis-guttman-1989.page-0232.chunk-001 -->
<!-- chunk-sha256: b2102bc980412377749850a46614f0809f4b4af92e09080d8f359e26b30b933d -->
An Australian-born tennis player, Evonne became an international celebrity when on July 2, 1971, she won the Wimbledon Women's Singles title under her SU, ME, and VE DSC, defeating fellow Australian Margaret Court. Of Aboriginal ancestry, she caused controversy in South Africa in 1971, being the first non-white to compete there in championship tennis under her MA 231 and URIC, JU ASC and NE DSC. She has constantly battled the media's insistence on making an issue of her ancestry and has let her annoyance at them be known. She had victories at all the leading tennis courts, including Wimbledon (London), Perth, the French Open, New Zealand, and the Australian Open. MA ASC is close to Melbourne, the location of the Australian Open, and her SU falls exactly on the ASC in Sydney, where her success as one of the first Aboriginals to achieve a world reputation has effected a significant challenge to Australian racism.

## Page 234

### CHRIS EVERT DECEMBER 21, 1954; 4:14 AM EST FT. LAUDERDALE, FL

<!-- chunk-id: acg-src-lewis-guttman-1989.page-0234.chunk-001 -->
<!-- chunk-sha256: 896539dbadbf3ce68c32955f1c4713b028b7900ea1b046a4a260d40c15682ad6 -->
As one of the world's top-rated women tennis players and holder of countless titles, this native of fiery, athletic Sagittarius has three planets rising in Scorpio with Pluto exactly overhead at birth. Chris Evert, a Florida native, began her training with her tennis pro dad at age 5, pouring every ounce of her determined Scorpio energy onto the court. People with Scorpio powerful in their charts seem inclined to experience the heights and the depths of experience throughout their lives. Winning world championships one year and losing them the next, Chrissie has always competed intensely, and her fans reflect her commitment with equivalent loyalty. Saturn, Pluto and the Moon are angular at her birth with Mars and Venus not far off. She gives a spirited performance, win or lose. She has played, won and lost to all the reigning queens 233 of tennis, including Margaret Court, Tracy Austin, Billie Jean King, Martina Navratilova, Evonne Goolagong, and Steffi Graf. For 13 years, Chris has wonat least one major title per year, something no one else has accomplished in her field. The wins have occurred at seven French Opens (UR and JU DSC), six U.S. Opens (MA IC, PLMH), three Wimbledons (MO, SA and VE MH), and two Australian Opens (SU and ME DSC, NE IC). Her eight-year marriage to British tennis champion, John Lloyd, born under Chris' VE MH, ended in divorce. After succeeding Billie Jean King as president of the Women's Tennis Association, she successfully fought Wimbledon organizers, threatening to lead a boycott of the entire women's pro team which included the world's top 40 female tennis pros. Her action resulted in the women being awarded prize money comparable to what the men were receiving.

## Page 236

### DOROTHY HAMILL SUEY 2659956; 9:18: PM CST CHICAGO, IL

<!-- chunk-id: acg-src-lewis-guttman-1989.page-0236.chunk-001 -->
<!-- chunk-sha256: 23f985ab1d77bae9dd35d2354237c99b2db8d079534129d5331c3f4e198b669f -->
In an electrifying performance at the 1976 Winter Olympics in Austria where her SU-UR conjunction exactly occupies the ASC, Dorothy Hamill captured the attention of millions of viewers worldwide with her flawless championship figure skating. She also captured a gold medal for the U.S. A few weeks later she won the Women's World Figure Skating Championship in Sweden (the SU, ME and PL ASC) to add to the three titles she already held in the U.S. She then turned pro. Dorothy was born in Chicago under a precise crossing 235 of PLDSC and VEIC with MA ASC in close proximity. Denver, Colorado (ME and JU DSC) was where she chose to train and develop her skills early in her career once she knew she wanted to take skating seriously. She won her first competition in Rhode Island in February 1974 (NE DSC), successfully returning to win two more events. She chose a choreographer from Toronto (MA ASC) to assist her in planning her balleton-ice performance in Austria which she dedicated to her idol, dancer Mikhail Baryshnikov. A Leo Sun with popular appeal and charisma to spare, she has gone on to do commercials and ABC sports specials.

### STEVIE CAUTHEN MAY 1, 1960; 8:43 PM EDT COVINGTON, KY

<!-- chunk-id: acg-src-lewis-guttman-1989.page-0238.chunk-002 -->
<!-- chunk-sha256: fed728e3ab79814ad4028c8e8f0c99e146442e04fa2a2302d4a54b57fa854b4d -->
Called the most successful young apprentice jockey in racing history, Stevie Cauthen became an overnight phenomenon. He began riding professionally at age 16 in his home state of Kentucky where his father worked with horses. During his debut at Belmont Park, he tooka serious fall under his PL MH ina threehorse collision. He recovered rapidly and was riding again shortly. Big wins occurred at River Downs (Ohio), Latonia (Kentucky), Arlington Park (Illinois), Belmont 237 roy Les oe (New York) and Santa Anita (California). He won almost every race that he ran on the East Coast, once winning 24 out of 53 races in a single week. In two years, he won more than 900 races, including the Triple Crown in the spring of 1978. He rode two winners on New Year's Day in 1979, but this was followed by a 110-race losing streak finally broken on February 1, 1979. The horse-racing centers of the East fall under his SU DSC and PL MH, and his losing streak began when he moved to the West Coast where his close MO-SA opposition is exactly angular on the meridian.

### FERNANDO VALENZUELA

## Page 240

### FERNANDO VALENZUELA NOVEMBER 1, 1960; 3:00 PM PST FUNDICION, MEXICO

<!-- chunk-id: acg-src-lewis-guttman-1989.page-0240.chunk-001 -->
<!-- chunk-sha256: 0546dff70f99d06c59cbe92388aca0edb5075a78a47dd1b44e5e43d29007fac5 -->
A Taracahitian Indian, Fernando lived in a farming village with his family. He and his brothers enjoyed playing "béisbol" from an early age. When he was about 16, he realized he could play professionally and competed in the national championships in La Paz (JU MH) where he performed well. He followed this with good playing in Yucatan near his SU and NE DSC where he was discovered by a Dodger scout. First signed to a minor league contract, he then progressed to the Los Angeles Dodgers where he astounded 239 the pros by leading them to a World Series victory over New York, their first since 1965. This earned him the Cy Young Award and the National League Rookie of the Year award in 1981, all on a $42,000-a-year contract. The following year he demanded $850,000. His demand was rejected and he refused to report for spring training. A compromise was reached and he returned to the Dodgers. His unprecedented rise to athletic stardom seems in keeping with JU MH, planet of sport and success, and PL DSC, planet of odds-defying, one-in-a-million miracles, both of which lines cross near Los Angeles, California. Between seasons, he enjoys visiting his family in Mexico.

## Page 243

### JOHN JACOB ASTOR JULY 17, 1763; 5:00 PM LMT WALDORF, GERMANY

<!-- chunk-id: acg-src-lewis-guttman-1989.page-0243.chunk-001 -->
<!-- chunk-sha256: f53dc09c8b42b655766b2bab2620b62add2bd64356cdaf01969e783e1850a428 -->
Born in Germany on the entrepreneurial and optimistic JU DSC line and impelled to travel by restless UR IC, John Jacob Astor arrived in Baltimore (MA and ME MR) at the age of 21 penniless. Inspired by tales of a fur trader he met on the transatlantic voyage, he decided to go into business trading with the Indians. Success in this enterprise along with his solid determination, shrewdness and driving ambition brought him into a commanding position in the burgeoning U.S./China trade, and within a few years he controlled all the fur companies then operating in the territory between St. Louis (VE MH, PLIC) and Denver (JU MH). He exported the largest portion of his merchandise to Canton, 243 China (SU IC), using New York middlemen as brokers

### (UR DSC, MA MR).

<!-- chunk-id: acg-src-lewis-guttman-1989.page-0243.chunk-002 -->
<!-- chunk-sha256: afe75e9f26fe13cbe70e10509ad6c8a9cdc5396e29b92135b214a45269a45dd6 -->
Astor chartered the American Fur Company and founded a subsidiary to the company, the Pacific Fur Company. Astoria, Oregon was settled by his company in 1811 and was the embarkation point for furs gathered from the entire Pacific Northwest region (JU MH). Its presence established an American claim to the territory. Astor exercised a virtual monopoly on the fur trade in the U.S. territories in the 1820s, and when he retired in 1834, he was the wealthiest man in the nation. He founded one of the original family dynasties in the U.S., built on financial success rather than aristocratic lineage, and in so doing, foreshadowed the financial and industrial future of the nation.

<a id="acg-src-lewis-guttman-1989.page-0245"></a>
<!-- page-id: acg-src-lewis-guttman-1989.page-0245 -->
## Page 245

### ANDREW CARNEGIE NOVEMBER 25, 1835; 6:00 AM LMT DUNFERMLINE, SCOTLAND

<!-- chunk-id: acg-src-lewis-guttman-1989.page-0245.chunk-001 -->
<!-- chunk-sha256: 69b270daf54efaaf7676d309a34730666210c7192917b12b54f01abc2bd1e3a6 -->
Born in Scotland with ME ASC conferring entrepreneurial skills and UR IC indicating a need to break away from his native surroundings, Andrew Carnegie came to the U.S. penniless and retired one of the wealthiest men in the world. His father was a weaver who emigrated with his family, settling near Pittsburgh (VE IC). This proved to be a fortunate spot for Carnegie. His various odd jobs included work on the railroad which led him to recognize the country's growing need for steel. Investing his wages in iron, he was eventually able to 245 resign his job and devote all his energy to business. The Andrew Carnegie Steel Company grew to become very successful, producing one quarter of the nation's steel along with much of the iron. With SU, MA and VEIC lines running through America's heartland and no outer planets nearby, his business easily prospered. When he sold his company for $250 million in 1901, he was one of the world's richest men. His benefactions, totaling approximately $350 million, include Carnegie Hall, the Carnegie Institute of Washington, the Carnegie Commission and the Carnegie Foundation. Carnegie Hall in New York City (nearest his VE IC) is an acoustic miracle that was for most of the 20th century the center of American classical music.

## Page 247

### WALLIS SIMPSON, THE DUCHESS OF WINDSOR JUNE 19, 1896; 10:30 PM EST BLUE RIDGE SUMMIT, PA

<!-- chunk-id: acg-src-lewis-guttman-1989.page-0247.chunk-001 -->
<!-- chunk-sha256: 0e0eb5f1e30498c0f61f1b9e39d0b4112ba34f004c8f8c9bca2296efc334fd41 -->
If being born ona JU DSC line implies that one might marry "well," indeed, this woman did. The biggest news story of 1936 was the romance of Edward VIII, King of England, and "the American socialite divorcee," Wallis Warfield Simpson. After months of scandal, Edward electrified the worid with his decision to abdicate the throne in order to "marry the woman I love." They married in Austria (SU ASC) on June 3,

### 1937. The Royal Family was no less outraged than the

<!-- chunk-id: acg-src-lewis-guttman-1989.page-0247.chunk-002 -->
<!-- chunk-sha256: c3c6cedf0e9924aa00ef3f820fd231fd64fb3eba6d90c05ad26e4c2f32c03b24 -->
rest of the world and moved quickly to "honor" the newlyweds with the title of Duke and Duchess of Windsor and an appointment for Edward as Governor of the Bahamas, where it was hoped they could be quickly forgotten (PL IC). Probably every woman in the world wondered what secret charm Wallis Simpson possessed that empowered her to lure a king from his throne. Born during the time of the Neptune-Pluto conjunction that sounded 247 the astrological death knell for monarchy, she had these planets rising in London along with the SU, ME and VE. So she was Cinderella, but her Prince was only a constitutional monarch, and he was forced to abdicate by the Prime Minister and the Church of England—a twice-divorced queen was unthinkable. Wallis Simpson's JU occupied London's IC, so she got a title out of it, but the affair illustrated that kingship had changed considerably since Henry VIII's time. The couple often visited Germany (the SU ASC and PL ASC) where it was rumored that they were Nazi sympathizers, but during the German occupation of France, they vacated their residency in Cannes to temporarily settle in Spain where JU is on the IC and VE occupies the ASC. It was here that they reportedly had their early romantic encounters. The Duchess outlived the Duke by 14 years, spending her last days in France, having vowed never to return to Great Britain, and having never been invited to do so. She returned only twice after their marriage—at his death and her own.

## Page 249

### BRUNO HAUPTMANN NOVEMBER 26, 1899; 1:00 PM MET KAMENZ, GERMANY

<!-- chunk-id: acg-src-lewis-guttman-1989.page-0249.chunk-001 -->
<!-- chunk-sha256: 675f0b9098505aca00e263e9852b805164e3e07c706493c588f7682372047752 -->
Born in Germany under a tense configuration of PL IC, MA MH and the MO DSC, Bruno Hauptmann immigrated to the U.S. in 1923. With several arrests in Germany before he left, he seemed destined to a life of misfortune as the relocation only placed him on different angles of the same probiematical planets. On March 1, 1932, the son of Charles Lindbergh was kidnapped in Sourlands, New Jersey, which sits pre- 249 cisely on Hauptmann's SU ASC line. His MO MH line is nearby with UR ASC and PL DSC. Although the child was never found alive or dead, Hauptmann was arrested for the crime and found guilty. Denying his guilt to the end, he was electrocuted on April 2, 1936. It is interesting to note that Charles Lindbergh (see map elsewhere in this text) has some strong ties with Germany, the birthplace of Bruno Hauptmann. Hauptmann's Mars-Pluto opposition is exact in right ascension and crosses his MO zenith at the Equator, an indication of a particularly powerful in mundo association. The MO, of course, symbolizes infants.

## Page 251

### BARBARA HUTTON NOVEMBER 14, 1912; 2:25 PM EST NEW YORK, NY

<!-- chunk-id: acg-src-lewis-guttman-1989.page-0251.chunk-001 -->
<!-- chunk-sha256: fb347af52bb1752dfd018b6196a63af03c6b743b22f1c7d15a668626ef8bd105 -->
Barbara Hutton emerged into the public eye as not only the heir to the Woolworth fortune but as a "poor little rich girl" heir to the Age of Industrialism gone sour. Born under VE MH opposite PL IC (see John Jacob Astor) and a millionaire by the age of four, Barbara Hutton was one of the few super-rich left in the world during the Depression which only encouraged her to flaunt what she had. At the death of her mother, her vast inheritance was left in trust to her father until she reached maturity. He seized this opportunity to pursue his interests in drinking, gambling and womanizing but had little time for young Barbara, who finally heard him say "1 love you" only on his deathbed many years later. She married seven times, never for long, searching, it seems, for the missing father image. The failure of her first two marriages were seemingly the hardest on the 251 still young, romantic idealist. Third husband Cary Grant from England (MO DSC) took care of her the best, but there was still much marital discord as a result of the personal conflict she never could resolve. Her one son, Lance Reventlow, from her second husband, was killed in an airplane crash while in his 20s exactly on her MA MH and UR ASC lines in Los Angeles. Her playgrounds in the U.S. were pretty much limited to New York (PLIC and VE MH) and Los Angeles (SU and MA MH, MO and UR ASC, NE DSC), but she was always ready to jet off to wherever in the world the next socialite party was to be held. Among some of her other favorite residences were Paris and the south of France (PL ASC), Cuernavaca, Mexico (SA IC) and Tangier (VE DSC). Although she inherited the vast Woolworth fortune which at one time was worth around $100 million (when a million dollars really meant something), she died in 1979 with less than $3,500 in her bank account.

## Page 253

### BETTY FORD APRIL 8, 1918; 3:45 PM CWT CHICAGO, IL

<!-- chunk-id: acg-src-lewis-guttman-1989.page-0253.chunk-001 -->
<!-- chunk-sha256: 63118ebd4deca8ba81e12631b013b5b799367e414a2f8ea585273ba255a1f034 -->
While the problems this woman has experienced in her life have been painful at a personal level, they have served as vehicles for tremendous healing on both individual and collective levels, offering an example of hope to anyone who has suffered similarly. The former First Lady grew up in Grand Rapids, Michigan (JU MH, VE DSC) where she was a talented, bright dancer and businesswoman. She went to New York, situated on her MO DSC, to join the Martha Graham Dance Troupe. Returning to Michigan, she became a department store buyer and also taught dance. As a politician's wife, she took an active stance onissues. Supportive of ERA, pro-choice and an advocate of women in politics, she became popular with feminists. Less than two months after her unexpected move to

### P4aye}

<!-- chunk-id: acg-src-lewis-guttman-1989.page-0253.chunk-002 -->
<!-- chunk-sha256: fea8b80c3e51cf25d67adc986965ff1fe073f0fb334db0cd193c09a49a70e182 -->
the White House where her MA ASC crosses her MO DSC, a mastectomy was performed on a malignant breast tumor. MA IC crosses PL ASC at Washington's latitude. If she achieved nothing else during her husband's administration than to focus attention onto a heretofore unspeakable subject of vital importance to millions of women worldwide, that would have been contribution enough. But long after the White House years, and as a direct result of them, she continued to transform her personal struggles into a collective healing experience. As a result of the pain her surgery subjected her to, she became dependent upon the analgesic drugs prescribed for her and publicly declared herself an addict. After successfully undergoing her own long recovery, she opened the Betty Ford Treatment Center for drug and alcohol problems in southern California which has been a major force in encouraging social recognition of chemical dependency. Her SA-NE conjunction (in Leo) rises at the clinic's site, and a SU*PL crossing suggests positive personal transformation.

## Page 255

### JOHN DELOREAN JANUARY 6, 1925; 12:00 PM EST DETROIT, MI

<!-- chunk-id: acg-src-lewis-guttman-1989.page-0255.chunk-001 -->
<!-- chunk-sha256: 5565cb255c7f75b25f827dcc1af0818567b035a4ee62d8268722b5fbb540a579 -->
John DeLorean was responsible for Detroit's dramatic shift in the late '60s to the production of smaller, more gas-efficient autos. An executive with General Motors from 1956 to 1973, he carefully moved up the ranks of management (JU MH), but just as the top was in sight, he unexpectedly resigned and went off on his own

### (MA ASC).

<!-- chunk-id: acg-src-lewis-guttman-1989.page-0255.chunk-002 -->
<!-- chunk-sha256: d313da0b0467ae6093c881441c669492bc853f5bb0e3c0c7e7cba81cb67a139d -->
Jupiter and Mars are the classic planets of daring, risktaking entrepreneurs. His innovative design techniques and up-to-date management style were at first challenged by Detroit's old guard but later proved to be cost-effective and highly profitable. Working first for Pontiac, then Chevrolet, he eventually tired and Pdehs, went west to create and design on his own. Lauded for its innovative design, the DeLorean automobile won praise, but it was priced out of the market for most U.S. automobile buyers. Financial hardship plagued his company which was based in Belfast, Northern Ireland under DeLorean's UR MH. Around the time of his second Saturn return, John DeLorean was arrested in a Los Angeles (NE DSC) hotel room and charged with conspiring to distribute $24 million worth of smuggled cocaine. Aside from the fact that the planet Neptune can symbolize shady deals, drugs and undercover activities, the NE DSC line over Los Angeles along with a PL*MA crossing at the exact latitude indicate the possibility of what his defense continually asserted: that he was framed by powers larger and more corrupt than he in a political and industrial territorial contest.

## Page 257

### QUEEN ELIZABETH II APRIL 21, 1926; 2:40 AM BDT LONDON, ENGLAND

<!-- chunk-id: acg-src-lewis-guttman-1989.page-0257.chunk-001 -->
<!-- chunk-sha256: 2e26eb25428d06468892a0ffe795288c258c68755aaff0d5677bd12f4021a249 -->
The British monarch who has reigned through much of the 20th century, Queen Elizabeth II ascended to the throne in 1952 during the Saturn-Neptune conjunction. Word came of her father's death while the young Princess was traveling in Kenya (UR and ME ASC). A serious and regal monarch with SA MH over London, she epitomizes Saturnian principles almost to a fault— she is dignified, puts responsibility to her job before her personal concerns, and follows the letter of the law in carrying out her duties. For the Capricornian British people, she symbolizes tradition, continuity and courage in the face of difficulty in an age when Britain's inter- 207 8755 20 4205 129 national power has been waning. She is married to Prince Philip, son of Andrew of Greece (VE ASC), and among their four children is Charles, Prince of Wales and heir to the throne (see his map in this section). Some trouble spots for Britain during her reign have been Northern Ireland and the Falkland Islands, both of which lie under the Queen's PL DSC. SA ASC passes through both Washington, D.C. and Ottawa, assuring that she retains the dignity and image of tradition in her dealings with those nations as well. Since she ascended the throne under a Saturn-Neptune conjunction, the upcoming recurrence of that aspect in 1989 may prove a critical turning point in her reign.

## Page 259

### JACQUELINE ONASSIS NIGY 2873989295 2-50' PM EDT SOUTHAMPTON, NY

<!-- chunk-id: acg-src-lewis-guttman-1989.page-0259.chunk-001 -->
<!-- chunk-sha256: e92b6d7e45087ebc8449a38f1e7086f1f82be766df1dd3c61e6561173c25f0de -->
AUTHORS' NOTE: Of the several contradictory birth times current for Jacqueline Onassis, this one was chosen as most in keeping with facts known about her life. With natal Neptune directly overhead at birth, Jacqueline Bouvier has embodied several archetypes and acted out collective fantasies on a national stage. The public's curiosity about her intensified the more she tried to shroud herself in an aura of mystery. While married to her first husband, President John F. Kennedy, the public was able to project onto her the Arthurian fantasy of Camelot. The Kennedys seemed to represent a new era in American politics, bringing youth, style and glamour into a heretofore stuffy White House. The act had a very short run, however, and her next role was that of mater dolorosa, the wounded, but glamorous widow. From what has since been revealed about her relation- Pda) ship to JFK (her NE MH goes exactly through his birthplace), the events in Dallas (the SU and ME MH) may have been a self-realization, freeing her from a rigid role unsuited to her true self. It did not, however, free her from publicity maniacs. The press pursued her as mercilessly as any Hollywood celebrity, and even running off to her sister in Europe where the SU and ME are again angular on the DSC did not help. Los Angeles, where JFK reputedly had extramarital affairs, is identified by UR DSC and PL MH on her map. Meanwhile, over in the Mediterranean several Greek Isles were not only home to Greek shipping tycoon Aristotle Onassis, her second husband, but also Jacqueline's JU on the IC. NE DSC crosses JU IC nearby, and these two planets of good fortune and surrender of ego provided her with a secure financial future in an isolated and probably confining milieu. After Onassis' death, Jacqueline returned to New York as a publishing executive (NE MH) where it can be presumed she still deals with collective fantasies.

## Page 261

### CHARLES MANSON NOVEMBER 12, 1934; 4:40 PM EST CINCINNATI, OH

<!-- chunk-id: acg-src-lewis-guttman-1989.page-0261.chunk-001 -->
<!-- chunk-sha256: 939bb323df761c18fc41f95af4b849bc7747fc557b6be0ceedabc0ef6959b7d1 -->
Deserted by his mother and raised by his grandmother in West Virginia, Charles Manson was briefly sent to Boys' Town in Nebraska. His first arrest in Peoria, Illinois for stealing food landed him in the Indiana Boys' School Reformatory (from which he escaped 18 times) under his UR ASC, ME DSC and JU DSC lines with PL IC nearby. Mars and Neptune can be an unusually unfortunate combination. While in some cases they can symbolize idealistic action (see Daniel Berrigan's map elsewhere in this text), they also can combine weird sex, drugs and violence in a particularly grisly manner. In Charles Manson's map, west of Los Angeles sits SA ASC; to the east, quite near the scene of his hideous crime (the group murder of eight-month pregnant actress Sharon Tate and four others), sit Manson's MA and NE DSC, 261 closely conjunct. Natally, Mars and Neptune are parallel in declination as well and oppose Saturn. The crime occurred on August 8, 1969, and two days later he murdered two others. In Cosmobiology, Ebertin suggests unfortunate consequences such as "a lasting weakness or illness due to misdirected energies stemming from deep feelings of inferiority" to be possible manifestations of a poorly integrated Mars-Saturn- Neptune combination. The unfortunate consequences included, in addition to the murders, a life-term prison sentence with no parole option. The "misdirected energies" were the brainwashing of a band of helplessly weak-egoed souls who, dominated by his charisma, were coerced into joining him in his monstrous activities. Mars and Neptune also come up in the maps of Harvey Milk and Dan White (q.v.) as well as being Gecrge Bush's planetary signature over Central America and thusly seem linked to particularly sensational and covert politically significant violence.

## Page 263

### DIANE VON FURSTENBERG DECEMBER 31, 1946; 3:00 AM GMT BRUXELLES, BELGIUM

<!-- chunk-id: acg-src-lewis-guttman-1989.page-0263.chunk-001 -->
<!-- chunk-sha256: 7ecf37758b4ad6feeca5b061f518807c67c26b6739987cc12ff3f03b4258b7d2 -->
Raised in Europe, Diane attended schools in Madrid, Geneva and Paris. In Geneva she met her husband-tobe, Prince Edward Egon von Furstenberg, heir to the Fiat fortune. They married in Paris between Diane's PL MH and JU ASC and on the latitude of two line crossings involving Pluto in July 1969. A tentative venture into the fashion industry led her to New York where she was an overnight success. Arriving in New York with a few dozen pieces, her orders quickly 263 escalated into the millions of dollars. Through central Europe pass both JU and VE on the ASC. Through New York City, UR is near the MH. A rectification of this birth time to one-half hour later would put these three planets in almost exact angular positions in the locations mentioned above. It would also put PL and SA ASC angular exactly in San Francisco where business might not proceed so effortlessly. A MO#NE crossing at New York's latitude shows sensitivity to women's needs and intuition of fashion trends and styles.

## Page 265

### URI GELLER DECEMBER 20, 1946; 2:00 AM EET TEL AVIV, PALESTINE

<!-- chunk-id: acg-src-lewis-guttman-1989.page-0265.chunk-001 -->
<!-- chunk-sha256: d436b89a67ef024003ecdaefb3a978adffc47d7b0417edb8774d0c2f568c6b71 -->
A telekinetic with a natal Mercury-Uranus opposition, Geller professes belief that his powers come through him from an intelligent, extraterrestrial energy, rather than labeling himself as "psychic." Born of a Hungarian (NE ASC) father and German (MA IC) mother in Israel, he moved to Cyprus while still quite young. If this time is accurate or a few minutes late, we can assume a role reversal in his parents' psychology. He claims first contact with an extra- 265 terrestrial force at about age three, and was taunted and teased by his schoolmates in Israel; he did better in Cyprus, a little further from the SA MH. He achieved notoriety when he was tested under rigorous supervision at the Stanford Research Institute and succeeded against enormous odds. His Geminian UR occupies the ASC at Stanford and Sagittarian ME, the DSC. He has been tested also in London, Paris, Germany and Scandinavia, and many have tried to discredit him. Since residing in Manhattan (SA ASC, NE IC), little has been heard from him and his powers have been sensationalized and disparaged.

## Page 267

### PRINCE CHARLES OF WALES NOVEMBER 14, 1948; 9:14 PM BST LONDON, ENGLAND

<!-- chunk-id: acg-src-lewis-guttman-1989.page-0267.chunk-001 -->
<!-- chunk-sha256: df82b0217f0f9a6d19756ff9590a47cb0efcd258cebd59357d745680e8005d2d -->
Rejecting his role as royal ribbon-cutter and hothouse heir-apparent, Prince Charles has fought hard to escape royal protocol by insisting that he is concerned and that his voice be heard on important social issues. In this, he aptly embodies his NEIC and PLASC angular through Britain. Dorothy Day had the same planets angular in New York (see map elsewhere in this book). But his VE IC poses the contradictions of a fondness for polo and his sedate palace domestic life, resulting in a frustrating and often seemingly confused sense of identity for the Prince. He would like to be more effective in issues concerning the homeless, the hungry, and the jobless, but his unwritten job description as King-in-waiting prevents him from doing much of anything very important while he does wait—short of posing and smiling for photographers and the press. Nor does he get any support from Margaret Thatcher 267 whose administration considers his actions interfering and meddlesome. Occasional interests in subjects like organic gardening, talking to his plants, homeopathy, Jungian psychology and communing with nature have palace gossipers and tabloids labeling the Prince as a "loon." He has studied Jung with Laurens Van der Post (Zurich is right on Charles' PL ASC), and it is even suggested that he has looked into astrology. Lately, however, there have been so many rumors about problems in his marriage to Princess Diana (see map elsewhere in this section) that they have diverted attention from the Prince's far-ranging personal interests. He is fond of Australia, where his UR DSC, JU ASC and SA MH hint at an area where he is freer to express his individuality in the less rigid social atmosphere there. Once he is King, his SU and SA lines in Moscow and Tokyo hint at increased relations with the Orient.

## Page 269

### STKES APRIL 19, 1954; 7:40 AM GMT

<!-- chunk-id: acg-src-lewis-guttman-1989.page-0269.chunk-001 -->
<!-- chunk-sha256: a38b8d8fe8ce3c7ea400283a42e589344a5355ce49f479343ab6ea87ff04732b -->
While it is not possible to publicly verify this anecdote or data since it is that of a private individual, the story is too good to pass up on that account. K was traveling in Europe with another woman, and they began to exhaust their traveling money at the same time they reached Monte Carlo. Rather than spend their last $20 on something sensible like food or a phone call home, they found themselves seated at the blackjack table in the world-famous Casino. It is here that the story ceases to be ordinary: of the 50 to 60 hands she played, K lost only five. She was dealt blackjacks between 20- 30 times, and the Casino, understandably distressed, changed dealers on her five times. As time wore on, the assembled Arab sheiks, heiresses and petty royalty began betting on her hands, compounding the losses to the Casino. Finally, when she had amassed a "pile of thousand-franc notes," she thought it best to quit while she was ahead. As she was leaving, she was accosted by a handsome bearded gentleman who introduced himself as a German count and invited K and her companion to spend 269 the weekend at his villa on Ibiza. There, he and K initiated a romantic liaison. As time went on, the count confessed himself to be falling in love and suggested that he and K should visit his mother in Germany. In

### Germany, however, midnight struck for our Cinderella

<!-- chunk-id: acg-src-lewis-guttman-1989.page-0269.chunk-002 -->
<!-- chunk-sha256: b01e5124f3aeb59415d260a86e7401de7ea93a45abb58c747cb7fa6494459991 -->
the count, back under the influence of his mother, began drinking and became abusive; the mother, of course, hated K. Realizing that all good things must come to an end, K left and flew to Paris, where she found her Astro*Carto*Graphy map awaiting her in her post office box. On it, she couldn't help noticing the exact crossing of JU ASC and PL IC right over Monte Carlo. This is reinforced by another crossing of JU*PL on the Casino's latitude in the North Atlantic, while the count seems to be symbolized by the SU*MA crossing in Idaho, close to Ibiza's latitude. In Germany, the PLIC crosses not Jupiter but MA DSC, indicating the change of atmosphere in her relationship. At the time all this occurred, K was enjoying a Jupiter return, the time every 12 years when Jupiter returns to its natal place, and since her Jupiter is in Gemini to start with, good luck in traveling does not seem inappropriate.

## Page 271

<!-- chunk-id: acg-src-lewis-guttman-1989.page-0271.chunk-001 -->
<!-- chunk-sha256: 83578c539911094b96033b4254c4174b876397639c8f7cf7f57aa9edb1b5e746 -->
16M, 83"

### PRINCESS DIANA OF WALES JULY 1, 1961; 7:45 PM BDT SANDRINGHAM, ENGLAND

<!-- chunk-id: acg-src-lewis-guttman-1989.page-0271.chunk-002 -->
<!-- chunk-sha256: 61a8d2cc886cbde38e53130efa417d6929be46c712c3069eaaa70a6b24105b01 -->
Certainly the most media-publicized event in the personal lives of British royalty in recent memory was the betrothal and marriage of Diana to Prince Charles and the birth of their children, among whom will be the heir to the British throne. A Cancerian who formerly taught schoolchildren in a London suburb before her engagement, Diana delights in the raising of her two sons. With ME DSC near London, she symbolizes an important bridge between ordinary people and the ruling class. Britons have projected onto her a fairy tale princess image, and unlike others in her position (Princess Grace, Jacqueline Onassis), she seems to enjoy it—perhaps on account of her youthfulness. Still, one wonders how truly content someone with 271 angular Mercury can be in the completely structured world of a constitutional monarchy. With aSUsPL (one of the recurring themes in the royal family) crossing on the latitude of London along with ME DSC, she is constantly the subject of London's tabloids. Currently, they focus on the state of her marriage to Charles and speculate endlessly upon its demise and resurrection. Diana seems to love the European jet set (SU DSC, NE MH) where she can indulge her tastes for luxury and frivolity. JU IC in New York City assures notoriety and comfort. While Charles would rather listen to classical music or be involved in social welfare programs, Diana relishes the life of a celebrity. Her Saturn return (1990-

### 91) may see restricting and confining influences in her

<!-- chunk-id: acg-src-lewis-guttman-1989.page-0271.chunk-003 -->
<!-- chunk-sha256: 1480ed4273591ff3bf95336c18e83d4c6c2437c556335337a4518c53028fdecd -->
life have a stabilizing effect on the royal marriage.

## Page 273

### PRINCE WILLIAM JUNE 21, 1982; 9:03 PM BDT LONDON, ENGLAND

<!-- chunk-id: acg-src-lewis-guttman-1989.page-0273.chunk-001 -->
<!-- chunk-sha256: 32e01605bbdb94a844e7605764a547ac4e7a6d13232802af38294cd905959f92 -->
Born under an exact eclipse of the Sun at the Summer Solstice in 1982, this double-Cancer, future King of England has a powerful chart. The exact JU-PL conjunction over London's MH suggests that he, like his father, will be deeply concerned with political and social issues, and, unlike the present Queen, will be willing to create his own rules and govern according to individualistic personal ethics. Nevertheless, NE ASC indicates as well as an interest in the welfare of others a possible spiritual direction. He and his father both share PL and NE lines over Britain, suggesting that the 21st century may see the monarchy continue its unwilling reform in light of new conditions and a 273 profound change in concepts of what it means to be British. The Pluto-Neptune conjunction of the early 1890s demarcated the "beginning of the end" of absolute monarchy and first symbolically manifested in the chart of Wallis Simpson (q.v.). Charles and William could reign over the creation of a new type of empire. An exact SA*PL crossing at London's latitude keys into other royal family charts. But the JU-PL combination most of all suggests a new trading or power role for the British economy—perhaps as some sort of economic hub or data-processing center in a computerized world system. It further hints at the development of new relationships with old members of the Empire, India and Canada in particular, since the same lines pass through the capitals of those countries.

<a id="acg-src-lewis-guttman-1989.page-0276"></a>

## Page 276

### UNITED STATES OF AMERICA

<!-- chunk-id: acg-src-lewis-guttman-1989.page-0276.chunk-001 -->
<!-- chunk-sha256: c3d0ef9f795e4e5dedc4edc4dd20efd10207a67d7268da8520b3d9cd6e17768c -->
Declaration of War: JULY 6, 1775; 11:00 AM LMT

### PHILADELPHIA, PA

<!-- chunk-id: acg-src-lewis-guttman-1989.page-0276.chunk-002 -->
<!-- chunk-sha256: c287bdb308eb8211d34aa5b963880a239e83a33f1b4511ee50224888b0a5c594 -->
Three maps are presented here for the birth of the USA. The Declaration of War-is one propounded by astrologer Helen Boyd, whose research in historical archives in London and the USA led to discovery of the time at which the Second Continental Congress declared war against Britain in 1775. This chart seems to embody best the foreign relations of the U.S., particularly those of a military nature. The MA lines are particularly apt: the ASC passes through the battlefields of the Revolution and those of the Civil War. The Civil War can be seen (especially by those in the South) as the first war of American empire building as it assured markets and a strengthened federalism in addition to settling the more widely recognized issue of slavery. The MA MH line passes through Berlin, unarguably the height of American aii, military projection abroad, and thus quite appropriate for the culmination. MA DSC passes through Viet Nam, a fitting symbol for the decline in power that military engagement there came to symbolize. The fourth MA line, the IC, passes through the Bering Strait, the U.S./U.S.S.R. common border area. It would be hard to imagine a more appropriate distribution of Mars lines. The PL MH lies atop Hiroshima and Nagasaki (see also Harry Truman and atomic bomb maps elsewhere in this text), while the nation of Iran is crossed by the SU DSC and PL ASC lines, perhaps part of the reason it is so often cast ina "shadow" or ultimate villain role. Moscow finds the MO in the MH, designating a place where sensitivity and receptivity are extreme. The map is made more potent when it is realized that MA MH had progressed to exact conjunction with PL MH over Hiroshima in 1945, identifying both enemies in World War II as well as the atomic bombing.

<a id="acg-src-lewis-guttman-1989.page-0277"></a>

## Page 278

### UNITED STATES OF AMERICA

<!-- chunk-id: acg-src-lewis-guttman-1989.page-0278.chunk-001 -->
<!-- chunk-sha256: ed6b49bb58fb492535f55720c5aab1363cc5d71705dbb99c457e551e303e259e -->
Declaration of Independence: JULY 4, 1776; 2:13 AM LMT

### PHILADELPHIA, PA

<!-- chunk-id: acg-src-lewis-guttman-1989.page-0278.chunk-002 -->
<!-- chunk-sha256: 4df4e9488c35f3d1f8556159819ee482a961912d0a21ab9955a41dab2a305745 -->
The Gemini rising map for the U.S. features each of the 10 planets passing through the continental U.S. and seems most appropriate for an internal view of the nation. The planets identifying different geographical regions of the country seem to symbolize those areas well. MA ASC dominates easternmost Maine where hardy Yankees typify the New Englander's tenaciousness in the face of antagonistic climate and terrain. The MO MH in the Northeast identifies the "pilgrim fathers" and the strong family orientation of early settlers as well as offering ironic comment as to the Puritans' evaluation of "pilgrim mothers"; MO and MA together are often heroic and somewhat misogynous. UR ASC over New York Harbor symbolizes the center of world capitalism and the development of individualism made possible by its success. The Statue of Liberty lies under this line, symbolizing the freedom sought by millions 279 of immigrants who were initially welcomed by her. PL MH through Central America typifies the endless covert wars of domination that have smoldered there since the U.S. became a nation, particularly since Pluto's discovery. ME on the IC through the Midwest defines American working-class values and the link between the East and West Coasts. SA DSC lies over the theocratic Mormon country along witha SU IC line, potent symbols of paternalistic family values. JUIC lures millions to Las Vegas, where money changes hands so fast that everyone is potentially a millionaire (at least for 15 minutes) and where conformity and mass culture have created a cult of wealth and optimism. VE IC goes right down Hollywood Boulevard, where America's own anima and feminine ideal were defined, deified, and turned into money. It also passes through the Mother Lode, the source of the nation's early wealth. NE DSC seems appropriate for the rainy, timberrich Northwest as well as for foggy San Francisco, whose history confirms that the Greeks were right in ascribing rulership of earthquakes to Poseidon.

## Page 280

### UNITED STATES OF AMERICA

<!-- chunk-id: acg-src-lewis-guttman-1989.page-0280.chunk-001 -->
<!-- chunk-sha256: 6bd25932d2b7dbc98e245eb0e9a8802c5bca140414ad9f4eec0f7dad91a95e0c -->
Declaration of Independence: JULY 4, 1776; 4:47 PM LMT

### PHILADELPHIA, PA

<!-- chunk-id: acg-src-lewis-guttman-1989.page-0280.chunk-002 -->
<!-- chunk-sha256: f5aa61a0d35201857453cdcaa8eceb6de88ea895fb55f36c3ddffd9496b9cb72 -->
Of the many charts proposed for the 4th of July, 1776, the Sagittarius rising version favored by Benjamin Sibley and Dane Rudhyar and rectified to a time of 4:47 PM by Barry Lynes seems most appropriate to the nation's external identity and significance to the world community. UR DSC and NE MH lines cross at Philadelphia, 281 suggesting either idealization of individualism or a unique and personal expression of unconscious cultural collectivism. In London, however, UR IC crosses PL ASC, darkly implying the decline of the empire and colonialism brought about again by Uranian individualism. The conjunction of these two planets in the 1960s saw a cultural revolution take place worldwide. In this chart, Iran is again under Pluto, though Venus and Jupiter in Viet Nam indicate that perhaps the failure there was invigorating for democracy, even if it was a disaster for the national pride and the military.

## Page 282

### UNITED KINGDOM JANUARY 1, 1801; 12:00 AM GMT LONDON, ENGLAND

<!-- chunk-id: acg-src-lewis-guttman-1989.page-0282.chunk-001 -->
<!-- chunk-sha256: 2b72d61c1d9a476d8ec9a38ecbdd5e06afe43d644bb4d1670bbd22a9a8e3c68c -->
Finding a chart for anationas oldas Britain is a difficult task. What most astrologers in the U.K. have settled on is the chart for the union of England, Scotland, Wales and Ireland at the beginning of the 19th century. Since Britain once possessed an empire that literally spanned the globe, no map could be expected to include every eventin every locality; still, this one does seem to have relevance for the Victorian Era. India has ME ASC and UR MH, the very same lines that Gandhi (see map elsewhere in this text), liberator of the subcontinent, would have in his nativity. Australia 283 shows many of the same planets in the western part of the nation as would be there when that country achieved its independence in 1901. The Falklands see MA MH and the SU DSC, a situation strongly reminiscent of Margaret Thatcher's map (q.v.) which has the same planets there. She was, of course, the Prime Minister in power when Britain spent millions to defend the unimportant islands against Argentinian invasion. And, of course, UR ASC goes through the Republic of Ireland, which was reluctantly under British rule from 1800 until 1922, and through Northern Ireland, an area of endless strife and dissension. This 1801 map seems validated when a person or situation arises that reinforces the lines already here, an attribute it shares with other national maps.

## Page 284

### CANADA JULY 1, 1867; 12:01 AM LMT OTTAWA, CANADA

<!-- chunk-id: acg-src-lewis-guttman-1989.page-0284.chunk-001 -->
<!-- chunk-sha256: 0aaad700df6f5b4c0db3198cb2c321f34b5a8e135615056f2bbb2c770fd47a32 -->
This map is for the creation of Canada as a political entity in the British Commonweaith of Nations. As national charts usually show relations with other nations best and since Canada was established as part of the British Empire, not too much should be expected. The Canadians have recently adopted a new Constitution (April 17, 1982, at 11:36 AM EST, Ottawa). Time will tell if its horoscope and map are more appropriate to their role in the contemporary world. This map shows the UR and SU IC lines passing directly through Ottawa and Washington which, significantly, are on almost the very same parallel of longitude. UR seems to consistently identify people and nations committed to individualism and democratic rights, and in these Canada has always been a leader in the Commonwealth. Canadians may find it amusing to note SA DSC going through Newfoundland, historically conservative and autonomous, even to the extent of having its own unique time zone. The Western provinces fall under the SA-PL opposition, and they have often felt "left out" of the decision-making process and unfairly taxed by the more populous East. NE ASC at Ottawa, of course, indicates the difficulty Canadians have had in defining themselves, caught 285 between American culture and English traditions. Brian

### Clark wrote in his book Charting Canada

<!-- chunk-id: acg-src-lewis-guttman-1989.page-0284.chunk-002 -->
<!-- chunk-sha256: c10589c12c4dd954172d853c9bee27f845f43afe90e3d98db7f9e725c108a06a -->
As a nation we often ask ourselves who we are, but as one sage Canadian has said, 'we may not be eager to find our identity in case it is not a very flattering picture.' With the Sun and Uranus only 15 minutes apart, we have the uniqueness befitting a country of the new world, with an independent spirit well hidden in our 4th House. The Cancer Sun needs close family ties and could nourish the world as long as we do it our way. A very fast Moon in Gemini in the 3rd House indicates our diverse roots and our bilingualism. It could also explain why Canadians use the telephone more than any other country in the world. Our 5th House Mercury and strong Uranus stress our interest in electronic technology and space age communications. A strong Neptune and 12th House Jupiter in Pisces indicate the exceptional idealism of many Canadians as well as the mysticism of our native Indians. The map for the new Canadian Constitution shows a SU-SA IC conjunction in the same longitude in which the SU-UR IC is found in this map. This may suggest a new conservatism, aligning the nation with business, political power and economical forces both there and in Washington. Tories have held power since its adoption. Saturn and Pluto are conjoined in the new Constitution chart and occupy the Ottawa IC so that the West, under SA-PL in the old chart, may play an increasingly important role in this new period of Canadian history.

## Page 286

### AUSTRALIAN FEDERATION JANUARY 1, 1901; 1:35 PM EST SYDNEY, AUSTRALIA

<!-- chunk-id: acg-src-lewis-guttman-1989.page-0286.chunk-001 -->
<!-- chunk-sha256: a626d74fec6ad5472b0baefcda09d17c37a5684705544b113e98683762a7d09e -->
Australia formally became a nation in the British Commonwealth with the reading of a proclamation in Sydney. Some argument can be made for a time of 14:00 GMT, December 31, 1900, as that is when the new year began in Sydney, but Australians themselves prefer the 1:35 PM time. The SU-SA MH conjunction, so typical of important charts in the Capricornian British sphere, lies over the all-but-uninhabited western desert where mineral wealth is just beginning to be exploited. The west coast, sparsely developed at present, finds the JU-ME conjunction in the MH, suggesting that Australia's role in the world of the future may be based here, already the center of entrepreneurial expansion anda business ethic not unlike that of the western USA. Oil has been discovered under the NE IC off the northwest coast. 287 UR ASC, PL DSC and VE ASC lines cross on the Turkish coast. There, in World War I, tens of thousands of Australian and New Zealand troops gave their lives in defense of the British homeland threatened by the German Empire, and this event is regarded among Australians as their most courageous act on the international stage. The same lines pass through Washington, an indication of the critical role the U.S. was to play in contributing to the protection of Australia from Japanese invasion in World War II, as well as the "loss of innocence" precipitated by Britain's slow reaction to Australia's imminent danger. The SU and SA occupy the ASC in South Africa, a reminder that when the nation was born Britain was already at war with the Boers over the future of southern Africa. With so many planets in late Sagittarius and early Capricorn, the 1990s will be a critical period for Australia and her identity as a nation.

## Page 288

### ISRAEL MAY 14, 1948; 4:37 PM EET TEL AVIV, ISRAEL

<!-- chunk-id: acg-src-lewis-guttman-1989.page-0288.chunk-001 -->
<!-- chunk-sha256: d6322a888320fdde341b4efb9cb1274cd3b46299c5215638ce432cd2458445fa -->
In viewing Israel's Astro*Carto*Graphy map, drawn for its birth as a nation, some very appropriate symbolism results in places where that nation has maintained significant relationships. Directly overhead is the MO MH in Leo, symbolizing its national inception as a family gathering of Jews from around the world. Just to the east, over the most unfriendly Arab nations which have beleaguered Israel since its birth, sit PL, SA and MA MH, a most belligerent trio. In the U.S., particularly over Washington and New York, are the MO ASC (identity, origins and relatives) and NE IC 289 (idealism centered around a homeland). NE on the ASC dominates the German heartland and the sites of the most infamous concentration camps from whose horrors the country was born. JU on the IC identifies England as the nation whose Foreign Secretary championed the cause of a Jewish homeland in the years after World War I. Any chart for this birth date shows real problems in the late 1980s and early 1990s: present-day Uranus, Neptune and Pluto all aspect their natal positions simultaneously with Pluto in Scorpio squaring itself and birth map Saturn and Mars through the mid- 1990s.

### SA ASC VOYAGE OF THE TITANIC

<a id="acg-src-lewis-guttman-1989.page-0290"></a>

## Page 290

### VOYAGE OF THE TITANIC APRIL 2, 1912; 8:00 PM LMT BELFAST, NORTHERN IRELAND

<!-- chunk-id: acg-src-lewis-guttman-1989.page-0290.chunk-001 -->
<!-- chunk-sha256: 6f2e13f111f65e1c77e286b9c769f77f22bbd9addeefa070ab052ccd470d9c9b -->
When the Titanic hit an iceberg on the night of April 13, 1912, more was destroyed than the largest and most luxurious ship afloat and the 1500 passengers for whom lifeboats had not been provided in the original plans. Western technology, soaring on a cloud of hubris, had proclaimed the ship "unsinkable," and, ironically, "The Last Word in Luxury." Many of its passengers were millionaires, one of whom, upon being informed that the ship had struck an iceberg, demanded that some of the ice be brought to freshen his drink. The news media, certain that nothing could happen to so great an enterprise, originally ran headlines proclaiming that the ship was intact and was being towed to New York. When the real news followed, a national shock wave ensued, comparable to that of the destruction of the Challenger and the assassination of John F. Kennedy (both events show 291 PL angular). Laws were passed requiring sufficient lifeboats for every passenger, lifeboat drills on every voyage, and, most importantly, federal regulation of radio use. But no laws could reconstitute the goddess of technology, who would never again be quite so omnipotent. Investigation revealed that the captain, intent on a rapid crossing, had maintained full speed despite several radio warnings of icebergs in the vicinity. The ship sank at longitude 50W14, latitude 41N46. This map is drawn for the departure of the ship from Belfast, where it had been built. Before setting out to cross the Atlantic, it stopped several times to board passengers, but in this departure, the first persons who would drown in the frigid dark waters 11 days later embarked. Aside from the fact that this sailing occurred two hours after a total eclipse of the Moon and a couple of days prior to a Mercury retrograde station, the map shows a close conjunction of MA and PL at the MH (in Gemini) at exactly 50W00 longitude. The ship never passed this point.

## Page 292

### FIRST ATOMIC REACTION DECEMBER 2, 1942; 4:25 PM CWT CHICAGO, IL

<!-- chunk-id: acg-src-lewis-guttman-1989.page-0292.chunk-001 -->
<!-- chunk-sha256: 0d91b1c335f6eee9a1230084b93eda6f4ce873bec14274888cd32f044181dfa6 -->
Part of the creation of the first atomic bomb was the success of the first controlled atomic reaction necessary to create enriched uranium and plutonium with which the bombs dropped on the Japanese were constructed. It took place under the bleachers at the University of Chicago in 1942, the culmination of the top secret Manhattan Project on which the best scientists of the world worked for years. Varying times (within an hour) are used as the exact time the reaction took place is a matter of conjecture. If the time presented here (4:25 PM CWT) is corrected to 15 minutes earlier, the UR DSC passes exactly through 293 Hiroshima (where the uranium bomb was dropped) and through Chernobyl] (UR MH) and Three Mile Island (UR ASC), all within two degrees. At Three Mile Island, PL IC is also present. It is important to note that the bomb tested at Alamagordo as well as that dropped on Nagasaki were constructed with plutonium; the Hiroshima bomb was made of uranium. Richland in eastern Washington state may well be the most radioactively contaminated site in the world since it has been the center of U.S. government atomic bomb production for 40 years. NE and the MO occupy the DSC where whole families are stricken with rare cancers, miscarriages and other unusual health problems. UR ASC and PLIC lines in the middle Atlantic states may foreshadow similar problems there.

## Page 294

### LSD FIRST INGESTED APRIL 19, 1943; 4:23 PM MET BASEL, SWITZERLAND

<!-- chunk-id: acg-src-lewis-guttman-1989.page-0294.chunk-001 -->
<!-- chunk-sha256: 293ff96ff552f27fe49ec6ab4dac1651fec829408d2f807197c8ec1559429326 -->
Atomic energy and LSD were born only a few months apart. On April 19, 1943, at 4:20 PM, Albert Hoffmann, a Swiss biochemist, administered an injection to himself in order to ascertain whether the drug that had so disoriented him the day before had in fact been LSD or something else. He quickly discovered that it was indeed the LSD that had so intoxicated him on the 18th since it also made him very high on the 19th. He scientifically detailed his bewildered attempts to ride home on his bicycle. yaks) The map shows NE ASC closely through Basel, Switzerland at the time he injected himself with the drug. It also shows VE and UR rising simultaneously exactly in San Francisco at that moment, indicating the place where the drug would find its greatest audience and contribute to a consciousness-based social revolution. NE IC and JU ASC are crossing in Washington, an interesting comment on the concern the CIA has shown in researching LSD for crowd control and brainwashing. The NE IC line passes near Timothy Leary's academic home in Boston, perhaps hinting at the role he would play in LSD's dissemination.

## Page 296

### FIRST ATOMIC BOMB TEST JULY 16, 1945; 5:29 AM MWT ALAMOGORDO, NM

<!-- chunk-id: acg-src-lewis-guttman-1989.page-0296.chunk-001 -->
<!-- chunk-sha256: e2ff4b6903b1770c7d7804c048f6d3d8ffa2abb90bd223cc000191ae7036f2a6 -->
"Tam become death, the destroyer of worlds," shuddered Robert Oppenheimer, quoting from the Bhagavad- Gita when the first atomic explosion fueled by plutonium filled the New Mexico sky with a new and terrible power. While Dr. Oppenheimer seems to have adjusted to his new role, most of the rest of us have not. The map for this event shows much about the new age of uranium and plutonium. The PL ASC line passes through Oak Ridge, Tennessee where the uranium for the bomb was refined and enriched; it passes through Chicago, where that same uranium was processed in the first atomic reactor and turned into the first concentrated plutonium to exist 297, on earth. It is estimated that a pound of this element, dispersed in the atmosphere, would give everyone on earth lung cancer. The same PL ASC line passes up into the polar regions then descends as PLDSC to pass through Hiroshima and Nagasaki where it would cross PL lines on maps for the USA (Declaration of War chart) and Harry Truman (q.v.). PL MH passes a little west of Chernobyl. The accuracy of these PL lines attests to the effectiveness of using "true rise" as opposed to the more common ecliptic intercept calculations in plotting lines. The aptness of Pluto for the "destroyer of worlds" seems to need little comment, except to observe that the development of atomic energy began in earnest after the discovery of that planet in 1930 and the rise to power of Hitler, whose scientists were the first to explore nuclear fission.

## Page 314

### THE LLEWELLYN MODERN ASTROLOGY LIBRARY

<!-- chunk-id: acg-src-lewis-guttman-1989.page-0314.chunk-001 -->
<!-- chunk-sha256: c747c737954060d6d09601662aca3357da8b228970568f64efbaf7dc30706c83 -->
Books for the Leading Edge of practical and applied astrology as we move toward the culmination of the 20th century. This is not speculative astrology, nor astrology so esoteric as to have little practical application in meeting the needs of people in these critical times. Yet, these books go far beyond the meaning of "practicality" as seen prior to the 1980s. Our needs are spiritual as well as mundane, planetary as well as particular, evolutionary as well as progressive. Astrology grows with the times, and our times make heavy demands upon Intelligence and Wisdom. The authors are all professional astrologers drawing from their own practice and knowledge of historical persons and events, demonstrating proof of their conclusions with the horoscopes of real people in real situations. Modern Astrology relates the individual person in the Universe in which he/she lives, not as a passive victim of alien forces, but as an active participant in an environment expanded to the breadth and depth of the Cosmos. Weare not alone, and our responsibilities are infinite. The horoscope is both a measure and a guide to personal movement—seeing every act undertaken, every decision made, every event, as time dynamic, with effects that move through the many dimensions of space and levels of consciousness in fulfillment of Will and Purpose. Every act becomes an act of Will, for we extend our awareness to consequences reaching to the ends of time and space. This is astrology supremely important to this unique period in human history, when Pluto transits through Scorpio, and Neptune through Capricorn. The books in this series are intended to provide insight into the critical needs and the critical decisions that must be made. These books, too, are "active agents," bringing to the reader knowledge which will liberate the higher forces inside each person to the end that we may fulfill that for which we were intended —Car] Llewellyn Weschcke

($7.00 for Canada & Mexico, $20.00 overseas, first class mail). Many bookstores also have The Llewellyn New Times available to their customers. Ask for it. Stay in touch! In The Llewellyn New Times' pages you will find news and reviews of new books, tapes and services, announcements of meetings and seminars, articles helpful to our readers, news of authors, advertising of products and services, special money-making opportunities, and much more.

## Page 316

### CHIRON

<!-- chunk-id: acg-src-lewis-guttman-1989.page-0316.chunk-001 -->
<!-- chunk-sha256: bbfd883513c754da853d3b2cea868eccab608b9000ac70f43ae8c7e1195a1f9e -->
by Barbara Hand Clow This new astrology book is about the most recently discovered planet, Chiron. This little-known planet was first sighted in 1977. It has an eccentric orbit on a 50-51-year cycle between Saturn and Uranus. It brought farsightedness into astrology because Chiron is the bridge to the outer planets, Neptune and Pluto, from the inner ones. The small but influential planet of Chiron reveals how the New Age Initiation will affect each one of us. Chiron is an Initiator, an Alchemist, a Healer, and a Spiritual Guide. For those who are astrologers, Chiron has more information than any other book about this planet. Learn why Chiron rules Virgo and the Sixth House. Have the necessary information about Chiron in each house, in each sign, and how the aspects affect each person's chart. Chiron is sure to become a best-selling, albeit controversial, book in the astrological world. The influences of Chiron are an important new factor in understanding capabilities and potentials which we all have. Chiron rules: Healing with the hands, Healing with crystals, Initiation and Alchemy and Alteration of the body by Mind and Spirit. Chiron also rules Cartomancy and the Tarot reader. As such it is an especially vital resource for everyone who uses the Tarot. 0-87542-094-X, 320 pgs., 6 x 9, charts,softcover $9.95 PLUTO: The Evolutionary Journey of the Soul by Jeff Green If you have ever asked "Why am I here?" or "What are my lessons?," then this book will help you to objectively learn the answers from an astrological point of view. Green shows you how the planet Pluto relates to the evolutionary and karmic lessons in this life and how past lives can be understood through the position of Pluto in your chart. Beyond presenting key principles and ideas about the nature of the evolutionary journey of the Soul, this book supplies practical, concise and specific astrological methods and techniques that pinpoint the answers to the above questions. If you are a professional counselor or astrologer, this book is indispensible to your practice. The reader who studies this material carefully and applies it to his or her own chart will discover an objective vehicle to uncover the essence of his or her own state of being. The understanding that this promotes can help you cooperate with, instead of resist, the evolutionary and karmic lessons in your life. Green describes the position of Pluto through all of the signs and houses, explains the aspects and transits of Pluto, discusses Pluto in aspect to the Moon's Nodes, and gives sample charts and readings. It is the most complete look at this "new" planet ever. 0-87542-296-9, 6 x 9, 360 pgs., softcover $12.95

### OPTIMUM CHILD

<!-- chunk-id: acg-src-lewis-guttman-1989.page-0316.chunk-002 -->
<!-- chunk-sha256: 5f3c66a9efe83f9a170afeb21e6dac111169a7bf4d5b3c29247e52bc2d5d2d86 -->
by Gloria Star This is a brand new approach to the subject of astrology as applied to children. Not much has been written on developmental astrology, and this book fills a gap that has needed filling for years. There is enough basic material for the novice astrologer to easily determine the needs of his or her child (or children). All it takes is the natal chart. A brief table of where the planets were when your child was born is included in the book so that even if you don't have a chart on your child, you can find out enough to fully develop his or her potentials. In Optimum Child you will find a thorough look at the planets, houses, rising signs, aspects and transits. Each section includes physical, mental and emotional activities and needs that this child would best respond to. It is the most comprehensive book yet on child astrology. This one is definitely not for children only. Every parent and professional astrologer should read it thoroughly. You should use it and help your child develop those talents and potentials inherent in what is shown within the natal chart. 0-87542-740-5, 360 pgs., 6 x 9, softcover $9.95

by Denning & Phillips This book is filled with guidelines and rites for powerful magical action. There are rites for the individual magician, rites for the magical group. The rites herein are given in full, and are revealed for the first time. Planetary Magick provides a full grasp of the root system of Western Magick, a system which evolved in Babylonia and became a principal factor in the development of Qabalah. By what means do the planetary powers produce change in people's moods, actions, circumstances? As the ancient script has it: "As above, so below." The powers which exist in the cosmos have their focal points also in you. The directing force of Mind which operates in and beyond the cosmos is the very source of your inner being. By directing the planetary powers as they exist within your psyche—in the Deep Mind—you can achieve inner harmony, happiness, prosperity, love. You can help others. You can win your heart's desire. The rites of Planetary Magick will powerfully open up level after level of the psyche, balancing and strengthening its perceptions and powers. 1-87542-193-8, 400 pgs., 6 x 9, color plates, softcover $19.95

### ARCHETYPES OF THE ZODIAC

by Kathleen Burt The horoscope is probably the most unique tool for personal growth you can ever have. This book is intended to help you understand how the energies within your horoscope manifest. Once you are aware of how your chart operates on an instinctual level, you can then work consciously with it to remove any obstacles to your growth. The technique offered in this book is based upon the incorporation of the esoteric rulers of the signs and the integration of their polar opposites. This technique has been very successful in helping the client or reader modify existing negative energies in a horoscope so as to improve the quality of his or her life and the understanding of his or her psyche. There is special focus in this huge comprehensive volume on the myths for each sign. Some signs may have as many as four different myths coming from all parts of the world. Allare discussed by the author. There is also emphasis on the Jungian Archetypes involved with each sign. This book has a depth often surprising to the readers of popular astrology books. It has a clarity of expression seldom found in books of the esoteric tradition. It is very easy to understand, even if you know nothing of Jungian philosophy or of mythology. It is intriguing, exciting and very helpful for all levels of astrologers. 0-87542-088-5, 592 pgs., 6 x 9, illus., softcover $14.95

by Steve Cozzi DIRECTION . .. it's what we all need, what we all seek. Is there a method that is clear, simple, concise and logical that will bring this needed direction to our lives? Yes. It's called Locational Astrology, a comprehensive system on the cutting edge of contemporary astrological thought and practice. Steve Cozzi, an expert in locality charts, computers, and the creator of this system of Locational Astrology, has put together a book that will show the average student of astrology as well as the professional how to use this innovative system to help not only themselves, but also their clients. This is not only a historical analysis of Local Space Astrology—it is also a practical guide for the individual reader, applicable to most aspects of everyday activities. Did you know that your route to work each day could actually shape the way you perform or how others perceive you? That where your home is located in your town could affect your state of mind? That where you decide to relocate could change your whole sense of self or your ability to create what you desire? This is all proven fact. Cozzi shows you how to determine your own planetary lines and how to best use them to further your ideals. Planets in Locality has over 50 figures and maps, geomantic compasses and other illustrations to help you find and plot planetary lines within your home, town, region and on out to the country and the world. It is an essential astrological volume. 0-87542-098-2, 308 pgs., 6 x 9, illus.,softcover $12.95
